// AttendSure - Mobile API Client with Automated Failover
// File: mobile/src/api/client.ts

import axios, { AxiosError, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import {
  getServerBaseUrl,
  resolveFastestServer,
  invalidateServerCache,
  cleanUrl,
} from '../services/configService';

export const AUTH_TOKEN_KEY = 'attendsure_auth_token';
const LEGACY_TOKEN_KEYS = ['user_token', 'attendsure_token'];

type AuthExpiredListener = () => void;
const authExpiredListeners = new Set<AuthExpiredListener>();

export function onAuthExpired(listener: AuthExpiredListener): () => void {
  authExpiredListeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    authExpiredListeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

function notifyAuthExpired(): void {
  authExpiredListeners.forEach((fn) => {
    try {
      fn();
    } catch {}
  });
}

async function readStoredAuthToken(): Promise<string | null> {
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
    try {
      const secureToken = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
      if (secureToken) return secureToken;
    } catch {}
  }

  let token = await AsyncStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
  if (!token) {
    for (const legacyKey of LEGACY_TOKEN_KEYS) {
      const legacyToken = await AsyncStorage.getItem(legacyKey);
      if (legacyToken) {
        token = legacyToken;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  return token;
}

function isSameServerOrigin(requestUrl: string, baseUrl: string): boolean {
  if (!requestUrl.startsWith('http://') && !requestUrl.startsWith('https://')) {
    return true;
  }
  try {
    return new URL(requestUrl).origin === new URL(baseUrl).origin;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export const apiClient = axios.create({
  timeout: 8000,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    Accept: 'application/json',
  },
});

apiClient.interceptors.request.use(
  async (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
    // 1. Resolve and sanitize base URL (guarantees no trailing slashes or /api)
    const rawBaseUrl = await getServerBaseUrl();
    const resolvedBase = cleanUrl(rawBaseUrl);
    config.baseURL = resolvedBase;

    // 2. Prevent duplicate /api/ prefixes and path traversal
    const requestPath = config.url || '';
    const isAbsoluteUrl = requestPath.startsWith('http://') || requestPath.startsWith('https://');
    if (!isAbsoluteUrl) {
      const sanitized = requestPath
        .replace(/^\/+/, '')
        .replace(/\.\.\//g, '')
        .replace(/^(api\/)+/i, '');
      config.url = `/api/${sanitized}`;
    }

    // 3. Attach auth token ONLY to requests targeting our own AttendSure server
    if (isSameServerOrigin(requestPath, resolvedBase) && config.headers && !config.headers.Authorization) {
      const token = await readStoredAuthToken();
      if (token) {
        const sanitizedToken = token.trim().replace(/[\r\n]/g, '');
        config.headers.Authorization = `Token ${sanitizedToken}`;
      }
    }

    return config;
  },
  (error) => Promise.reject(error)
);

apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  async (error: AxiosError) => {
    // Never retry or invalidate server cache when a request was intentionally cancelled on unmount
    if (axios.isCancel(error) || error.code === 'ERR_CANCELED') {
      return Promise.reject(error);
    }

    const originalRequest = error.config as InternalAxiosRequestConfig & {
      _retryCount?: number;
    };

    // Clear stored credentials on 401 Unauthorized (except login endpoint itself)
    if (error.response?.status === 401) {
      const reqUrl = String(originalRequest?.url || '');
      if (!reqUrl.includes('/auth/login')) {
        await AsyncStorage.multiRemove([
          AUTH_TOKEN_KEY,
          'user_token',
          'attendsure_token',
          'attendsure_user_profile',
          'attendsure_user',
          'attendsure_role',
        ]);
        if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
          try {
            await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
          } catch {}
        }
        notifyAuthExpired();
      }
      return Promise.reject(error);
    }

    // Automated failover if network times out or drops
    const isNetworkError =
      !error.response ||
      error.code === 'ECONNABORTED' ||
      error.message?.includes('Network Error') ||
      error.message?.includes('timeout');

    if (isNetworkError && originalRequest) {
      originalRequest._retryCount = originalRequest._retryCount || 0;

      if (originalRequest._retryCount < 1) {
        originalRequest._retryCount += 1;
        invalidateServerCache();

        try {
          const newBaseUrl = await resolveFastestServer();
          originalRequest.baseURL = cleanUrl(newBaseUrl);
          return apiClient(originalRequest);
        } catch (discoveryError) {
          return Promise.reject(discoveryError);
        }
      }
    }

    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default apiClient;

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Animated Brand Badge
 * File: mobile/src/branding/AttendSureBadge.tsx
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Animated, type StyleProp, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { AttendSureLogo } from './BrandLogos';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';

export interface AttendSureBadgeProps {
  size?: number;
  animate?: boolean;
  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
}

export const AttendSureBadge: React.FC<AttendSureBadgeProps> = ({
  size = 96,
  animate = false,
  style,
}) => {
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();
  const scaleAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;
  const iconSize = Math.round(size * 0.62);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!animate) {
      scaleAnim.setValue(1);
      return;
    }

    const breatheAnimation = Animated.loop(
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.timing(scaleAnim, {
          toValue: 1.05,
          duration: 1200,
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
        Animated.timing(scaleAnim, {
          toValue: 1,
          duration: 1200,
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
      ])
    );

    breatheAnimation.start();
    return () => {
      breatheAnimation.stop();
    };
  }, [animate, scaleAnim]);

  return (
    <Animated.View
      style={[
        styles.badgeContainer,
        {
          width: size,
          height: size,
          borderRadius: size / 2,
          borderColor: palette.borderStrong,
          shadowColor: palette.primary,
          transform: [{ scale: scaleAnim }],
        },
        style,
      ]}
    >
      <AttendSureLogo size={iconSize} style={{ borderWidth: 0 }} />
    </Animated.View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  badgeContainer: {
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    borderWidth: 2,
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.18,
    shadowRadius: 10,
    elevation: 6,
    overflow: 'hidden',
  },
});

export default AttendSureBadge;

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Brand Logo Wrapper
 * File: mobile/src/branding/AttendSureLogo.tsx
 */

import React from 'react';
import type { StyleProp, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { AttendSureLogo as BrandAttendSureLogo } from './BrandLogos';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';

export interface AttendSureLogoProps {
  size?: number;
  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
}

export const AttendSureLogo: React.FC<AttendSureLogoProps> = ({ size = 48, style }) => {
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();

  return (
    <BrandAttendSureLogo
      size={size}
      style={[
        {
          borderColor: palette.border,
          borderWidth: palette.isDark ? 1 : 0,
        },
        style,
      ]}
    />
  );
};

export default AttendSureLogo;

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Centralized Brand Logo Components
 * File: mobile/src/branding/BrandLogos.tsx
 */

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, type StyleProp, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';

export const ATTENDSURE_LOGO_ASSET = require('../../assets/AttendSureLogo.svg');
export const TECHBLAZER_LOGO_ASSET = require('../../assets/techblazer.svg');

export interface AttendSureLogoProps {
  size?: number;
  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
}

export const AttendSureLogo: React.FC<AttendSureLogoProps> = ({ size = 48, style }) => {
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.logoCircle,
        {
          width: size,
          height: size,
          borderRadius: size / 2,
          borderColor: palette.border,
          borderWidth: palette.isDark ? 1 : 0,
        },
        style,
      ]}
    >
      <Image
        source={ATTENDSURE_LOGO_ASSET}
        style={{ width: size, height: size }}
        contentFit="contain"
        priority="high"
        cachePolicy="memory-disk"
      />
    </View>
  );
};

export interface TechBlazerLogoProps {
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
}

export const TechBlazerLogo: React.FC<TechBlazerLogoProps> = ({
  width = 76,
  height = 16,
  style,
}) => {
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();

  if (palette.isDark) {
    return (
      <View
        style={[
          styles.darkLogoPill,
          { borderColor: palette.border },
          style,
        ]}
      >
        <Image
          source={TECHBLAZER_LOGO_ASSET}
          style={{ width, height }}
          contentFit="contain"
          cachePolicy="memory-disk"
        />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={style}>
      <Image
        source={TECHBLAZER_LOGO_ASSET}
        style={{ width, height }}
        contentFit="contain"
        cachePolicy="memory-disk"
      />
    </View>
  );
};

export interface TechBlazerSignatureProps {
  size?: number;
  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
}

export const TechBlazerSignature: React.FC<TechBlazerSignatureProps> = ({
  size = 22,
  style,
}) => {
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();

  return (
    <View style={[styles.signatureContainer, style]}>
      <Text style={[styles.fromText, { color: palette.textMuted }]}>from</Text>
      <View style={styles.brandRow}>
        <Text style={[styles.brandText, { fontSize: size, color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
          TECHBLAZER
        </Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { useThemeMode } from '../../context/ThemeContext';

interface BadgeProps {
  label: string;
  variant?: 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'info' | 'neutral';
}

export function Badge({ label, variant = 'neutral' }: BadgeProps) {
  const { palette, ui, fonts } = useThemeMode();

  const map = {
    success: { bg: palette.successLight, text: palette.success },
    warning: { bg: palette.warningLight, text: palette.warning },
    danger: { bg: palette.dangerLight, text: palette.danger },
    info: { bg: palette.infoLight, text: palette.info },
    neutral: { bg: palette.surfaceSubtle, text: palette.textSecondary },
  };
  const cur = map[variant];

  return (
    <View style={[ui.badge, { backgroundColor: cur.bg }]}>
      <Text style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.xs, fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold, color: cur.text }}>{label}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Text, TouchableOpacity, ActivityIndicator, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useThemeMode } from '../../context/ThemeContext';

interface ButtonProps {
  title: string;
  onPress: () => void;
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'danger' | 'outline';
  loading?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  style?: ViewStyle;
}

export function Button({ title, onPress, variant = 'primary', loading = false, disabled = false, style }: ButtonProps) {
  const { palette, radius, spacing, fonts } = useThemeMode();

  const stylesMap = {
    primary: { bg: palette.buttonBg, text: palette.onPrimary, border: 'transparent' },
    danger: { bg: palette.dangerBg, text: palette.onPrimary, border: 'transparent' },
    secondary: { bg: palette.surfaceSubtle, text: palette.textPrimary, border: palette.border },
    outline: { bg: 'transparent', text: palette.textSecondary, border: palette.borderStrong },
  };
  const cfg = stylesMap[variant];

  return (
    <TouchableOpacity
      style={[
        {
          height: 44,
          borderRadius: radius.md,
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
          backgroundColor: cfg.bg,
          borderColor: cfg.border,
          borderWidth: cfg.border !== 'transparent' ? 1 : 0,
          opacity: disabled ? 0.5 : 1,
        },
        style,
      ]}
      onPress={onPress}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      activeOpacity={0.8}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <ActivityIndicator color={cfg.text} size="small" />
      ) : (
        <Text style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.md, fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold, color: cfg.text }}>{title}</Text>
      )}
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Text, View, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useThemeMode } from '../../context/ThemeContext';

interface CardProps {
  title?: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  rightAction?: React.ReactNode;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  style?: ViewStyle;
}

export function Card({ title, subtitle, rightAction, children, style }: CardProps) {
  const { palette, ui, fonts, spacing } = useThemeMode();

  return (
    <View style={[ui.card, style]}>
      {(title || rightAction) && (
        <View style={[ui.rowBetween, { marginBottom: spacing.sm, paddingBottom: spacing.xs, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: palette.border }]}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            {title && <Text style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.xs, fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold, color: palette.textSecondary, textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>{title}</Text>}
            {subtitle && <Text style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.xs, color: palette.textMuted, marginTop: 2 }}>{subtitle}</Text>}
          </View>
          {rightAction && <View>{rightAction}</View>}
        </View>
      )}
      <View>{children}</View>
    </View>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Text, TextInput, View, TextInputProps } from 'react-native';
import { useThemeMode } from '../../context/ThemeContext';

interface InputProps extends TextInputProps {
  label: string;
  error?: string;
}

export function Input({ label, error, style, ...rest }: InputProps) {
  const { palette, ui, fonts, spacing } = useThemeMode();

  return (
    <View style={{ marginBottom: spacing.md }}>
      <Text style={ui.label}>{label}</Text>
      <TextInput
        style={[
          ui.input,
          error ? { borderColor: palette.danger, backgroundColor: palette.dangerLight } : null,
          style,
        ]}
        placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
        {...rest}
      />
      {error && <Text style={{ fontSize: fonts.size.xs, color: palette.danger, marginTop: 4 }}>{error}</Text>}
    </View>
  );
}

/**
 * AttendSure - Civil Service Form No. 48 Daily Time Record View
 * File: mobile/src/components/reports/DTRReportView.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  Modal,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import axios from 'axios';
import { apiClient } from '../../api/client';
import { useThemeMode } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { cleanText, getFriendlyErrorMessage } from '../../constants/theme';

export interface DailyDTRRow {
  day: number;
  date_str: string;
  day_of_week: string;
  is_weekend: boolean;
  am_arrival: string;
  am_departure: string;
  pm_arrival: string;
  pm_departure: string;
  undertime_hours: number | string;
  undertime_minutes: number | string;
  is_loafing?: boolean;
  loafing_excused?: boolean;
  loafing_remarks?: string;
}

export interface DTRReportData {
  faculty_id: number;
  faculty_name: string;
  employee_id: string;
  department?: string;
  month: string;
  month_number: number;
  year: number;
  regular_days_hours: string;
  saturdays_hours: string;
  school_head: string;
  school_head_title?: string;
  dtr_submitted?: boolean;
  dept_head_approved: boolean;
  approved_by_dept_head_at?: string | null;
  user_role?: string;
  can_approve_dept?: boolean;
  can_clear_loafing?: boolean;
  rows: DailyDTRRow[];
  total_undertime_hours: number;
  total_undertime_minutes: number;
  days_present: number;
}

export interface DTRReportViewProps {
  data: DTRReportData | null;
  selectedMonth?: number;
  selectedYear?: number;
  onMonthChange?: (month: number, year: number) => void;
  submitting?: boolean;
  onSubmit?: () => void;
  onRefreshNeeded?: () => void;
}

const MONTH_NAMES = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
];

const showNotice = (title: string, message: string) => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

export const DTRReportView: React.FC<DTRReportViewProps> = ({
  data,
  selectedMonth = new Date().getMonth() + 1,
  selectedYear = new Date().getFullYear(),
  onMonthChange,
  submitting = false,
  onSubmit,
  onRefreshNeeded,
}) => {
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();
  const [viewSide, setViewSide] = useState<'FRONT' | 'BACK' | 'BOTH'>('FRONT');
  const [selectedViolationRow, setSelectedViolationRow] = useState<DailyDTRRow | null>(null);
  const [pardonRemarks, setPardonRemarks] = useState<string>('');
  const [actionProcessing, setActionProcessing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showDatePickerModal, setShowDatePickerModal] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, []);

  // Dynamically derive year choices from the current calendar year (never hardcoded)
  const yearOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    const anchor = Math.max(currentYear, selectedYear);
    const list = [anchor - 2, anchor - 1, anchor, anchor + 1];
    if (!list.includes(selectedYear)) {
      list.unshift(selectedYear);
    }
    return Array.from(new Set(list)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  }, [selectedYear]);

  if (!data) return null;

  const isApproved = Boolean(data.dept_head_approved);
  const isSubmitted = Boolean(data.dtr_submitted);
  const facultyDisplayName = cleanText(data.faculty_name, 160) || 'Faculty Member';
  const resolvedHead = cleanText(data.school_head, 160) || 'School Head / In-Charge';
  const resolvedTitle = cleanText(data.school_head_title, 120) || 'In-Charge';
  const safeMonthIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(11, selectedMonth - 1));
  const selectedMonthLabel = MONTH_NAMES[safeMonthIndex] || '';

  // Normalize 31 rows
  const rawRows = Array.isArray(data.rows) ? data.rows : [];
  const rows31: DailyDTRRow[] = [];

  for (let dayNum = 1; dayNum <= 31; dayNum++) {
    const found = rawRows.find((r) => r.day === dayNum);
    if (found) {
      rows31.push(found);
    } else {
      rows31.push({
        day: dayNum,
        date_str: '',
        day_of_week: '',
        is_weekend: false,
        am_arrival: '',
        am_departure: '',
        pm_arrival: '',
        pm_departure: '',
        undertime_hours: '',
        undertime_minutes: '',
        is_loafing: false,
        loafing_excused: false,
      });
    }
  }

  // Month navigation handlers
  const handlePrevMonth = () => {
    if (!onMonthChange) return;
    if (selectedMonth <= 1) {
      onMonthChange(12, selectedYear - 1);
    } else {
      onMonthChange(selectedMonth - 1, selectedYear);
    }
  };

  const handleNextMonth = () => {
    if (!onMonthChange) return;
    if (selectedMonth >= 12) {
      onMonthChange(1, selectedYear + 1);
    } else {
      onMonthChange(selectedMonth + 1, selectedYear);
    }
  };

  // Excuse early departure / attendance flag
  const handleExcuseLoafing = async () => {
    if (!selectedViolationRow || actionProcessing) return;

    const cleanRemarks = cleanText(pardonRemarks, 300);
    if (!cleanRemarks) {
      showNotice('Required', 'Please enter official remarks or justification.');
      return;
    }

    abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    setActionProcessing(true);
    try {
      const encodedFacultyId = encodeURIComponent(String(data.faculty_id));
      await apiClient.post(
        `/reports/dtr/${encodedFacultyId}/`,
        {
          action: 'EXCUSE_LOAFING',
          faculty_id: data.faculty_id,
          date_str: selectedViolationRow.date_str,
          remarks: cleanRemarks,
        },
        { signal: controller.signal }
      );

      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Excused', `Departure on ${selectedViolationRow.date_str} has been excused.`);
      setSelectedViolationRow(null);
      setPardonRemarks('');
      if (onRefreshNeeded) onRefreshNeeded();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (axios.isCancel(err) || (err as { code?: string })?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED') {
        return;
      }
      showNotice(
        'Unable to Update',
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not excuse this attendance record. Please try again.')
      );
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setActionProcessing(false);
      }
    }
  };

  // Front DTR Sheet (official printed document stays white paper with crisp ink)
  const renderFrontSheet = () => (
    <View style={[styles.sheetContainer, { borderColor: palette.borderStrong }]}>
      {/* Draft Watermark */}
      {!isApproved && (
        <View style={styles.watermarkOverlay} pointerEvents="none">
          <View style={styles.watermarkRotate}>
            <View style={styles.watermarkBorder}>
              <Text style={styles.watermarkText}>DRAFT</Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.watermarkSub}>NOT APPROVED BY DEPT HEAD</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Interactive Date Navigation Bar */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.topDateNavCard,
          { backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle, borderColor: palette.border },
        ]}
      >
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.dateNavArrow}
          onPress={handlePrevMonth}
          accessibilityLabel="Previous month"
        >
          <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={16} color={palette.primary} />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.dateDisplayBtn}
          onPress={() => setShowDatePickerModal(true)}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
          accessibilityLabel="Choose month and year"
        >
          <Ionicons name="calendar-outline" size={14} color={palette.primary} />
          <Text style={[styles.dateDisplayText, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
            {selectedMonthLabel} {selectedYear}
          </Text>
          <Ionicons name="caret-down-outline" size={11} color={palette.textMuted} />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.dateNavArrow}
          onPress={handleNextMonth}
          accessibilityLabel="Next month"
        >
          <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={16} color={palette.primary} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {/* Statutory Header */}
      <Text style={styles.formMeta}>Civil Service Form No. 48</Text>
      <Text style={styles.formTitle}>DAILY TIME RECORD</Text>
      <Text style={styles.formDivider}>----- o0o -----</Text>

      {/* Name Line */}
      <View style={styles.nameBlock}>
        <Text style={styles.nameText}>{facultyDisplayName.toUpperCase()}</Text>
        <View style={styles.blackUnderline} />
        <Text style={styles.captionText}>(Name)</Text>
        {data.employee_id ? (
          <Text style={styles.empIdCaption}>Employee ID: {cleanText(data.employee_id, 60)}</Text>
        ) : null}
      </View>

      {/* Month & Prescribed Hours */}
      <View style={styles.hoursBlock}>
        <Text style={styles.monthCoverText}>
          For the month of <Text style={styles.boldUnderline}>{selectedMonthLabel}</Text>, 20
          <Text style={styles.boldUnderline}>{String(selectedYear).slice(-2)}</Text>
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.hoursSpecRow}>
          <Text style={styles.hoursSpecLabel}>
            Official hours of arrival{'\n'}and departure
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.hoursSpecBox}>
            <Text style={styles.hoursText}>
              Regular Days:{' '}
              <Text style={styles.underlineVal}>
                {cleanText(data.regular_days_hours, 80) || '8:00 AM - 12:00 PM / 1:00 PM - 5:00 PM'}
              </Text>
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.hoursText}>
              Saturdays:{' '}
              <Text style={styles.underlineVal}>
                {cleanText(data.saturdays_hours, 80) || 'As Required'}
              </Text>
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* 31-Day Grid Table */}
      <View style={styles.table}>
        <View style={styles.tableRowHeader}>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colDay, { borderBottomWidth: 0 }]}>
            <Text style={styles.thBold}>Days</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colHalf, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thBold}>A. M.</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colHalf, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thBold}>P. M.</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUt, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thBold}>UNDERTIME</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[styles.tableRowHeader, { borderBottomWidth: 1.2 }]}>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colDay]} />
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>ARRIVAL</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>DEPARTURE</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>ARRIVAL</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>DEPARTURE</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>Hours</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.thSmall}>Minutes</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {rows31.map((row) => {
          const isViolating = Boolean(row.is_loafing && !row.loafing_excused);
          const isWeekend = Boolean(row.is_weekend);

          return (
            <View
              key={String(row.day)}
              style={[
                styles.tableRow,
                isViolating
                  ? { backgroundColor: '#fee2e2' }
                  : isWeekend
                  ? { backgroundColor: '#fafafa' }
                  : null,
              ]}
            >
              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colDay]}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => {
                    if (data.can_clear_loafing && row.is_loafing) {
                      setSelectedViolationRow(row);
                    }
                  }}
                  disabled={!data.can_clear_loafing || !row.is_loafing}
                  style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' }}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.dayText}>{row.day}</Text>
                  {row.is_loafing ? (
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        fontSize: 8,
                        fontWeight: '800',
                        marginLeft: 1,
                        color: isViolating ? '#dc2626' : '#059669',
                      }}
                    >
                      {isViolating ? '!' : '✓'}
                    </Text>
                  ) : null}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, isViolating && styles.redText]}>
                  {row.am_arrival}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, isViolating && styles.redText]}>
                  {row.am_departure}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, isViolating && styles.redText]}>
                  {row.pm_arrival}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTime, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, isViolating && styles.redText]}>
                  {row.pm_departure}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, row.undertime_hours ? styles.redText : null]}>
                  {row.undertime_hours}
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
                <Text style={[styles.tdText, row.undertime_minutes ? styles.redText : null]}>
                  {row.undertime_minutes}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          );
        })}

        {/* Totals Row (flex aligned with data columns) */}
        <View style={styles.totalRow}>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colDay]} />
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colTotalFlex]}>
            <Text style={styles.totalThText}>TOTAL</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.totalValText}>
              {data.total_undertime_hours > 0 ? String(data.total_undertime_hours) : ''}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.cell, styles.colUtPart, styles.borderLeft]}>
            <Text style={styles.totalValText}>
              {data.total_undertime_minutes > 0 ? String(data.total_undertime_minutes) : ''}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Certification */}
      <Text style={styles.certBody}>
        I CERTIFY on my honor that the above is a true and correct report of the
        hours of work performed, record of which was made daily at the time of
        arrival and departure from office.
      </Text>

      <View style={styles.sigBox}>
        <Text style={styles.sigName}>{facultyDisplayName}</Text>
        <View style={styles.sigLine} />
        <Text style={styles.captionText}>(Signature of Faculty / Employee)</Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.sigBox}>
        <Text style={styles.verifiedText}>Verified as to prescribed office hours:</Text>
        <Text style={styles.sigName}>{resolvedHead}</Text>
        <View style={styles.sigLine} />
        <Text style={styles.captionText}>{resolvedTitle}</Text>
      </View>

      <Text style={styles.backNote}>(See Instructions on back)</Text>

      {/* Submit Button */}
      {!isSubmitted && onSubmit && (
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            styles.submitBtn,
            { backgroundColor: palette.buttonBg },
            submitting && { opacity: 0.6 },
          ]}
          onPress={onSubmit}
          disabled={submitting}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
        >
          {submitting ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.onPrimary} />
          ) : (
            <>
              <Ionicons name="send-outline" size={16} color={palette.onPrimary} />
              <Text style={[styles.submitBtnText, { color: palette.onPrimary }]}>
                Submit DTR
              </Text>
            </>
          )}
        </TouchableOpacity>
      )}
    </View>
  );

  // Back Instructions Sheet
  const renderBackSheet = () => (
    <View style={[styles.sheetContainer, { borderColor: palette.borderStrong }]}>
      <Text style={styles.instTitle}>INSTRUCTIONS</Text>
      <View style={styles.instLine} />

      <Text style={styles.instP}>
        Civil Service Form No. 48, after completion, should be filed in the records
        of the Bureau or Office which submits the monthly report on Civil Service Form
        No. 3 to the Bureau of Civil Service.
      </Text>

      <Text style={styles.instP}>
        In lieu of the above, court interpreters and stenographers who accompany
        the judges of the Court of First Instance will fill out the daily time
        reports on this form in triplicate, after which they should be approved by
        the judge with whom service has been rendered, or by an officer of the
        Department of Justice authorized to do so. The original should be forwarded
        promptly after the end of the month to the Bureau of Civil Service, thru the
        Department of Justice; the duplicate kept in the Department of Justice;
        and the triplicate, in the office of the Clerk of Court where service was
        rendered.
      </Text>

      <Text style={styles.instP}>
        In the space provided for the purpose on the other side will be indicated the
        office hours the employee is required to observe, as for example, &quot;Regular
        days, 8:00 to 12:00 and 1:00 to 4:00; Saturdays 8:00 to 1:00.&quot;
      </Text>

      <Text style={styles.instP}>
        Attention is invited to paragraph 3, Civil Service Rule XV, Executive Order
        No. 5, series of 1909, which reads as follows:
      </Text>

      <View style={styles.instQuote}>
        <Text style={styles.instQuoteText}>
          &quot;Each chief of a Bureau or Office shall require a daily record of
          attendance of all the officers and employees under him entitled to leave or
          absence or vacation (including faculty) to be kept on the proper form and
          also a systematic office record showing for each day all absences from
          duty from any cause whatever. At the beginning of each month he shall
          report to the Commissioner on the proper form of all absences from any cause
          whatever, including the exact amount of undertime of each person for each
          day. Officers or employees serving in the field or on the water need not be
          required to keep a daily record, but all absences of such employees must
          be included in the monthly report of changes and absences. Falsification of
          time records will render the offending officers or employee liable to
          summary removal from the service and criminal prosecution.&quot;
        </Text>
      </View>

      <Text style={styles.instNote}>
        <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>(NOTE: </Text>A record made from memory
        at sometime subsequent to the occurrence of an event is not reliable. Non
        observance of office hours deprives the employee of the leave privileges
        although he may have rendered overtime service. Where service rendered
        outside of the Office for the whole morning or afternoon, notation to that
        effect should be made clearly.)
      </Text>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
      {/* Side Toggle Bar */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.sideToggleRow,
          { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border },
        ]}
      >
        {(['FRONT', 'BACK', 'BOTH'] as const).map((side) => {
          const active = viewSide === side;
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={side}
              style={[
                styles.sideToggleBtn,
                active && { backgroundColor: palette.buttonBg },
              ]}
              onPress={() => setViewSide(side)}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.sideToggleText,
                  { color: active ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary },
                ]}
              >
                {side === 'FRONT'
                  ? 'Front (DTR)'
                  : side === 'BACK'
                  ? 'Back (Instructions)'
                  : 'Full View'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      {/* Sheet Output */}
      {(viewSide === 'BOTH' || viewSide === 'FRONT') && renderFrontSheet()}
      {(viewSide === 'BOTH' || viewSide === 'BACK') && renderBackSheet()}

      {/* Month & Year Picker Modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={showDatePickerModal}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setShowDatePickerModal(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.modalCard,
              { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Ionicons name="calendar" size={20} color={palette.primary} />
              <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
                Select Month &amp; Year
              </Text>
            </View>

            {/* Dynamic Year Selector */}
            <Text style={[styles.pickerSectionLabel, { color: palette.textMuted }]}>YEAR</Text>
            <View style={styles.yearGrid}>
              {yearOptions.map((y) => {
                const active = selectedYear === y;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={y}
                    style={[
                      styles.yearChip,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: active ? palette.buttonBg : palette.surfaceSubtle,
                        borderColor: active ? palette.buttonBg : palette.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                    onPress={() => onMonthChange && onMonthChange(selectedMonth, y)}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.yearChipText,
                        { color: active ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {y}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            {/* Months 3x4 Grid */}
            <Text style={[styles.pickerSectionLabel, { color: palette.textMuted }]}>MONTH</Text>
            <View style={styles.monthsGrid}>
              {MONTH_NAMES.map((m, idx) => {
                const mNum = idx + 1;
                const active = selectedMonth === mNum;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={m}
                    style={[
                      styles.monthChip,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: active ? palette.buttonBg : palette.surfaceSubtle,
                        borderColor: active ? palette.buttonBg : palette.border,
                      },
                    ]}
                    onPress={() => {
                      if (onMonthChange) onMonthChange(mNum, selectedYear);
                      setShowDatePickerModal(false);
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.monthChipText,
                        { color: active ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {m.slice(0, 3)}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.modalCloseBtn,
                { backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle, borderColor: palette.borderStrong },
              ]}
              onPress={() => setShowDatePickerModal(false)}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.modalCloseBtnText, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>Close</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Early Departure Review Modal */}
      {selectedViolationRow && (
        <Modal
          visible={Boolean(selectedViolationRow)}
          transparent
          animationType="fade"
          onRequestClose={() => setSelectedViolationRow(null)}
        >
          <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.modalCard,
                { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
                <Ionicons name="warning" size={24} color={palette.danger} />
                <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
                  Early Departure Review
                </Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={[styles.modalDesc, { color: palette.textSecondary }]}>
                An unexcused departure was recorded for{' '}
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: palette.textPrimary }}>
                  {facultyDisplayName}
                </Text>{' '}
                on{' '}
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: palette.textPrimary }}>
                  {selectedViolationRow.date_str}
                </Text>
                . If not excused, this day may incur a deduction.
              </Text>

              <Text style={[styles.violationNote, { color: palette.danger }]}>
                Attendance Note: {cleanText(selectedViolationRow.loafing_remarks, 200) || 'None'}
              </Text>

              <Text style={[styles.modalInputLabel, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
                Official Remarks / Justification:
              </Text>
              <TextInput
                style={[
                  styles.modalInput,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle,
                    borderColor: palette.borderStrong,
                    color: palette.textPrimary,
                  },
                ]}
                value={pardonRemarks}
                onChangeText={setPardonRemarks}
                placeholder="Enter justification for excusing departure..."
                placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                maxLength={300}
              />

              <View style={styles.modalActions}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[
                    styles.modalBtnCancel,
                    { backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle, borderColor: palette.border },
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => setSelectedViolationRow(null)}
                  disabled={actionProcessing}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.modalBtnCancelText, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
                    Cancel
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[
                    styles.modalBtnConfirm,
                    { backgroundColor: palette.buttonBg },
                    actionProcessing && { opacity: 0.6 },
                  ]}
                  onPress={handleExcuseLoafing}
                  disabled={actionProcessing}
                >
                  {actionProcessing ? (
                    <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.onPrimary} />
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Ionicons name="checkmark-done" size={15} color={palette.onPrimary} />
                      <Text style={[styles.modalBtnConfirmText, { color: palette.onPrimary }]}>
                        Excuse &amp; Clear
                      </Text>
                    </>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  sideToggleRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    borderRadius: 8,

  },

  empIdCaption: { fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '700', color: '#004b87', marginTop: 1 },
  hoursBlock: { marginVertical: 4 },
  monthCoverText: { fontSize: 10.5, color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: 4 },
  boldUnderline: { fontWeight: '800', textDecorationLine: 'underline' },
  hoursSpecRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center' },
  hoursSpecLabel: { fontSize: 9, color: '#0f172a', width: 110, lineHeight: 12 },
  hoursSpecBox: { flex: 1, borderLeftWidth: 1.5, borderLeftColor: '#0f172a', paddingLeft: 6 },
  hoursText: { fontSize: 8.5, color: '#0f172a', marginVertical: 0.5 },
  underlineVal: { textDecorationLine: 'underline', fontWeight: '700' },

  table: { borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#0f172a', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', marginTop: 4 },
  tableRowHeader: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
  },
  tableRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1',
    minHeight: 18,
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  totalRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
    minHeight: 20,
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  cell: { paddingVertical: 1, paddingHorizontal: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  borderLeft: { borderLeftWidth: 1, borderLeftColor: '#0f172a' },
  colDay: { width: 30 },

  colTime: { flex: 1 },
  colUt: { flex: 1.2 },

  colTotalFlex: { flex: 4, alignItems: 'flex-end', paddingRight: 6 },
  thBold: { fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a', textAlign: 'center' },
  thSmall: { fontSize: 6.8, fontWeight: '800', color: '#334155', textAlign: 'center' },
  dayText: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a' },
  tdText: { fontSize: 8.5, color: '#334155', textAlign: 'center' },
  redText: { color: '#dc2626', fontWeight: '800' },
  totalThText: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', color: '#0f172a', textAlign: 'right' },
  totalValText: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', color: '#dc2626', textAlign: 'center' },
  certBody: { fontSize: 8.5, color: '#0f172a', textAlign: 'justify', lineHeight: 12, marginTop: 8 },
  sigBox: { alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 12 },
  sigName: { fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a' },
  sigLine: { width: '70%', height: 1, backgroundColor: '#0f172a', marginTop: 1 },
  verifiedText: { fontSize: 8.5, color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: 6 },
  backNote: { fontSize: 7.5, fontStyle: 'italic', textAlign: 'center', color: '#64748b', marginTop: 8 },

  submitBtn: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    paddingVertical: 10,
    borderRadius: 8,
    marginTop: 12,
    gap: 6,
  },
  submitBtnText: { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800' },

  instTitle: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '900',
    textAlign: 'center',
    color: '#0f172a',
    letterSpacing: 0.6,
  },
  instLine: { height: 1, backgroundColor: '#0f172a', marginVertical: 6 },
  instP: { fontSize: 8.5, color: '#0f172a', lineHeight: 12.5, textAlign: 'justify', marginBottom: 6 },
  instQuote: { borderLeftWidth: 1.5, borderLeftColor: '#0f172a', paddingLeft: 6, marginVertical: 4 },
  instQuoteText: {
    fontSize: 8,
    fontStyle: 'italic',
    color: '#0f172a',
    lineHeight: 11.5,
    textAlign: 'justify',
  },
  instNote: { fontSize: 8, color: '#334155', lineHeight: 11.5, textAlign: 'justify', marginTop: 4 },

  modalOverlay: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.7)',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    padding: 16,
  },
  modalCard: {
    borderRadius: 12,
    padding: 16,
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 380,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  modalHeader: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 },
  modalTitle: { fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '800' },
  pickerSectionLabel: { fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '800', marginBottom: 6, letterSpacing: 0.5 },
  yearGrid: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, marginBottom: 14 },
  yearChip: {
    flex: 1,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderRadius: 6,
    alignItems: 'center',
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  yearChipText: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '700' },
  monthsGrid: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 6, marginBottom: 14 },
  monthChip: {
    width: '31%',
    paddingVertical: 8,
    borderRadius: 6,
    alignItems: 'center',

  },
  monthChipText: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '700' },
  modalCloseBtn: {

    borderRadius: 6,
    alignItems: 'center',
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  modalCloseBtnText: { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700' },

  modalDesc: { fontSize: 11, lineHeight: 15, marginBottom: 8 },
  violationNote: { fontSize: 10.5, fontStyle: 'italic', marginBottom: 10 },
  modalInputLabel: { fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '700', marginBottom: 4 },
  modalInput: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 6,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
    fontSize: 12,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  modalActions: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 8 },
  modalBtnCancel: {
    paddingVertical: 7,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    borderRadius: 6,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  modalBtnCancelText: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '700' },
  modalBtnConfirm: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 4,
    paddingVertical: 7,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    borderRadius: 6,
  },
  modalBtnConfirmText: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '800' },
});

export default DTRReportView;

/**
 * AttendSure - School Form 1 (SF1) Official School Register View
 * File: mobile/src/components/reports/SF1ReportView.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import axios from 'axios';
import { apiClient } from '../../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../../context/ThemeContext';
import { cleanText, getFriendlyErrorMessage } from '../../constants/theme';
import { DepEdReportHeader, DepEdReportFooter } from './DepEdReportHeaderFooter';

export interface Learner {
  id: number;
  lrn: string;
  name: string;
  sex: string;
  birthdate: string;
  age: number | string;
  birth_place?: string;
  mother_tongue: string;
  ethnic_group: string;
  religion: string;
  house_street: string;
  barangay: string;
  municipality_city: string;
  province: string;
  father_name: string;
  mother_maiden_name: string;
  guardian_name: string;
  guardian_relationship: string;
  parent_contact: string;
  remarks: string;
}

export interface SF1ReportViewProps {
  data: any;
  learners?: Learner[];
  sectionId?: number | string;
}

// Single source of truth for SF1 header & data column widths
const SF1_COLS = {
  num: 32,
  lrn: 95,
  name: 170,
  sex: 38,
  birthdate: 75,
  age: 36,
  motherTongue: 85,
  ipGroup: 75,
  religion: 75,
  address: 140,
  father: 120,
  mother: 120,
  guardian: 110,
  contact: 90,
  remarks: 80,
} as const;

const SF1_TABLE_INNER_WIDTH = Object.values(SF1_COLS).reduce((sum, w) => sum + w, 0); // 1341
const SF1_TABLE_WIDTH = SF1_TABLE_INNER_WIDTH + 2; // +2 for 1px left/right border
const SF1_CANVAS_WIDTH = SF1_TABLE_WIDTH + 12; // +6px padding on each side

export const SF1ReportView: React.FC<SF1ReportViewProps> = ({ data, learners, sectionId }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();
  const [groupingMode, setGroupingMode] = useState<'COMBINED' | 'SEPARATED'>('COMBINED');
  const [fetchedData, setFetchedData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loadingSelf, setLoadingSelf] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, []);

  // Optional self-fetch if rendered standalone with sectionId and no parent-supplied data
  useEffect(() => {
    if (data || !sectionId) return;

    abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    setLoadingSelf(true);
    setLoadError(null);

    apiClient
      .get(`reports/sf1/${encodeURIComponent(String(sectionId))}/`, {
        signal: controller.signal,
      })
      .then((res) => {
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setFetchedData(res.data || null);
        }
      })
      .catch((err: unknown) => {
        if (axios.isCancel(err) || (err as { code?: string })?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED') {
          return;
        }
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setLoadError(getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not load School Form 1 records.'));
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setLoadingSelf(false);
        }
      });

    return () => {
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [data, sectionId]);

  const activeData = data || fetchedData;

  // Dynamic Adviser Resolution
  const activeUserFullName =
    user?.full_name ||
    `${user?.first_name || ''} ${user?.last_name || ''}`.trim() ||
    user?.faculty_name ||
    user?.username ||
    '';

  const userRole = String(user?.role || user?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE')
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase();

  const isFacultyRole = ['FACULTY', 'ADVISER', 'EMPLOYEE'].includes(userRole);

  // 1. Memoized Combined Learner List (respects parent search-filtered learners prop)
  const sortedCombinedLearners = useMemo(() => {
    if (!activeData || activeData.has_enrolled_students === false) return [];
    const raw: Learner[] = Array.isArray(learners)
      ? learners
      : activeData.students || activeData.learners || [];
    return [...raw].sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''));
  }, [activeData, learners]);

  // 2. Memoized Gender Breakdown
  const { maleLearners, femaleLearners } = useMemo(() => {
    if (!activeData || activeData.has_enrolled_students === false) {
      return { maleLearners: [], femaleLearners: [] };
    }
    const raw: Learner[] = Array.isArray(learners)
      ? learners
      : activeData.students || activeData.learners || [];
    const m = raw.filter((l: Learner) => l.sex === 'M' || l.sex === 'Male');
    const f = raw.filter((l: Learner) => l.sex === 'F' || l.sex === 'Female');
    return {
      maleLearners: [...m].sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '')),
      femaleLearners: [...f].sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '')),
    };
  }, [activeData, learners]);

  if (loadingSelf && !activeData) {
    return (
      <View
        style={[
          styles.stateBox,
          { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border },
        ]}
      >
        <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.primary} />
        <Text style={[styles.stateText, { color: palette.textSecondary }]}>
          Loading School Form 1...
        </Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (loadError && !activeData) {
    return (
      <View
        style={[
          styles.stateBox,
          { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.danger },
        ]}
      >
        <Ionicons name="alert-circle-outline" size={22} color={palette.danger} />
        <Text style={[styles.stateText, { color: palette.danger }]}>{loadError}</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (!activeData) return null;

  const resolvedAdviserName =
    (isFacultyRole && activeUserFullName.length > 0 ? activeUserFullName : null) ||
    activeData.adviser_name ||
    activeUserFullName ||
    'Class Adviser';

  const resolvedSchoolHead = cleanText(activeData.school_head, 160) || 'School Head';

  const formatLearnerAddress = (learner: Learner & { address?: string }) =>
    [learner.house_street, learner.barangay, learner.municipality_city, learner.province]
      .map((part) => cleanText(part, 100))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(', ') ||
    cleanText(learner.address, 200) ||
    '';

  const maleCount = activeData.total_male ?? maleLearners.length;
  const femaleCount = activeData.total_female ?? femaleLearners.length;
  const totalCount = activeData.total_combined ?? maleCount + femaleCount;

  const activeStudentList =
    groupingMode === 'COMBINED'
      ? sortedCombinedLearners
      : [...maleLearners, ...femaleLearners];
  const isListEmpty = activeStudentList.length === 0;

  // Sanitized guardian parser
  const parseGuardian = (name: string, rel: string) => {
    const commonRel = [
      'MOTHER',
      'FATHER',
      'AUNT',
      'UNCLE',
      'GRANDMOTHER',
      'GRANDFATHER',
      'GUARDIAN',
    ];
    const safeName = cleanText(name, 120);
    const safeRel = cleanText(rel, 60);
    if (
      commonRel.includes(safeName.toUpperCase()) &&
      !commonRel.includes(safeRel.toUpperCase())
    ) {
      return { gName: safeRel, gRel: safeName };
    }
    return { gName: safeName, gRel: safeRel.toLowerCase() === 'mather' ? 'Mother' : safeRel };
  };

  const renderLearnerRow = (learner: Learner, idx: number, forcedSex?: 'M' | 'F') => {
    const { gName } = parseGuardian(learner.guardian_name, learner.guardian_relationship);
    return (
      <View key={String(learner.id || idx)} style={styles.tdRow}>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.num }]}>{idx + 1}</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.lrn, fontWeight: '700' }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.lrn, 30)}
        </Text>
        <Text
          style={[
            styles.td,
            { width: SF1_COLS.name, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4, fontWeight: '700' },
          ]}
        >
          {cleanText(learner.name, 120)}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.sex }]}>
          {forcedSex || cleanText(learner.sex, 10)}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.birthdate }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.birthdate, 30)}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.age }]}>{learner.age}</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.motherTongue }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.mother_tongue, 60)}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.ipGroup }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.ethnic_group, 60) || 'N/A'}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.religion }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.religion, 60) || 'N/A'}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.address, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
          {formatLearnerAddress(learner)}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.father, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.father_name, 100) || 'N/A'}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.mother, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.mother_maiden_name, 100) || 'N/A'}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.guardian, textAlign: 'left', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
          {gName || 'N/A'}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, { width: SF1_COLS.contact }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.parent_contact, 40) || 'N/A'}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.td, styles.lastCell, { width: SF1_COLS.remarks }]}>
          {cleanText(learner.remarks, 120)}
        </Text>
      </View>
    );
  };

  const leftLogoUrl = activeData.left_logo || null;
  const rightLogoUrl = activeData.right_logo || null;

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.sheetContainer,
        { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.borderStrong },
      ]}
    >
      {/* Interactive Layout Mode Control Bar */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.controlBar,
          { backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle, borderColor: palette.border },
        ]}
      >
        <Text style={[styles.controlLabel, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
          Class List Layout:
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.toggleRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              styles.toggleBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor:
                  groupingMode === 'COMBINED' ? palette.buttonBg : palette.surface,
                borderColor:
                  groupingMode === 'COMBINED' ? palette.buttonBg : palette.borderStrong,
              },
            ]}
            onPress={() => setGroupingMode('COMBINED')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <Ionicons
              name="people-outline"
              size={13}
              color={groupingMode === 'COMBINED' ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary}
            />
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.toggleText,
                {
                  color: groupingMode === 'COMBINED' ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              One List
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              styles.toggleBtn,
              {
                backgroundColor:
                  groupingMode === 'SEPARATED' ? palette.buttonBg : palette.surface,
                borderColor:
                  groupingMode === 'SEPARATED' ? palette.buttonBg : palette.borderStrong,
              },
            ]}
            onPress={() => setGroupingMode('SEPARATED')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <Ionicons
              name="git-branch-outline"
              size={13}
              color={groupingMode === 'SEPARATED' ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary}
            />
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.toggleText,
                {
                  color: groupingMode === 'SEPARATED' ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              By Sex
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Full Sheet Document Horizontal Scroll (Width matched to column sum) */}
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator>
        <View style={styles.officialDocumentCanvas}>
          {/* 1. Official DepEd DMOS Header */}
          <DepEdReportHeader
            leftLogoUrl={leftLogoUrl}
            rightLogoUrl={rightLogoUrl}
            region={activeData.region}
            division={activeData.division}
            formTitle="School Form 1 (SF 1) School Register"
            formSubtitle="(This replaces Form 1, Master List & STS Form 2)"
          />

          {/* 2. Metadata Grid */}
          <View style={styles.metaGrid}>
            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>School ID: </Text>
                {activeData.school_id || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>Region: </Text>
                {activeData.region || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>Division: </Text>
                {activeData.division || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>District: </Text>
                {activeData.district || '—'}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>School Name: </Text>
                {activeData.school_name || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>School Year: </Text>
                {activeData.academic_year || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>Grade Level: </Text>
                {activeData.grade_level || '—'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaCell}>
                <Text style={styles.metaKey}>Section: </Text>
                {activeData.section_name || '—'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* 3. SF1 Official Table */}
          <View style={styles.table}>
            <View style={styles.thRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.num }]}>#</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.lrn }]}>LRN</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.name }]}>
                LEARNER&apos;S NAME{'\n'}(Last, First, Middle)
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.sex }]}>SEX</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.birthdate }]}>BIRTH DATE</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.age }]}>AGE</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.motherTongue }]}>MOTHER TONGUE</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.ipGroup }]}>IP GROUP</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.religion }]}>RELIGION</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.address }]}>ADDRESS</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.father }]}>FATHER&apos;S NAME</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.mother }]}>MOTHER&apos;S MAIDEN</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.guardian }]}>GUARDIAN</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, { width: SF1_COLS.contact }]}>CONTACT</Text>
              <Text style={[styles.th, styles.lastCell, { width: SF1_COLS.remarks }]}>
                REMARKS
              </Text>
            </View>

            {isListEmpty ? (
              <View style={{ padding: 24, alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b' }}>
                  {activeData.notice ||
                    `No students enrolled in Section ${activeData.section_name || 'this class'}.`}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ) : groupingMode === 'COMBINED' ? (
              activeStudentList.map((learner: Learner, idx: number) =>
                renderLearnerRow(learner, idx)
              )
            ) : (
              <>
                <View style={styles.genderHeader}>
                  <Text style={styles.genderHeaderText}>MALE ({maleLearners.length})</Text>
                </View>
                {maleLearners.map((learner: Learner, idx: number) =>
                  renderLearnerRow(learner, idx, 'M')
                )}

                <View style={[styles.genderHeader, { backgroundColor: '#fdf2f8' }]}>
                  <Text style={[styles.genderHeaderText, { color: '#db2777' }]}>
                    FEMALE ({femaleLearners.length})
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {femaleLearners.map((learner: Learner, idx: number) =>
                  renderLearnerRow(learner, idx, 'F')
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </View>

          {/* 4. Official DepEd Indicators & Remarks Guide Box */}
          <View style={styles.remarksGuideContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.remarksGuideTitle}>
              List and Code of Indicators under REMARKS column:
            </Text>
            <View style={styles.remarksGrid}>
              <View style={styles.remarksCol}>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Transferred Out (T/O): </Text>
                  Name of Public (P) / Private (PR) School &amp; Effectivity Date
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Transferred In (T/I): </Text>
                  Name of Public (P) / Private (PR) School &amp; Effectivity Date
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>CCT Recipient: </Text>
                  CCT C/H ID Number &amp; Effectivity Date
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Balik-Aral (B/A): </Text>
                  Name of school last attended &amp; Year
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.remarksCol}>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Dropped Out (DRP): </Text>
                  Reason and Effectivity Date
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Late Enrollment (LE): </Text>
                  Reason for enrollment beyond 1st Friday of June
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Learner With Disability (LWD): </Text>
                  Specify condition
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.remarksItem}>
                  <Text style={styles.codeBold}>Accelerated (ACL): </Text>
                  Specify Level &amp; Effectivity Date
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* 5. Summary Box & Dynamic Signatures */}
          <View style={styles.summaryBox}>
            <View style={styles.summaryInfoCol}>
              <Text style={styles.summaryTitle}>Registered Learners Summary</Text>
              <Text style={styles.summaryText}>
                Male: <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{maleCount}</Text>
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.summaryText}>
                Female: <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{femaleCount}</Text>
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.summaryText}>
                Total Enrolled: <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{totalCount}</Text>
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.signCol}>
              <View style={styles.signBlock}>
                <Text style={styles.signName}>{resolvedAdviserName}</Text>
                <View style={styles.signLine} />
                <Text style={styles.signCaption}>Prepared by (Class Adviser)</Text>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.signBlock}>
                <Text style={styles.signName}>{resolvedSchoolHead}</Text>
                <View style={styles.signLine} />
                <Text style={styles.signCaption}>Certified Correct (School Head)</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* 6. Official DepEd DMOS Footer */}
          <DepEdReportFooter
            schoolLogoUrl={activeData.school_logo || activeData.right_logo || null}
            address={activeData.school_address || activeData.address}
            contactNumber={activeData.school_contact || activeData.contact_number}
            email={activeData.school_email || activeData.email}
          />
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  stateBox: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 10,
    padding: 20,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    gap: 8,
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  stateText: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: '600',
    textAlign: 'center',
  },
  sheetContainer: {
    borderWidth: 1.5,
    borderRadius: 6,
    padding: 10,
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  controlBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    padding: 8,
    borderRadius: 6,
    borderWidth: 1,
    marginBottom: 10,
  },
  controlLabel: { fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800' },
  toggleRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 },
  toggleBtn: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 4,
    paddingVertical: 5,
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
    borderRadius: 6,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  toggleText: { fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '700' },

  officialDocumentCanvas: {
    width: SF1_CANVAS_WIDTH,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    paddingHorizontal: 6,
    paddingVertical: 6,
  },

  metaGrid: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
    padding: 6,
    marginBottom: 8,
    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
    width: SF1_TABLE_WIDTH,
  },
  metaRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginVertical: 2 },
  metaCell: { fontSize: 9, color: '#0f172a' },
  metaKey: { fontWeight: '700' },

  table: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#0f172a', width: SF1_TABLE_WIDTH },
  thRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
  },
  th: {
    fontSize: 7.5,
    fontWeight: '800',
    color: '#0f172a',
    textAlign: 'center',
    paddingVertical: 4,
    borderRightWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
  },
  genderHeader: {
    backgroundColor: '#eff6ff',
    paddingVertical: 2,
    paddingLeft: 6,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
  },
  genderHeaderText: { fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0284c7' },
  tdRow: { flexDirection: 'row', borderBottomWidth: 1, borderColor: '#cbd5e1', minHeight: 20 },
  td: {
    fontSize: 8,
    color: '#0f172a',
    textAlign: 'center',
    paddingVertical: 3,
    borderRightWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1',
  },
  lastCell: {
    borderRightWidth: 0,
  },

  remarksGuideContainer: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    padding: 6,
    marginTop: 4,
    width: SF1_TABLE_WIDTH,
  },
  remarksGuideTitle: {
    fontSize: 8,
    fontWeight: '900',
    color: '#0f172a',
    marginBottom: 2,
    textTransform: 'uppercase',
  },
  remarksGrid: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    gap: 8,
  },
  remarksCol: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  remarksItem: {
    fontSize: 7.2,
    color: '#1e293b',
    lineHeight: 10,
    marginVertical: 0.5,
  },
  codeBold: {
    fontWeight: '800',
    color: '#0f172a',
  },

  summaryBox: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#0f172a',
    padding: 8,
    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
    marginTop: 4,
    width: SF1_TABLE_WIDTH,
  },
  summaryInfoCol: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center' },
  summaryTitle: { fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a', marginBottom: 4 },
  summaryText: { fontSize: 9, color: '#334155', marginVertical: 1 },
  signCol: { flex: 1.5, flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-around', gap: 10 },
  signBlock: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center' },
  signName: { fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a' },
  signLine: { width: '100%', height: 1, backgroundColor: '#0f172a', marginTop: 2 },
  signCaption: { fontSize: 7.5, color: '#475569', marginTop: 1, textAlign: 'center' },
});

export default SF1ReportView;
