/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Circular Radar & Spiral Loader Splash Screen
 * File: mobile/src/components/SplashScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Animated,
  Easing,
  Platform,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Modal,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import * as NativeSplashScreen from 'expo-splash-screen';
import { getServerBaseUrl, setServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';
import { AttendSureLogo } from '../branding/BrandLogos';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import {
  cleanText,
  isSafeUrl,
  getFriendlyErrorMessage,
} from '../constants/theme';

export interface SplashScreenProps {
  loading?: boolean;
  minDurationMs?: number;
  onFinish?: () => void;
}

const SPIRAL_DOTS = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, index) => index);

const showNotice = (title: string, message: string) => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

// Cybersecurity: Reject dangerous protocols and validate strict http/https URL
const isValidHttpServerUrl = (rawUrl: string): boolean => {
  const trimmed = cleanText(rawUrl, 300);
  if (!trimmed) return false;
  const lower = trimmed.toLowerCase();
  if (
    lower.startsWith('javascript:') ||
    lower.startsWith('data:') ||
    lower.startsWith('file:') ||
    lower.startsWith('vbscript:')
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  if (!isSafeUrl(trimmed)) return false;
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
    return (parsed.protocol === 'http:' || parsed.protocol === 'https:') && Boolean(parsed.hostname);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
};

export const SplashScreen: React.FC<SplashScreenProps> = ({
  loading = false,
  minDurationMs = 1200,
  onFinish,
}) => {
  const { palette, isDark } = useThemeMode();
  const [canExit, setCanExit] = useState(false);
  const [hidden, setHidden] = useState(false);

  const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const isHoldingSplashRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  // Fade out animation
  const opacityAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;

  // Radar wave & sweep animations
  const radarWave1 = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const radarWave2 = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const radarSweep = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  // Spiral loader master clock (0 -> 1 loop)
  const spiralClock = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  // Server Settings Modal State
  const [showToolsModal, setShowToolsModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [customServerUrl, setCustomServerUrl] = useState<string>('');
  const [savingUrl, setSavingUrl] = useState<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  // Start continuous radar & spiral animations
  useEffect(() => {
    const wave1Loop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.timing(radarWave1, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 2400,
        easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
        useNativeDriver: true,
      })
    );

    const wave2Loop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.delay(1200),
        Animated.timing(radarWave2, {
          toValue: 1,
          duration: 2400,
          easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
      ])
    );

    const sweepLoop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.timing(radarSweep, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 2200,
        easing: Easing.linear,
        useNativeDriver: true,
      })
    );

    const spiralLoop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.timing(spiralClock, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 1100,
        easing: Easing.linear,
        useNativeDriver: true,
      })
    );

    wave1Loop.start();
    wave2Loop.start();
    sweepLoop.start();
    spiralLoop.start();

    return () => {
      wave1Loop.stop();
      wave2Loop.stop();
      sweepLoop.stop();
      spiralLoop.stop();
    };
  }, [radarWave1, radarWave2, radarSweep, spiralClock]);

  // Splash duration timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
      NativeSplashScreen.hideAsync().catch(() => {});
    }

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setCanExit(true);
      }
    }, minDurationMs);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [minDurationMs]);

  // Exit transition once timer finishes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && canExit && !isHoldingSplashRef.current) {
      const fadeAnim = Animated.timing(opacityAnim, {
        toValue: 0,
        duration: 350,
        useNativeDriver: true,
      });
      fadeAnim.start(({ finished }) => {
        if (finished && isMountedRef.current) {
          setHidden(true);
          if (onFinish) onFinish();
        }
      });
      return () => {
        fadeAnim.stop();
      };
    }
    return undefined;
  }, [loading, canExit, onFinish, opacityAnim]);

  const openTools = async () => {
    isHoldingSplashRef.current = true;
    try {
      const activeUrl = await getServerBaseUrl();
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setCustomServerUrl(activeUrl);
        setShowToolsModal(true);
      }
    } catch {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setCustomServerUrl('');
        setShowToolsModal(true);
      }
    }
  };

  const closeToolsAndContinue = () => {
    setShowToolsModal(false);
    isHoldingSplashRef.current = false;
    if (onFinish) onFinish();
  };

  const saveServerAddress = async () => {
    if (savingUrl) return;
    const sanitized = cleanText(customServerUrl, 300);
    if (!isValidHttpServerUrl(sanitized)) {
      showNotice(
        'Invalid Address',
        'Please enter a valid web address starting with http:// or https://.'
      );
      return;
    }

    setSavingUrl(true);
    try {
      await setServerBaseUrl(sanitized);
      showNotice('Saved', 'School server address updated.');
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        closeToolsAndContinue();
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      showNotice(
        'Invalid Address',
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Please check the school server address and try again.')
      );
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setSavingUrl(false);
      }
    }
  };

  if (hidden) return null;

  const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();

  const wave1Scale = radarWave1.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: [0.95, 1.85] });
  const wave1Opacity = radarWave1.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: [0.75, 0] });

  const wave2Scale = radarWave2.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: [0.95, 1.85] });
  const wave2Opacity = radarWave2.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: [0.75, 0] });

  const sweepRotate = radarSweep.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: ['0deg', '360deg'] });

  return (
    <Animated.View
      style={[
        styles.container,
        { backgroundColor: palette.background, opacity: opacityAnim },
      ]}
    >
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeContainer} edges={['top', 'bottom']}>
        <StatusBar style={isDark ? 'light' : 'dark'} />

        {/* Top-Left Server Settings Button */}
        <View style={styles.topBar}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.toolsButton}
            onPress={openTools}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
            accessibilityLabel="Server settings"
          >
            <View style={[styles.toolsIconHalo, { backgroundColor: palette.primarySoft }]}>
              <View style={[styles.toolsIconCircle, { backgroundColor: palette.buttonBg }]}>
                <Ionicons name="settings-sharp" size={16} color={palette.onPrimary} />
              </View>
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* Center Circular Radar Logo + Spiral Loader */}
        <View style={styles.centerSection}>
          <View style={styles.radarContainer}>
            {/* Expanding Radar Wave 1 */}
            <Animated.View
              pointerEvents="none"
              style={[
                styles.radarRing,
                {
                  borderColor: palette.primary,
                  transform: [{ scale: wave1Scale }],
                  opacity: wave1Opacity,
                },
              ]}
            />

            {/* Expanding Radar Wave 2 */}
            <Animated.View
              pointerEvents="none"
              style={[
                styles.radarRing,
                {
                  borderColor: palette.secondary,
                  transform: [{ scale: wave2Scale }],
                  opacity: wave2Opacity,
                },
              ]}
            />

            {/* Rotating Radar Sweep Ring */}
            <Animated.View
              pointerEvents="none"
              style={[
                styles.radarSweepRing,
                {
                  borderTopColor: palette.primary,
                  borderRightColor: `${palette.primary}55`,
                  transform: [{ rotate: sweepRotate }],
                },
              ]}
            />

            {/* Solid Circular Logo Emblem */}
            <View
              style={[
                styles.emblemCircle,
                {
                  borderColor: palette.primary,
                  shadowColor: palette.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AttendSureLogo size={56} style={{ borderWidth: 0 }} />
            </View>
          </View>

          <Text style={[styles.brandTitle, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>AttendSure</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.brandSubtitle, { color: palette.textSecondary }]}>
            School Attendance System
          </Text>

          {/* 8-Dot Spiral Loading Spinner */}
          <View style={styles.spiralContainer} accessibilityLabel="Loading">
            {SPIRAL_DOTS.map((i) => {
              const angleDeg = `${i * 45}deg`;
              const phase = i / 8;

              const inputRange = [0, phase, (phase + 0.5) % 1, 1].sort((a, b) => a - b);
              const uniqueInput = Array.from(new Set(inputRange));
              const outputScale = uniqueInput.map((val) => {
                const dist = Math.abs(((val - phase + 1.5) % 1) - 0.5) * 2;
                return 1 - dist * 0.68;
              });
              const outputOpacity = uniqueInput.map((val) => {
                const dist = Math.abs(((val - phase + 1.5) % 1) - 0.5) * 2;
                return 1 - dist * 0.8;
              });

              const dotScale = spiralClock.interpolate({
                inputRange: uniqueInput,
                outputRange: outputScale,
              });
              const dotOpacity = spiralClock.interpolate({
                inputRange: uniqueInput,
                outputRange: outputOpacity,
              });

              return (
                <View key={i} style={[styles.spiralArm, { transform: [{ rotate: angleDeg }] }]}>
                  <Animated.View
                    style={[
                      styles.spiralDot,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: palette.textPrimary,
                        transform: [{ scale: dotScale }],
                        opacity: dotOpacity,
                      },
                    ]}
                  />
                </View>
              );
            })}
          </View>

          <Text style={[styles.waitText, { color: palette.textMuted }]}>Please wait...</Text>
        </View>

        {/* Bottom Footer */}
        <View style={styles.footerSection}>
          <Text style={[styles.signatureText, { color: palette.textMuted }]}>
            &copy; {currentYear} AttendSure &bull;{' '}
            <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: palette.textPrimary }}>TechBlazer</Text>
          </Text>
        </View>

        {/* Server Settings Modal */}
        <Modal
          visible={showToolsModal}
          transparent
          animationType="fade"
          onRequestClose={closeToolsAndContinue}
        >
          <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.modalCard,
                { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.modalHeaderRow}>
                <Ionicons name="server-outline" size={20} color={palette.primary} />
                <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
                  Server Settings
                </Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={[styles.modalSubtitle, { color: palette.textSecondary }]}>
                Enter your school server web address
              </Text>

              <TextInput
                style={[
                  styles.modalInput,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle,
                    borderColor: palette.borderStrong,
                    color: palette.textPrimary,
                  },
                ]}
                value={customServerUrl}
                onChangeText={setCustomServerUrl}
                placeholder="https://school.example.edu"
                placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                autoCapitalize="none"
                autoCorrect={false}
                keyboardType="url"
                maxLength={300}
                editable={!savingUrl}
              />

              <View style={styles.modalActions}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[
                    styles.modalCancelBtn,
                    { borderColor: palette.borderStrong, backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle },
                  ]}
                  onPress={closeToolsAndContinue}
                  disabled={savingUrl}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.modalCancelText, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
                    Cancel
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[
                    styles.modalSaveBtn,
                    { backgroundColor: palette.buttonBg, opacity: savingUrl ? 0.6 : 1 },
                  ]}
                  onPress={saveServerAddress}
                  disabled={savingUrl}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.modalSaveText, { color: palette.onPrimary }]}>Save</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Modal>
      </SafeAreaView>
    </Animated.View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    position: 'absolute',

  },

  brandSubtitle: {

  spiralContainer: {

    marginTop: 24,
    position: 'relative',
  },
  spiralArm: {
    position: 'absolute',
    top: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    left: 0,
    alignItems: 'center',

    height: 8,
    borderRadius: 4,

    fontWeight: '600',
    marginTop: 10,

    paddingBottom: 12,

const SAFE_RASTER_DATA_URI = /^data:image\/(png|jpeg|jpg|webp|gif);base64,/i;

export const isSafeUrl = (url?: string | null): url is string => {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return false;
  const trimmed = url.trim();
  const clean = trimmed.toLowerCase();
  if (!clean || ['null', 'undefined', 'none'].includes(clean)) return false;
  if (
    clean.startsWith('javascript:') ||
    clean.startsWith('vbscript:') ||
    clean.startsWith('file:') ||
    clean.startsWith('data:image/svg')
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  if (clean.startsWith('data:')) {
    return SAFE_RASTER_DATA_URI.test(trimmed);
  }
  if (clean.startsWith('/')) {
    return !clean.startsWith('//');
  }
  return clean.startsWith('http://') || clean.startsWith('https://');
};

export const cleanText = (val?: string | null, maxLen = 200): string => {
  if (!val) return '';
  return String(val)
    .replace(/[\u0000-\u001F\u007F<>]/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
    .slice(0, maxLen);
};

export const getFriendlyErrorMessage = (
  err: unknown,
  fallback = 'Something went wrong. Please try again.'
): string => {
  const e = err as {
    response?: { status?: number; data?: unknown };
    code?: string;
    message?: string;
  };
  const status = e?.response?.status;
  const data = e?.response?.data;

  const isUnsafeRaw = (text: string): boolean => {
    const trimmed = text.trim();
    return (
      trimmed.startsWith('<!DOCTYPE') ||
      trimmed.startsWith('<html') ||
      trimmed.includes('Traceback (most recent call last)') ||
      trimmed.includes('File "/') ||
      trimmed.length > 280
    );
  };

  if (typeof data === 'string' && data.trim() && !isUnsafeRaw(data)) {
    return data.trim();
  }

  if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
    const record = data as Record<string, unknown>;
    const candidate = record.detail || record.error || record.message;
    if (typeof candidate === 'string' && candidate.trim() && !isUnsafeRaw(candidate)) {
      return candidate.trim();
    }
    const firstVal = Object.values(record)[0];
    if (Array.isArray(firstVal) && typeof firstVal[0] === 'string' && !isUnsafeRaw(firstVal[0])) {
      return firstVal[0];
    }
    if (typeof firstVal === 'string' && firstVal.trim() && !isUnsafeRaw(firstVal)) {
      return firstVal.trim();
    }
  }

  if (status === 401) return 'Your session expired. Please sign in again.';
  if (status === 403) return 'You do not have permission to perform this action.';
  if (status === 404) return 'The requested information could not be found.';
  if (status && status >= 500) return 'The server is temporarily unavailable. Please try again shortly.';

  if (e?.code === 'ECONNABORTED' || e?.message?.toLowerCase().includes('timeout')) {
    return 'The request took too long. Please check your connection and try again.';
  }
  if (e?.message?.toLowerCase().includes('network error')) {
    return 'Cannot reach the school server. Check your Wi-Fi or server address.';
  }

  if (err instanceof Error && err.message && !isUnsafeRaw(err.message) && !err.message.includes('Request failed with status')) {
    return err.message;
  }

  return fallback;
};

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Authentication Context & Session Manager
 * File: mobile/src/context/AuthContext.tsx
 */

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { apiClient, AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, onAuthExpired } from '../api/client';

const USER_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_user_profile';

export interface AuthenticatedUser {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  first_name: string;
  last_name: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  full_name: string;
  faculty_name: string;
  faculty_id: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  system_role: string;
  position?: string;
  department?: string | null;
  division?: string | null;
  contact_number?: string;
  phone?: string;
  photo?: string | null;
  avatar?: string | null;
  school_name?: string;
  qr_token?: string | null;
  bound_device_id?: string | null;
  is_superuser: boolean;
  is_staff: boolean;
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: AuthenticatedUser | null;
  token: string | null;
  loading: boolean;
  isLoading: boolean;
  login: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshUser: () => Promise<void>;
  updateUser: (updatedFields: Partial<AuthenticatedUser>) => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const saveTokenSafely = async (token: string): Promise<void> => {
  await AsyncStorage.setItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
    try {
      await SecureStore.setItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
    } catch {}
  }
};

const readTokenSafely = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
    try {
      const secureToken = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
      if (secureToken) return secureToken;
    } catch {}
  }
  return AsyncStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
};

const clearTokenSafely = async (): Promise<void> => {
  await AsyncStorage.multiRemove([
    AUTH_TOKEN_KEY,
    USER_CACHE_KEY,
    'user_token',
    'attendsure_token',
    'attendsure_user',
    'attendsure_role',
  ]);
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
    try {
      await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
    } catch {}
  }
};

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<AuthenticatedUser | null>(null);
  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const isRefreshingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      abortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, []);

  const refreshUser = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isRefreshingRef.current) return;
    isRefreshingRef.current = true;
    abortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortRef.current = controller;

    try {
      const storedToken = await readTokenSafely();
      if (!storedToken) {
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setUser(null);
          setToken(null);
        }
        return;
      }

      const res = await apiClient.get('/api/me/', { signal: controller.signal });
      if (isMountedRef.current && res.data && res.data.id) {
        setUser(res.data);
        setToken(storedToken);
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.data));
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const anyErr = err as { response?: { status?: number }; code?: string };
      if (anyErr?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED') return;
      const status = anyErr?.response?.status;
      if (status === 401 || status === 403) {
        await clearTokenSafely();
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setUser(null);
          setToken(null);
        }
      }
    } finally {
      isRefreshingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const restoreSession = async () => {
      try {
        const storedToken = await readTokenSafely();
        const cachedUserStr = await AsyncStorage.getItem(USER_CACHE_KEY);

        if (isMountedRef.current && storedToken && cachedUserStr) {
          setToken(storedToken);
          setUser(JSON.parse(cachedUserStr));
        }

        if (storedToken) {
          await refreshUser();
        }
      } catch {
        await clearTokenSafely();
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setUser(null);
          setToken(null);
        }
      } finally {
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    void restoreSession();

    const unsubscribeAuthExpired = onAuthExpired(() => {
      clearTokenSafely().finally(() => {
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setToken(null);
          setUser(null);
        }
      });
    });

    return () => {
      unsubscribeAuthExpired();
    };
  }, [refreshUser]);

  const login = async (username: string, password: string): Promise<void> => {
    const cleanUsername = username.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '').trim();
    const cleanPassword = password.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '').trim();

    if (!cleanUsername || !cleanPassword) {
      throw new Error('Please enter both username and password.');
    }

    const res = await apiClient.post('/api/auth/login/', {
      username: cleanUsername,
      password: cleanPassword,
    });

    const d = res.data || {};
    const receivedToken: string | undefined = d.token || d.access || d.key;
    const receivedUser: AuthenticatedUser | undefined = d.user || (d.id ? d : undefined);

    if (!receivedToken || !receivedUser) {
      throw new Error('Invalid response received from authentication server.');
    }

    await saveTokenSafely(receivedToken);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(receivedUser));

    setToken(receivedToken);
    setUser(receivedUser);

    refreshUser().catch(() => {});
  };

  const logout = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await clearTokenSafely();
    setToken(null);
    setUser(null);
  };

  const updateUser = async (updatedFields: Partial<AuthenticatedUser>): Promise<void> => {
    if (!user) return;
    const nextUser = { ...user, ...updatedFields };
    setUser(nextUser);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextUser));
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        token,
        loading,
        isLoading: loading,
        login,
        logout,
        refreshUser,
        updateUser,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = (): AuthContextType => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

export default AuthContext;

Relative Path : mobile\src\context\ThemeContext.tsx
Metrics       : 102 lines | 2.7 KB

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Live Theme Context (Light, Dark & High-Contrast)
 * File: mobile/src/context/ThemeContext.tsx
 */

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import {
  PALETTES,
  FONTS,
  SPACING,
  RADIUS,
  createMobileStyles,
  type ThemeMode,
  type ThemePalette,
} from '../constants/theme';

const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'attendsure_color_mode';

interface ThemeContextValue {
  mode: ThemeMode;
  isDark: boolean;
  palette: ThemePalette;
  fonts: typeof FONTS;
  spacing: typeof SPACING;
  radius: typeof RADIUS;
  ui: ReturnType<typeof createMobileStyles>;
  setThemeMode: (mode: ThemeMode) => void;
  toggleDarkMode: () => void;
  setDarkMode: (value: boolean) => void;
}

const defaultPalette = PALETTES.light;

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextValue>({
  mode: 'light',
  isDark: false,
  palette: defaultPalette,
  fonts: FONTS,
  spacing: SPACING,
  radius: RADIUS,
  ui: createMobileStyles(defaultPalette),
  setThemeMode: () => {},
  toggleDarkMode: () => {},
  setDarkMode: () => {},
});

export const ThemeProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<ThemeMode>('light');

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    AsyncStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)
      .then((saved) => {
        if (mounted && (saved === 'dark' || saved === 'light' || saved === 'high-contrast')) {
          setMode(saved);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const setThemeMode = (nextMode: ThemeMode) => {
    setMode(nextMode);
    AsyncStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, nextMode).catch(() => {});
  };

  const setDarkMode = (value: boolean) => setThemeMode(value ? 'dark' : 'light');

  // Cycles: Light -> Dark -> High-Contrast Daylight
  const toggleDarkMode = () => {
    const order: ThemeMode[] = ['light', 'dark', 'high-contrast'];
    const next = order[(order.indexOf(mode) + 1) % order.length];
    setThemeMode(next);
  };

  const palette = PALETTES[mode];
  const ui = useMemo(() => createMobileStyles(palette), [palette]);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      mode,
      isDark: palette.isDark,
      palette,
      fonts: FONTS,
      spacing: SPACING,
      radius: RADIUS,
      ui,
      setThemeMode,
      toggleDarkMode,
      setDarkMode,
    }),
    [mode, palette, ui]
  );

  return <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ThemeContext.Provider>;
};

export const useThemeMode = () => useContext(ThemeContext);
export type { ThemePalette };

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Root Stack Navigator
 * File: mobile/src/navigation/AppNavigator.tsx
 */

import React from 'react';
import { View, ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { createNativeStackNavigator } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';

import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import LoginScreen from '../screens/LoginScreen';
import MainTabNavigator from './MainTabNavigator';
import ProfileSettingsScreen from '../screens/ProfileSettingsScreen';
import ReportsScreen from '../screens/ReportsScreen';

export type RootStackParamList = {
  Login: undefined;
  Main: undefined;
  ProfileSettings: undefined;
  Reports: undefined;
};

const Stack = createNativeStackNavigator<RootStackParamList>();

export const AppNavigator: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, isLoading, loading } = useAuth();
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();
  const isCheckingSession = Boolean(isLoading ?? loading);

  if (isCheckingSession) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.loadingContainer, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
        <ActivityIndicator size="large" color={palette.primary} />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Stack.Navigator
      screenOptions={{
        headerShown: false,
        contentStyle: { backgroundColor: palette.background },
      }}
    >
      {!user ? (
        <Stack.Screen name="Login" component={LoginScreen} />
      ) : (
        <>
          <Stack.Screen name="Main" component={MainTabNavigator} />
          <Stack.Screen
            name="ProfileSettings"
            component={ProfileSettingsScreen}
            options={{
              headerShown: false,
              animation: 'slide_from_right',
            }}
          />
          <Stack.Screen
            name="Reports"
            component={ReportsScreen}
            options={{
              headerShown: false,
              animation: 'slide_from_right',
            }}
          />
        </>
      )}
    </Stack.Navigator>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  loadingContainer: {
    flex: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
});

export default AppNavigator;

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Role-Aware Main Tab Navigator (Faculty & Parent Modes)
 * File: mobile/src/navigation/MainTabNavigator.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Text,
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { getFriendlyErrorMessage, type ThemePalette } from '../constants/theme';

import DashboardScreen from '../screens/DashboardScreen';
import ParentPortalScreen from '../screens/ParentPortalScreen';
import CalendarScreen from '../screens/CalendarScreen';
import ScannerScreen from '../screens/ScannerScreen';
import ReportsScreen from '../screens/ReportsScreen';
import MenuScreen from '../screens/MenuScreen';
import SettingsScreen from '../screens/SettingsScreen';
import GatePassScreen from '../screens/GatePassScreen';
import ChatScreen from '../screens/ChatScreen';
import AdvisoryMatrixScreen from '../screens/AdvisoryMatrixScreen';
import DTRReportView, { DTRReportData } from '../components/reports/DTRReportView';

export type TabType =
  | 'home'
  | 'class_list'
  | 'gate_pass'
  | 'chat'
  | 'menu'
  | 'scanner'
  | 'reports'
  | 'calendar'
  | 'settings'
  | 'dtr';

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};

const showNotice = (title: string, message: string): void => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

export const MainTabNavigator: React.FC = () => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { palette, fonts } = useThemeMode();
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(palette, fonts), [palette, fonts]);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>('home');

  const isParentUser = useMemo(() => {
    const role = String(user?.role || user?.system_role || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return role === 'PARENT';
  }, [user?.role, user?.system_role]);

  const [dtrMonth, setDtrMonth] = useState<number>(new Date().getMonth() + 1);
  const [dtrYear, setDtrYear] = useState<number>(new Date().getFullYear());
  const [dtrData, setDtrData] = useState<DTRReportData | null>(null);
  const [dtrLoading, setDtrLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [dtrSubmitting, setDtrSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const dtrAbortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const fetchDTR = useCallback(
    async (silent = false) => {
      if (isParentUser) return;
      dtrAbortRef.current?.abort();
      const controller = new AbortController();
      dtrAbortRef.current = controller;

      if (!silent && isMounted.current) setDtrLoading(true);
      const cacheKey = `attendsure_dtr_menu_${dtrMonth}_${dtrYear}`;

      try {
        const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(cacheKey);
        if (cached && isMounted.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
          setDtrData(JSON.parse(cached));
        }
      } catch {}

      try {
        const res = await apiClient.get<DTRReportData>('reports/dtr/', {
          params: { month: dtrMonth, year: dtrYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (isMounted.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
          setDtrData(res.data);
          await AsyncStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(res.data)).catch(() => {});
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (isCanceledError(err)) return;
      } finally {
        if (isMounted.current && !silent) {
          setDtrLoading(false);
        }
      }
    },
    [dtrMonth, dtrYear, isParentUser]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
      dtrAbortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'dtr' && !isParentUser) {
      void fetchDTR(false);
    }
    return () => {
      dtrAbortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [activeTab, fetchDTR, isParentUser]);

  const handleSubmitDtr = async () => {
    if (!dtrData || dtrSubmitting) return;
    setDtrSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const encodedFacultyId = encodeURIComponent(String(dtrData.faculty_id));
      await apiClient.post(`reports/dtr/${encodedFacultyId}/`, {
        action: 'SUBMIT_DTR',
        faculty_id: dtrData.faculty_id,
        month: dtrData.month_number,
        year: dtrData.year,
        regular_days_hours: dtrData.regular_days_hours,
        saturdays_hours: dtrData.saturdays_hours,
      });
      showNotice('Submitted', 'Your Daily Time Record has been sent to your Department Head.');
      void fetchDTR(true);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      showNotice(
        'Unable to Submit',
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not submit your Daily Time Record right now.')
      );
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setDtrSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const renderSubHeader = (title: string, subtitle: string, backTarget: TabType = 'menu') => (
    <View style={[styles.subHeader, { paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 14) }]}>
      <View style={styles.subHeaderTop}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.backCircle}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab(backTarget)}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
          accessibilityLabel="Go back"
        >
          <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={palette.onPrimary} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <View style={styles.subHeaderTextWrap}>
          <Text style={styles.subHeaderEyebrow}>PROFILE &amp; TOOLS</Text>
          <Text style={styles.subHeaderTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
            {title}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.subHeaderSubtitle} numberOfLines={1}>
            {subtitle}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  const navItems: Array<{
    key: TabType;
    label: string;
    icon: keyof typeof Ionicons.glyphMap;
    activeIcon: keyof typeof Ionicons.glyphMap;
  }> = useMemo(
    () =>
      isParentUser
        ? [
            { key: 'home', label: 'Children', icon: 'people-outline', activeIcon: 'people' },
            { key: 'gate_pass', label: 'Passes', icon: 'clipboard-outline', activeIcon: 'clipboard' },
            { key: 'chat', label: 'Messages', icon: 'chatbubble-outline', activeIcon: 'chatbubble' },
            { key: 'settings', label: 'Account', icon: 'person-outline', activeIcon: 'person' },
          ]
        : [
            { key: 'home', label: 'Home', icon: 'home-outline', activeIcon: 'home' },
            { key: 'class_list', label: 'Classes', icon: 'book-outline', activeIcon: 'book' },
            { key: 'gate_pass', label: 'Passes', icon: 'clipboard-outline', activeIcon: 'clipboard' },
            { key: 'chat', label: 'Messages', icon: 'chatbubble-outline', activeIcon: 'chatbubble' },
            { key: 'menu', label: 'Profile', icon: 'person-outline', activeIcon: 'person' },
          ],
    [isParentUser]
  );

  const menuSubTabs: TabType[] = ['menu', 'settings', 'calendar', 'dtr', 'reports', 'scanner'];
  const resolvedNavKey: TabType = isParentUser
    ? activeTab
    : menuSubTabs.includes(activeTab)
      ? 'menu'
      : activeTab;

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.screenContainer}>
        {activeTab === 'home' &&
          (isParentUser ? (
            <ParentPortalScreen onNavigate={setActiveTab} />
          ) : (
            <DashboardScreen onNavigate={setActiveTab} />
          ))}
        {activeTab === 'class_list' && !isParentUser && <AdvisoryMatrixScreen />}
        {activeTab === 'gate_pass' && <GatePassScreen onBack={() => setActiveTab('home')} />}
        {activeTab === 'chat' && <ChatScreen />}
        {activeTab === 'menu' && !isParentUser && <MenuScreen onSelectScreen={setActiveTab} />}
        {activeTab === 'scanner' && !isParentUser && (
          <ScannerScreen onBack={() => setActiveTab('menu')} />
        )}
        {activeTab === 'reports' && !isParentUser && (
          <View style={styles.subScreenFill}>
            {renderSubHeader('School Reports', 'SF1, SF2, SF4 and Form 48 records', 'menu')}
            <ReportsScreen embedded />
          </View>
        )}
        {activeTab === 'calendar' && (
          <View style={styles.subScreenFill}>
            {renderSubHeader(
              'School Calendar',
              'Suspensions, holidays and schedules',
              isParentUser ? 'home' : 'menu'
            )}
            <CalendarScreen />
          </View>
        )}
        {activeTab === 'settings' && (
          <View style={styles.subScreenFill}>
            {renderSubHeader(
              'Settings',
              'Profile, color mode and server address',
              isParentUser ? 'home' : 'menu'
            )}
            <SettingsScreen />
          </View>
        )}
        {activeTab === 'dtr' && !isParentUser && (
          <View style={styles.subScreenFill}>
            {renderSubHeader('Daily Time Record', 'Civil Service Form No. 48', 'menu')}
            <ScrollView
              contentContainerStyle={styles.subScreenScroll}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
              refreshControl={
                <RefreshControl
                  refreshing={dtrLoading}
                  onRefresh={() => void fetchDTR(false)}
                  colors={[palette.primary]}
                  tintColor={palette.primary}
                />
              }
            >
              {dtrLoading && !dtrData ? (
                <View style={styles.loadingBox}>
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.primary} />
                  <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading attendance records...</Text>
                </View>
              ) : (
                <DTRReportView
                  data={dtrData}
                  selectedMonth={dtrMonth}
                  selectedYear={dtrYear}
                  onMonthChange={(m, y) => {
                    setDtrMonth(m);
                    setDtrYear(y);
                  }}
                  submitting={dtrSubmitting}
                  onSubmit={handleSubmitDtr}
                  onRefreshNeeded={() => void fetchDTR(true)}
                />
              )}
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Floating Bottom Navigation Dock */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.floatingDockWrapper,
          { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 10) },
        ]}
        pointerEvents="box-none"
      >
        <View style={styles.floatingDock}>
          {navItems.map((item) => {
            const isFocused = resolvedNavKey === item.key;
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={item.key}
                onPress={() => setActiveTab(item.key)}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                style={[styles.navPillBtn, isFocused && styles.navPillBtnActive]}
                accessibilityRole="tab"
                accessibilityState={{ selected: isFocused }}
                accessibilityLabel={item.label}
              >
                <Ionicons
                  name={isFocused ? item.activeIcon : item.icon}
                  size={20}
                  color={isFocused ? palette.primary : palette.textMuted}
                />
                {isFocused && (
                  <Text style={styles.navPillText} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {item.label}
                  </Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Class List & Attendance Monitor
 * File: mobile/src/screens/AdvisoryMatrixScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  ActivityIndicator,
  RefreshControl,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { getServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';
import {
  isSafeUrl,
  cleanText,
  getFriendlyErrorMessage,
} from '../constants/theme';

const CLASS_LIST_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_advisory_class_list';

interface SubjectStatus {
  schedule_id: number;
  subject_code: string;
  status: 'PRESENT' | 'LATE' | 'CUTTING' | 'UNVERIFIED' | 'UNSCANNED';
  time: string;
}

interface AdviseeRow {
  number: number;
  student_id: number;
  student_name: string;
  lrn: string;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  gate_status: string;
  gate_direction: 'IN' | 'OUT' | 'NONE';
  attended_count: number;
  cutting_count: number;
  total_subjects: number;
  compliance_rate: number;
  subjects: SubjectStatus[];
}

interface CachedClassListPayload {
  has_advisory_class: boolean;
  section_name: string;
  grade_level: string;
  matrix: AdviseeRow[];
}

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};

export const AdvisoryMatrixScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { palette, ui, fonts } = useThemeMode();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [filterMode, setFilterMode] = useState<'ALL' | 'CUTTING'>('ALL');
  const [hasAdvisoryClass, setHasAdvisoryClass] = useState(false);
  const [sectionName, setSectionName] = useState('');
  const [gradeLevel, setGradeLevel] = useState('');
  const [matrix, setMatrix] = useState<AdviseeRow[]>([]);
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState('');

  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const fetchClassListData = useCallback(async (fromCacheFirst = false) => {
    abortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortRef.current = controller;

    if (fromCacheFirst) {
      try {
        const [cachedStr, base] = await Promise.all([
          AsyncStorage.getItem(CLASS_LIST_CACHE_KEY),
          getServerBaseUrl(),
        ]);
        if (isMountedRef.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
          if (base) setServerBase(base);
          if (cachedStr) {
            const parsed: CachedClassListPayload = JSON.parse(cachedStr);
            setHasAdvisoryClass(Boolean(parsed.has_advisory_class));
            setSectionName(parsed.section_name || '');
            setGradeLevel(parsed.grade_level || '');
            setMatrix(Array.isArray(parsed.matrix) ? parsed.matrix : []);
          }
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<CachedClassListPayload>('classroom/adviser-matrix/', {
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      if (!isMountedRef.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;

      setErrorMsg(null);
      if (res.data?.has_advisory_class) {
        const payload: CachedClassListPayload = {
          has_advisory_class: true,
          section_name: res.data.section_name || '',
          grade_level: res.data.grade_level || '',
          matrix: Array.isArray(res.data.matrix) ? res.data.matrix : [],
        };
        setHasAdvisoryClass(true);
        setSectionName(payload.section_name);
        setGradeLevel(payload.grade_level);
        setMatrix(payload.matrix);
        AsyncStorage.setItem(CLASS_LIST_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload)).catch(() => {});
      } else {
        setHasAdvisoryClass(false);
        setMatrix([]);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current || isCanceledError(err)) return;
      setErrorMsg(getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not load your class list right now.'));
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    void fetchClassListData(true);
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      abortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [fetchClassListData]);

  const resolveSafePhoto = useCallback(
    (url?: string | null): string | undefined => {
      if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return undefined;
      const trimmed = url.trim();
      if (!trimmed) return undefined;
      const candidate =
        trimmed.startsWith('http://') ||
        trimmed.startsWith('https://') ||
        trimmed.startsWith('data:')
          ? trimmed
          : serverBase
          ? `${serverBase.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${trimmed.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`
          : '';
      return isSafeUrl(candidate) ? candidate : undefined;
    },
    [serverBase]
  );

  const cleanSearch = cleanText(search, 80).toLowerCase();

  const filteredMatrix = useMemo(() => {
    return matrix.filter((row) => {
      const matchSearch =
        !cleanSearch ||
        String(row.student_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(cleanSearch) ||
        String(row.lrn || '').toLowerCase().includes(cleanSearch);
      if (!matchSearch) return false;
      return filterMode === 'CUTTING' ? row.cutting_count > 0 : true;
    });
  }, [matrix, cleanSearch, filterMode]);

  const cuttingTotal = useMemo(
    () => matrix.filter((m) => m.cutting_count > 0).length,
    [matrix]
  );

  const getInitials = (name: string) => {
    const parts = String(name || '')
      .replace(/,/g, '')
      .trim()
      .split(/\s+/)
      .filter(Boolean);
    return parts.length >= 2
      ? `${parts[0][0]}${parts[1][0]}`.toUpperCase()
      : String(name || 'ST').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  };

  if (loading && matrix.length === 0) {
    return (
      <View style={[ui.screen, ui.centerBox]}>
        <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.primary} />
        <Text style={[ui.subtitle, { marginTop: 8 }]}>Loading class list...</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (!hasAdvisoryClass) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={[ui.screen, ui.centerBox]} edges={['top']}>
        <Ionicons name="information-circle-outline" size={44} color={palette.textMuted} />
        <Text style={[ui.title, { marginTop: 8 }]}>No Assigned Advisory Class</Text>
        <Text style={[ui.subtitle, { textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 4 }]}>
          {errorMsg || 'Your faculty account is not assigned as a Class Adviser yet.'}
        </Text>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[ui.buttonPrimary, { marginTop: 16, paddingHorizontal: 20 }]}
          onPress={() => {
            setLoading(true);
            void fetchClassListData(false);
          }}
        >
          <Ionicons name="refresh-outline" size={16} color={palette.onPrimary} />
          <Text style={ui.buttonPrimaryText}>Refresh</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={ui.screen} edges={['top']}>
      {/* Header */}
      <View
        style={{
          padding: 14,
          backgroundColor: palette.surface,
          borderBottomWidth: 1,
          borderBottomColor: palette.border,
        }}
      >
        <Text style={ui.title}>Class List</Text>
        <Text style={ui.subtitle}>
          {[gradeLevel, sectionName].filter(Boolean).join(' - ')} • {matrix.length} Students
        </Text>

        <View style={[ui.rowCenter, { marginTop: 10 }]}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setFilterMode('ALL')}
            style={[
              ui.badge,
              {
                backgroundColor:
                  filterMode === 'ALL' ? palette.buttonBg : palette.surfaceSubtle,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: filterMode === 'ALL' ? palette.buttonBg : palette.border,
                paddingVertical: 6,
                paddingHorizontal: 12,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={{
                fontSize: fonts.size.xs,
                fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold,
                color: filterMode === 'ALL' ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary,
              }}
            >
              All ({matrix.length})
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setFilterMode('CUTTING')}
            style={[
              ui.badge,
              {
                backgroundColor:
                  filterMode === 'CUTTING' ? palette.dangerBg : palette.surfaceSubtle,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: filterMode === 'CUTTING' ? palette.dangerBg : palette.border,
                paddingVertical: 6,
                paddingHorizontal: 12,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Text
              style={{
                fontSize: fonts.size.xs,
                fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold,
                color: filterMode === 'CUTTING' ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary,
              }}
            >
              Missed Period ({cuttingTotal})
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {errorMsg ? (
        <View
          style={{
            marginHorizontal: 12,
            marginTop: 10,
            padding: 10,
            borderRadius: 10,
            backgroundColor: palette.warningLight,
            borderWidth: 1,
            borderColor: palette.warning,
          }}
        >
          <Text style={{ fontSize: fonts.xs, color: palette.textPrimary, fontWeight: fonts.weight.semibold }}>
            {errorMsg}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {/* Search Bar */}
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingTop: 10 }}>
        <View style={[ui.input, ui.rowCenter, { height: 42 }]}>
          <Ionicons name="search" size={16} color={palette.textMuted} />
          <TextInput
            style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: fonts.size.sm, color: palette.textPrimary }}
            placeholder="Search student name or LRN..."
            placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
            value={search}
            onChangeText={setSearch}
            maxLength={80}
          />
          {search.length > 0 && (
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearch('')}>
              <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={18} color={palette.textMuted} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Class List */}
      <FlatList
        data={filteredMatrix}
        keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.student_id)}
        contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 12, paddingBottom: 115 }}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={() => {
              setRefreshing(true);
              void fetchClassListData(false);
            }}
            colors={[palette.primary]}
            tintColor={palette.primary}
          />
        }
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: 40, paddingHorizontal: 20 }}>
            <Ionicons name="people-outline" size={38} color={palette.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[ui.heading, { marginTop: 8, textAlign: 'center' }]}>
              No matching students
            </Text>
            <Text style={[ui.subtitle, { textAlign: 'center' }]}>
              {filterMode === 'CUTTING'
                ? 'No students have missed periods right now.'
                : 'Try clearing your search or pulling down to refresh.'}
            </Text>
          </View>
        }
        renderItem={({ item }) => {
          const isCutting = item.cutting_count > 0;
          const safePhoto = resolveSafePhoto(item.photo_url);

          return (
            <View style={[ui.card, isCutting && { borderColor: palette.danger }]}>
              <View style={ui.rowBetween}>
                <View style={[ui.rowCenter, { flex: 1 }]}>
                  <View style={[ui.subtleBox, { paddingVertical: 3, paddingHorizontal: 7 }]}>
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        fontSize: fonts.size.xs,
                        fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                        color: palette.textSecondary,
                      }}
                    >
                      {item.number}
                    </Text>
                  </View>

                  <View
                    style={{
                      width: 38,
                      height: 38,
                      borderRadius: 19,
                      backgroundColor: palette.primarySoft,
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      overflow: 'hidden',
                    }}
                  >
                    {safePhoto ? (
                      <Image source={{ uri: safePhoto }} style={{ width: 38, height: 38 }} contentFit="cover" />
                    ) : (
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          fontSize: fonts.size.sm,
                          fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                          color: palette.primary,
                        }}
                      >
                        {getInitials(item.student_name)}
                      </Text>
                    )}
                  </View>

                  <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        fontSize: fonts.size.md,
                        fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                        color: palette.textPrimary,
                      }}
                      numberOfLines={1}
                    >
                      {item.student_name}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={ui.subtitle}>LRN: {item.lrn}</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      fontSize: fonts.size.md,
                      fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                      color: item.compliance_rate >= 80 ? palette.success : palette.danger,
                    }}
                  >
                    {item.attended_count}/{item.total_subjects}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={ui.subtitle}>{item.compliance_rate}%</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              {/* Subject Status Pills */}
              <View
                style={{
                  flexDirection: 'row',
                  flexWrap: 'wrap',
                  gap: 6,
                  marginTop: 10,
                  paddingTop: 8,
                  borderTopWidth: 1,
                  borderTopColor: palette.border,
                }}
              >
                {(Array.isArray(item.subjects) ? item.subjects : []).map((sub) => {
                  const sColor =
                    sub.status === 'PRESENT'
                      ? palette.success
                      : sub.status === 'LATE'
                      ? palette.warning
                      : sub.status === 'CUTTING'
                      ? palette.danger
                      : palette.textMuted;
                  const sBg =
                    sub.status === 'PRESENT'
                      ? palette.successLight
                      : sub.status === 'LATE'
                      ? palette.warningLight
                      : sub.status === 'CUTTING'
                      ? palette.dangerLight
                      : palette.surfaceSubtle;
                  return (
                    <View key={sub.schedule_id} style={[ui.badge, { backgroundColor: sBg }]}>
                      <Text
                        style={{
                          fontSize: fonts.size.xxs,
                          fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                          color: sColor,
                        }}
                      >
                        {sub.subject_code}: {sub.status === 'UNSCANNED' ? '—' : sub.status}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  );
                })}
              </View>
            </View>
          );
        }}
      />
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

export default AdvisoryMatrixScreen;
