/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - School Calendar & Schedule Overrides
 * File: mobile/src/screens/CalendarScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Switch,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  Alert,
  ActivityIndicator,
  RefreshControl,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import {
  cleanText,
  getFriendlyErrorMessage,
  type ThemePalette,
} from '../constants/theme';

const CACHED_CALENDAR_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_calendar_data';

interface CalendarException {
  id: number;
  type: 'SUSPENSION' | 'HOLIDAY' | 'EVENT';
  date: string;
  reason: string;
  affects_all_grades?: boolean;
  created_at?: string;
}

const getLocalTodayDateString = (): string => {
  const now = new Date();
  const year = now.getFullYear();
  const month = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

const isValidIsoDate = (dateStr: string): boolean => {
  const match = /^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])$/.exec(dateStr);
  if (!match) return false;
  const year = Number(match[1]);
  const month = Number(match[2]);
  const day = Number(match[3]);
  const dt = new Date(year, month - 1, day);
  return dt.getFullYear() === year && dt.getMonth() === month - 1 && dt.getDate() === day;
};

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};

const showAlert = (title: string, message: string): void => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

export const CalendarScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { palette, fonts } = useThemeMode();
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(palette, fonts), [palette, fonts]);

  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const rawRole = String(currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || '')
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase();
  const isAdmin =
    rawRole === 'ADMIN' ||
    rawRole === 'SUPERADMIN' ||
    rawRole === 'PRINCIPAL' ||
    (Boolean(currentUser?.is_superuser) && rawRole !== 'FACULTY');

  const [weekendRestEnabled, setWeekendRestEnabled] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const weekendRestRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const [editingExceptionId, setEditingExceptionId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<'SUSPENSION' | 'HOLIDAY' | 'EVENT'>('SUSPENSION');
  const [exceptionDate, setExceptionDate] = useState<string>(getLocalTodayDateString);
  const [exceptionReason, setExceptionReason] = useState<string>('');
  const [exceptions, setExceptions] = useState<CalendarException[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // 1. Cache-First Initialization
  const loadCachedCalendar = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(CACHED_CALENDAR_KEY);
      if (cached && isMounted.current) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(cached);
        if (typeof parsed.weekend_rest_enabled === 'boolean') {
          weekendRestRef.current = parsed.weekend_rest_enabled;
          setWeekendRestEnabled(parsed.weekend_rest_enabled);
        }
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.exceptions)) {
          setExceptions(parsed.exceptions);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Live network fetch will populate state
    }
  }, []);

  // 2. Stable Data Fetching Callback
  const fetchCalendarData = useCallback(async () => {
    abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get('/calendar/exceptions/', {
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      const payload = res.data;

      if (!isMounted.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;

      let nextWeekendRest = weekendRestRef.current;
      let nextExceptions: CalendarException[] = [];

      if (payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload)) {
        if (typeof payload.weekend_rest_enabled === 'boolean') {
          nextWeekendRest = payload.weekend_rest_enabled;
          weekendRestRef.current = payload.weekend_rest_enabled;
          setWeekendRestEnabled(payload.weekend_rest_enabled);
        }
        nextExceptions = Array.isArray(payload.results) ? payload.results : [];
      } else if (Array.isArray(payload)) {
        nextExceptions = payload;
      }

      setExceptions(nextExceptions);

      AsyncStorage.setItem(
        CACHED_CALENDAR_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({
          weekend_rest_enabled: nextWeekendRest,
          exceptions: nextExceptions,
        })
      ).catch(() => {});
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (isCanceledError(err)) return;
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    void loadCachedCalendar().then(() => {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        void fetchCalendarData();
      }
    });

    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
      abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [loadCachedCalendar, fetchCalendarData]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    void fetchCalendarData();
  }, [fetchCalendarData]);

  // 3. Weekend Rest Toggle
  const handleToggleWeekendRest = async (value: boolean) => {
    if (!isAdmin) return;
    const previousValue = weekendRestEnabled;
    weekendRestRef.current = value;
    setWeekendRestEnabled(value);

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.patch('/calendar/exceptions/', {
        weekend_rest_enabled: value,
      });
      if (isMounted.current && typeof res.data?.weekend_rest_enabled === 'boolean') {
        weekendRestRef.current = res.data.weekend_rest_enabled;
        setWeekendRestEnabled(res.data.weekend_rest_enabled);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      weekendRestRef.current = previousValue;
      if (isMounted.current) setWeekendRestEnabled(previousValue);
      showAlert(
        'Update Failed',
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not save weekend rest setting.')
      );
    }
  };

  // 4. Input Sanitization & Override Submission
  const handleAddException = async () => {
    if (!isAdmin) {
      showAlert('Permission Required', 'Only school administrators can add calendar overrides.');
      return;
    }

    const cleanedReason = cleanText(exceptionReason, 255);
    const cleanedDate = cleanText(exceptionDate, 10);

    if (!cleanedReason) {
      showAlert('Required', 'Please enter a reason or school notice.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidIsoDate(cleanedDate)) {
      showAlert('Invalid Date', 'Please enter a valid date in YYYY-MM-DD format.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const payload = {
        type: selectedType,
        date: cleanedDate,
        reason: cleanedReason,
        affects_all_grades: true,
      };

      if (editingExceptionId) {
        const res = await apiClient.patch<CalendarException>(
          `/calendar/exceptions/${encodeURIComponent(String(editingExceptionId))}/`,
          payload
        );
        if (!isMounted.current) return;
        if (res.data && res.data.id) {
          setExceptions((prev) => prev.map((i) => (i.id === res.data.id ? res.data : i)));
        } else {
          await fetchCalendarData();
        }
        setEditingExceptionId(null);
        setExceptionReason('');
        showAlert('Updated', 'Calendar override updated.');
      } else {
        const res = await apiClient.post<CalendarException>('/calendar/exceptions/', payload);
        if (!isMounted.current) return;

        if (res.data && res.data.id) {
          setExceptions((prev) => [res.data, ...prev.filter((i) => i.id !== res.data.id)]);
        } else {
          await fetchCalendarData();
        }

        setExceptionReason('');
        showAlert('Saved', 'Calendar override saved.');
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMounted.current) return;
      showAlert(
        'Save Failed',
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not save calendar override right now.')
      );
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setSubmitting(false);
      }
    }
  };

  // 5. Deletion Handler with Numeric ID Validation
  const executeDeleteException = async (id: number) => {
    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) return;

    try {
      await apiClient.delete(`/calendar/exceptions/${encodeURIComponent(String(id))}/`);
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setExceptions((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      showAlert('Error', getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not remove this calendar override.'));
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteException = (id: number) => {
    if (!isAdmin) {
      showAlert('Permission Required', 'Only school administrators can remove overrides.');
      return;
    }

    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      if (window.confirm('Remove this calendar override?')) {
        void executeDeleteException(id);
      }
      return;
    }

    Alert.alert('Confirm Delete', 'Remove this calendar override?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      {
        text: 'Delete',
        style: 'destructive',
        onPress: () => void executeDeleteException(id),
      },
    ]);
  };

  const getBadgeColors = (type: CalendarException['type']) => {
    if (type === 'SUSPENSION') return { bg: palette.dangerLight, text: palette.danger };
    if (type === 'HOLIDAY') return { bg: palette.warningLight, text: palette.warning };
    return { bg: palette.infoLight, text: palette.info };
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={onRefresh}
            colors={[palette.primary]}
            tintColor={palette.primary}
          />
        }
      >
        {/* Weekend Rest Card */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.weekendCardRow}>
            <View style={styles.cardIconBox}>
              <Ionicons name="calendar" size={20} color={palette.primary} />
            </View>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 10 }}>
              <Text style={styles.cardHeading}>Weekend Rest</Text>
              <Text style={styles.cardDetailText}>
                Saturdays and Sundays are excluded from required attendance logging
              </Text>
            </View>
            {isAdmin ? (
              <Switch
                value={weekendRestEnabled}
                onValueChange={(val) => void handleToggleWeekendRest(val)}
                trackColor={{ false: palette.borderStrong, true: palette.primarySoft }}
                thumbColor={weekendRestEnabled ? palette.buttonBg : palette.surface}
              />
            ) : (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.readOnlyPill,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: weekendRestEnabled
                      ? palette.successLight
                      : palette.surfaceSubtle,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.readOnlyPillText,
                    { color: weekendRestEnabled ? palette.success : palette.textMuted },
                  ]}
                >
                  {weekendRestEnabled ? 'ACTIVE' : 'OFF'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Admin Form: Add / Edit Override */}
        {isAdmin ? (
          <View style={styles.card}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderTitle}>
              {editingExceptionId ? 'EDIT SCHEDULE OVERRIDE' : 'ADD SCHEDULE OVERRIDE'}
            </Text>

            <View style={styles.fieldRow}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Effective Date (YYYY-MM-DD)</Text>
              <View style={styles.inputBox}>
                <Ionicons
                  name="calendar-outline"
                  size={16}
                  color={palette.textMuted}
                  style={{ marginRight: 8 }}
                />
                <TextInput
                  style={styles.inputText}
                  value={exceptionDate}
                  onChangeText={setExceptionDate}
                  placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD"
                  placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  maxLength={10}
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.fieldRow}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Category</Text>
              <View style={styles.typeSelectorRow}>
                {(['SUSPENSION', 'HOLIDAY', 'EVENT'] as const).map((type) => {
                  const active = selectedType === type;
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={type}
                      style={[styles.typePill, active && styles.typePillActive]}
                      onPress={() => setSelectedType(type)}
                      activeOpacity={0.8}
                    >
                      <Text style={[styles.typePillText, active && styles.typePillTextActive]}>
                        {type}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.fieldRow}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Reason / School Notice</Text>
              <View style={[styles.inputBox, { height: 44 }]}>
                <Ionicons
                  name="document-text-outline"
                  size={16}
                  color={palette.textMuted}
                  style={{ marginRight: 8 }}
                />
                <TextInput
                  style={styles.inputText}
                  placeholder="Enter official reason or notice"
                  placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                  value={exceptionReason}
                  onChangeText={setExceptionReason}
                  maxLength={255}
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.addOverrideBtn, submitting && styles.btnDisabled]}
              onPress={() => void handleAddException()}
              disabled={submitting}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
            >
              {submitting ? (
                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.onPrimary} />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Ionicons
                    name="add-circle-outline"
                    size={16}
                    color={palette.onPrimary}
                    style={{ marginRight: 6 }}
                  />
                  <Text style={styles.addOverrideBtnText}>
                    {editingExceptionId ? 'Save Changes' : 'Save Override'}
                  </Text>
                </>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
            {editingExceptionId ? (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={{ marginTop: 8, alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: 6 }}
                onPress={() => {
                  setEditingExceptionId(null);
                  setExceptionReason('');
                  setExceptionDate(getLocalTodayDateString());
                }}
              >
                <Text style={{ fontSize: fonts.xs, fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold, color: palette.textSecondary }}>
                  Cancel Edit
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.infoBanner}>
            <Ionicons name="information-circle-outline" size={18} color={palette.primary} />
            <Text style={styles.infoBannerText}>
              Class suspensions, holidays, and schedule overrides are managed by the School Admin.
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Active Overrides List */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.listHeaderRow}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderTitle}>
              ACTIVE OVERRIDES ({exceptions.length})
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.listSubText}>Overrides regular schedule</Text>
          </View>

          {loading && exceptions.length === 0 ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.primary} style={{ marginVertical: 14 }} />
          ) : exceptions.length === 0 ? (
            <Text style={styles.noExceptionsText}>No active date overrides set.</Text>
          ) : (
            exceptions.map((item) => {
              const badge = getBadgeColors(item.type);
              return (
                <View key={String(item.id)} style={styles.exceptionItemRow}>
                  <View style={[styles.typeBadge, { backgroundColor: badge.bg }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.typeBadgeText, { color: badge.text }]}>{item.type}</Text>
                  </View>

                  <View style={{ flex: 1, marginHorizontal: 12 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.exceptionDateText}>{item.date}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.exceptionReasonText}>{item.reason}</Text>
                  </View>

                  {isAdmin && (
                    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 }}>
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() => {
                          setEditingExceptionId(item.id);
                          setSelectedType(item.type || 'HOLIDAY');
                          setExceptionDate(item.date || getLocalTodayDateString());
                          setExceptionReason(item.reason || '');
                        }}
                        style={styles.deleteBtn}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                        accessibilityLabel="Edit override"
                      >
                        <Ionicons name="create-outline" size={16} color={palette.primary} />
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        onPress={() => handleDeleteException(item.id)}
                        style={styles.deleteBtn}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                        accessibilityLabel="Delete override"
                      >
                        <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={16} color={palette.danger} />
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
              );
            })
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

const createStyles = (palette: ThemePalette, fonts: ReturnType<typeof useThemeMode>['fonts']) =>
  StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },
    scrollContent: { paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingTop: 12, paddingBottom: 115, gap: 12 },
    card: {
      backgroundColor: palette.surface,

    fieldRow: { marginBottom: 10 },
    fieldLabel: { fontSize: fonts.xs, fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold, color: palette.textSecondary, marginBottom: 5 },
    inputBox: {
      flexDirection: 'row',
      alignItems: 'center',
      backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle,
      borderRadius: 10,
      borderWidth: 1,
      borderColor: palette.borderStrong,
      paddingHorizontal: 12,
      height: 42,

    inputText: { flex: 1, fontSize: fonts.sm, color: palette.textPrimary },
    typeSelectorRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 },
    typePill: {
      flex: 1,
      alignItems: 'center',
      paddingVertical: 8,
      borderRadius: 8,
      backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle,
      borderWidth: 1,
      borderColor: palette.border,
    },
    typePillActive: { backgroundColor: palette.buttonBg, borderColor: palette.buttonBg },
    typePillText: { fontSize: fonts.xxs, fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold, color: palette.textSecondary },
    typePillTextActive: { color: palette.onPrimary },
    addOverrideBtn: {
      flexDirection: 'row',
      alignItems: 'center',
      justifyContent: 'center',
      backgroundColor: palette.buttonBg,
      paddingVertical: 11,
      borderRadius: 10,
      marginTop: 4,
    },
    addOverrideBtnText: { fontSize: fonts.sm, fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold, color: palette.onPrimary },
    btnDisabled: { opacity: 0.6 },
    noExceptionsText: { fontSize: fonts.xs, color: palette.textMuted, textAlign: 'center', paddingVertical: 14 },
    exceptionItemRow: {
      flexDirection: 'row',
      alignItems: 'center',
      paddingVertical: 10,
      borderBottomWidth: 1,
      borderBottomColor: palette.border,
    },
    typeBadge: { paddingVertical: 3, paddingHorizontal: 7, borderRadius: 6 },
    typeBadgeText: { fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold, fontSize: 9, letterSpacing: 0.3 },
    exceptionDateText: { fontSize: fonts.sm, fontWeight: fonts.weight.bold, color: palette.textPrimary },
    exceptionReasonText: { fontSize: fonts.xs, color: palette.textSecondary, marginTop: 1 },
    deleteBtn: { padding: 6 },
  });

export default CalendarScreen;

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Parent & Faculty Messages
 * File: mobile/src/screens/ChatScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  FlatList,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  RefreshControl,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { cleanText, getFriendlyErrorMessage, type ThemePalette } from '../constants/theme';

const THREADS_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_chat_threads';

interface ThreadItem {
  thread_key: string;
  student_id: number;
  student_name: string;
  section: string;
  faculty_id: number;
  faculty_name: string;
  parent_name: string;
  last_message: string;
  last_time: string;
  unread_count: number;
}

interface MessageItem {
  id: number;
  sender_name: string;
  is_mine: boolean;
  body: string;
  time_only: string;
}

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};

const showNotice = (title: string, message: string): void => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

const getInitials = (name: string): string => {
  const parts = String(name || '')
    .replace(/,/g, '')
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length >= 2) return `${parts[0][0]}${parts[1][0]}`.toUpperCase();
  return String(name || 'FA').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
};

export const ChatScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { palette, fonts } = useThemeMode();
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(palette, fonts), [palette, fonts]);
  const isParentUser =
    String(user?.role || user?.system_role || '')
      .trim()
      .toUpperCase() === 'PARENT';

  const [threads, setThreads] = useState<ThreadItem[]>([]);
  const [activeThread, setActiveThread] = useState<ThreadItem | null>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<MessageItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState<string>('');
  const [filterMode, setFilterMode] = useState<'ALL' | 'UNREAD'>('ALL');
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<string>('');
  const [sending, setSending] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [editingMsgId, setEditingMsgId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const flatListRef = useRef<FlatList<MessageItem> | null>(null);
  const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const threadsAbortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const messagesAbortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const scrollTimeoutRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      threadsAbortRef.current?.abort();
      messagesAbortRef.current?.abort();
      if (scrollTimeoutRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(scrollTimeoutRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const loadThreads = useCallback(async (silent = false) => {
    threadsAbortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    threadsAbortRef.current = controller;

    if (!silent) {
      try {
        const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(THREADS_CACHE_KEY);
        if (cached && isMountedRef.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(cached);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) setThreads(parsed);
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<{ threads?: ThreadItem[] }>('chat/threads/', {
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      if (!isMountedRef.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.threads) ? res.data.threads : [];
      setThreads(list);
      AsyncStorage.setItem(THREADS_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list)).catch(() => {});
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (isCanceledError(err)) return;
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, []);

  const loadMessages = useCallback(async (thread: ThreadItem, readCache = false) => {
    messagesAbortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    messagesAbortRef.current = controller;

    const msgCacheKey = `attendsure_cached_chat_msgs_${thread.student_id}_${thread.faculty_id}`;
    if (readCache) {
      try {
        const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(msgCacheKey);
        if (cached && isMountedRef.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(cached);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) setMessages(parsed);
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<{ messages?: MessageItem[] }>('chat/messages/', {
        params: { student_id: thread.student_id, faculty_id: thread.faculty_id },
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      if (!isMountedRef.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data?.messages) ? res.data.messages : [];
      setMessages(list);
      AsyncStorage.setItem(msgCacheKey, JSON.stringify(list)).catch(() => {});
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (isCanceledError(err)) return;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadThreads(false);
  }, [loadThreads]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeThread) {
      messagesAbortRef.current?.abort();
      setMessages([]);
      return;
    }
    void loadMessages(activeThread, true);
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      void loadMessages(activeThread, false);
    }, 8000);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      messagesAbortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [activeThread, loadMessages]);

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!activeThread || sending) return;
    const text = cleanText(draft, 1000);
    if (!text) return;

    setDraft('');
    setSending(true);
    try {
      if (editingMsgId) {
        const res = await apiClient.put<{ id: number; body: string }>('chat/messages/', {
          id: editingMsgId,
          body: text,
        });
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
        const updatedBody = res.data?.body || text;
        setMessages((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.id === editingMsgId ? { ...m, body: updatedBody } : m)));
        setEditingMsgId(null);
      } else {
        const res = await apiClient.post<MessageItem>('chat/messages/', {
          student_id: activeThread.student_id,
          faculty_id: activeThread.faculty_id,
          body: text,
        });
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
        setMessages((prev) => [...prev, res.data]);
        if (scrollTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(scrollTimeoutRef.current);
        scrollTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          if (isMountedRef.current) {
            flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true });
          }
        }, 100);
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      setDraft(text);
      showNotice(
        editingMsgId ? 'Update Failed' : 'Message Not Sent',
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, editingMsgId ? 'Could not update your message.' : 'Could not send your message. Please try again.')
      );
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setSending(false);
      }
    }
  };

  const executeDeleteMessage = async (id: number) => {
    try {
      await apiClient.delete('chat/messages/', { params: { id } });
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== id));
      if (editingMsgId === id) {
        setEditingMsgId(null);
        setDraft('');
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Delete Failed', getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not delete this message.'));
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteMessage = (id: number) => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      if (window.confirm('Delete this message?')) {
        void executeDeleteMessage(id);
      }
      return;
    }
    Alert.alert('Delete Message', 'Permanently delete this message?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Delete', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => void executeDeleteMessage(id) },
    ]);
  };

  const totalUnread = threads.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (t.unread_count || 0), 0);
  const cleanSearch = cleanText(search, 80).toLowerCase();

  const filteredThreads = threads.filter((t) => {
    if (filterMode === 'UNREAD' && t.unread_count <= 0) return false;
    if (!cleanSearch) return true;
    return (
      String(t.student_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(cleanSearch) ||
      String(t.faculty_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(cleanSearch) ||
      String(t.parent_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(cleanSearch) ||
      String(t.section || '').toLowerCase().includes(cleanSearch)
    );
  });

  if (!activeThread) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.container}>
        {/* Curved Header */}
        <View style={[styles.header, { paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 14) }]}>
          <View style={styles.headerTopRow}>
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Messages</Text>
              <Text style={styles.headerSub}>
                {totalUnread === 0 ? 'You are all caught up' : `${totalUnread} unread message(s)`}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.headerIconBtn}
              onPress={() => {
                setRefreshing(true);
                void loadThreads(true);
              }}
              accessibilityLabel="Refresh messages"
            >
              <Ionicons name="refresh-outline" size={20} color={palette.onPrimary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Search Input & Filter Row */}
        <View style={styles.controlsWrap}>
          <View style={styles.searchRow}>
            <View style={styles.searchBox}>
              <Ionicons name="search-outline" size={18} color={palette.textMuted} />
              <TextInput
                style={styles.searchInput}
                value={search}
                onChangeText={setSearch}
                placeholder="Search faculty or parents"
                placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                maxLength={80}
              />
              {search.length > 0 && (
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearch('')}>
                  <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={17} color={palette.textMuted} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.filterIconBtn}
              onPress={() => setFilterMode(filterMode === 'ALL' ? 'UNREAD' : 'ALL')}
              accessibilityLabel="Toggle unread filter"
            >
              <Ionicons name="options-outline" size={18} color={palette.primary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.pillsRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.filterPill,
                filterMode === 'ALL' ? styles.filterPillActive : styles.filterPillInactive,
              ]}
              onPress={() => setFilterMode('ALL')}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.filterPillText,
                  { color: filterMode === 'ALL' ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary },
                ]}
              >
                All
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.filterPill,
                filterMode === 'UNREAD' ? styles.filterPillActive : styles.filterPillInactive,
              ]}
              onPress={() => setFilterMode('UNREAD')}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.filterPillText,
                  { color: filterMode === 'UNREAD' ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary },
                ]}
              >
                Unread
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        {loading && threads.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.centerBox}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.primary} />
          </View>
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={filteredThreads}
            keyExtractor={(item) => item.thread_key}
            contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingBottom: 120 }}
            refreshControl={
              <RefreshControl
                refreshing={refreshing}
                onRefresh={() => {
                  setRefreshing(true);
                  void loadThreads(true);
                }}
                colors={[palette.primary]}
                tintColor={palette.primary}
              />
            }
            ListEmptyComponent={
              <View style={styles.centerBox}>
                <Ionicons name="chatbubbles-outline" size={44} color={palette.textMuted} />
                <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>No conversations found</Text>
                <Text style={styles.mutedText}>
                  Linked parent and faculty chat threads appear here automatically.
                </Text>
              </View>
            }
            renderItem={({ item }) => {
              const counterpartName = isParentUser
                ? item.faculty_name || item.student_name
                : item.parent_name || item.student_name || item.faculty_name;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.threadRow}
                  onPress={() => setActiveThread(item)}
                  activeOpacity={0.75}
                >
                  <View style={styles.initialsCircle}>
                    <Text style={styles.initialsText}>{getInitials(counterpartName)}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.threadTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {counterpartName}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={styles.threadSub} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {item.student_name} • {item.section}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  {item.unread_count > 0 && (
                    <View style={styles.unreadBadge}>
                      <Text style={styles.unreadText}>{item.unread_count}</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            }}
          />
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      style={styles.container}
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      keyboardVerticalOffset={80}
    >
      <View style={[styles.header, { paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 14) }]}>
        <View style={styles.headerTopRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => {
              setActiveThread(null);
              void loadThreads(true);
            }}
            style={styles.headerIconBtn}
            accessibilityLabel="Back to conversations"
          >
            <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={palette.onPrimary} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
            <Text style={styles.headerTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
              {activeThread.student_name}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.headerSub} numberOfLines={1}>
              {activeThread.section} • {activeThread.faculty_name}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      <FlatList
        ref={flatListRef}
        data={messages}
        keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
        contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, paddingBottom: 24, gap: 8 }}
        onContentSizeChange={() => flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: false })}
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.msgBubble,
              item.is_mine ? styles.msgBubbleMine : styles.msgBubbleOther,
            ]}
          >
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.senderMeta,
                  {
                    color: item.is_mine ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textMuted,
                    opacity: item.is_mine ? 0.85 : 1,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                {item.sender_name} • {item.time_only}
              </Text>
              {item.is_mine && (
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => {
                      setEditingMsgId(item.id);
                      setDraft(item.body);
                    }}
                    hitSlop={{ top: 6, bottom: 6, left: 6, right: 6 }}
                    accessibilityLabel="Edit message"
                  >
                    <Ionicons name="create-outline" size={13} color={palette.onPrimary} />
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => handleDeleteMessage(item.id)}
                    hitSlop={{ top: 6, bottom: 6, left: 6, right: 6 }}
                    accessibilityLabel="Delete message"
                  >
                    <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={13} color={palette.onPrimary} />
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              )}
            </View>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.msgText,
                { color: item.is_mine ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textPrimary },
              ]}
            >
              {item.body}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
      />

      <View
        style={[
          styles.inputRow,
          { marginBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 10) + 72 },
        ]}
      >
        {editingMsgId ? (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.sendBtn, { backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle }]}
            onPress={() => {
              setEditingMsgId(null);
              setDraft('');
            }}
            accessibilityLabel="Cancel editing message"
          >
            <Ionicons name="close" size={18} color={palette.textSecondary} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ) : null}
        <TextInput
          style={styles.inputBox}
          value={draft}
          onChangeText={setDraft}
          placeholder={editingMsgId ? 'Edit your message...' : 'Type a message...'}
          placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
          maxLength={1000}
          returnKeyType="send"
          onSubmitEditing={() => void handleSend()}
        />
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.sendBtn, (!draft.trim() || sending) && { opacity: 0.5 }]}
          onPress={() => void handleSend()}
          disabled={!draft.trim() || sending}
          accessibilityLabel="Send message"
        >
          {sending ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.onPrimary} />
          ) : (
            <Ionicons name="send" size={18} color={palette.onPrimary} />
          )}
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
};

function calculateDistanceMeters(
  lat1: number,
  lon1: number,
  lat2: number,
  lon2: number
): number {
  const R = 6371000.0;
  const dLat = ((lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI) / 180.0;
  const dLon = ((lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI) / 180.0;
  const radLat1 = (lat1 * Math.PI) / 180.0;
  const radLat2 = (lat2 * Math.PI) / 180.0;

  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +
    Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.cos(radLat1) * Math.cos(radLat2);
  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
  return Math.round(R * c);
}

function formatDistance(meters: number): string {
  if (meters >= 1000) {
    return `${(meters / 1000).toFixed(2)} km`;
  }
  return `${Math.round(meters)} m`;
}

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};
