================================================================================
/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Exit Permits & Official Travel Passes
 * File: mobile/src/screens/GatePassScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  RefreshControl,
  Modal,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ScrollView,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import QRCode from 'react-native-qrcode-svg';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import {
  cleanText,
  getFriendlyErrorMessage,
  type ThemePalette,
} from '../constants/theme';

const PASSES_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_exit_passes';

export interface GatePassItem {
  id: number;
  pass_number: string;
  bearer_type: 'STUDENT' | 'FACULTY';
  student?: number | null;
  student_name?: string;
  student_lrn?: string;
  faculty?: number | null;
  faculty_name?: string;
  faculty_emp_id?: string;
  pass_type: string;
  reason: string;
  valid_from: string;
  valid_to: string;
  status: 'PENDING' | 'ACTIVE' | 'USED' | 'DECLINED' | 'EXPIRED';
  attachment_url?: string | null;
  adviser_name?: string;
}

interface GatePassScreenProps {
  onBack?: () => void;
}

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};

const showNotice = (title: string, message: string): void => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

export const GatePassScreen: React.FC<GatePassScreenProps> = ({ onBack }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { palette, fonts } = useThemeMode();
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(palette, fonts), [palette, fonts]);

  const userRole = String(user?.role || user?.system_role || 'FACULTY').trim().toUpperCase();
  const isPrincipalOrAdmin = ['ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL', 'DEPT_HEAD'].includes(userRole);
  const canIssuePass = userRole !== 'PARENT';
  const canConfirmExit = [
    'ADMIN',
    'SUPERADMIN',
    'PRINCIPAL',
    'DEPT_HEAD',
    'FACULTY',
    'ADVISER',
    'STAFF',
    'EMPLOYEE',
  ].includes(userRole);

  const [passes, setPasses] = useState<GatePassItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>('ACTIVE');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  const [selectedPass, setSelectedPass] = useState<GatePassItem | null>(null);
  const [showIssueModal, setShowIssueModal] = useState(false);
  const [passTarget, setPassTarget] = useState<'STUDENT' | 'FACULTY'>('STUDENT');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [actionBusy, setActionBusy] = useState(false);

  const [reviewingPass, setReviewingPass] = useState<GatePassItem | null>(null);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    student_id: '',
    pass_type: 'Medical / Health Emergency',
    reason: '',
    duration_hours: '4',
  });

  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const fetchPasses = useCallback(async (silent = false) => {
    abortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortRef.current = controller;

    if (!silent) {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setLoading(true);
      try {
        const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(PASSES_CACHE_KEY);
        if (cached && isMountedRef.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(cached);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) setPasses(parsed);
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<{ results?: GatePassItem[] } | GatePassItem[]>(
        'gate-passes/',
        { signal: controller.signal }
      );
      if (!isMountedRef.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;
      const data = res.data;
      const list = Array.isArray(data)
        ? data
        : Array.isArray(data?.results)
        ? data.results
        : [];
      setPasses(list);
      AsyncStorage.setItem(PASSES_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list)).catch(() => {});
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current || isCanceledError(err)) return;
      if (!silent) {
        showNotice('Notice', getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not load exit passes right now.'));
      }
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    void fetchPasses(false);
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      abortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [fetchPasses]);

  const onRefresh = () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    void fetchPasses(true);
  };

  const filteredPasses = useMemo(() => {
    const q = cleanText(searchQuery, 80).toLowerCase();
    return passes.filter((p) => {
      const matchesStatus = statusFilter === 'ALL' || p.status === statusFilter;
      const passNum = (p.pass_number || '').toLowerCase();
      const holder = (p.student_name || p.faculty_name || '').toLowerCase();
      const reason = (p.reason || '').toLowerCase();
      const matchesSearch = !q || passNum.includes(q) || holder.includes(q) || reason.includes(q);
      return matchesStatus && matchesSearch;
    });
  }, [passes, statusFilter, searchQuery]);

  const handleIssueSubmit = async () => {
    let parsedStudentId: number | null = null;
    if (passTarget === 'STUDENT') {
      const cleanStudentId = cleanText(form.student_id, 12);
      if (!cleanStudentId) {
        showNotice('Required', 'Please enter the Student ID Number.');
        return;
      }
      parsedStudentId = Number.parseInt(cleanStudentId, 10);
      if (
        !Number.isFinite(parsedStudentId) ||
        parsedStudentId <= 0 ||
        String(parsedStudentId) !== cleanStudentId
      ) {
        showNotice('Invalid Student ID', 'Please enter a valid numeric Student ID.');
        return;
      }
    }

    const cleanedReason = cleanText(form.reason, 300);
    if (!cleanedReason) {
      showNotice('Required', 'Please enter a valid reason or destination.');
      return;
    }

    const parsedHours = Number.parseInt(cleanText(form.duration_hours, 2), 10);
    const hours = Math.max(1, Math.min(12, Number.isFinite(parsedHours) ? parsedHours : 4));
    const now = new Date();
    const expiry = new Date(now.getTime() + hours * 3600000);

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, string | number> = {
        bearer_type: passTarget,
        pass_type: cleanText(form.pass_type, 80),
        reason: cleanedReason,
        valid_from: now.toISOString(),
        valid_to: expiry.toISOString(),
      };

      if (passTarget === 'STUDENT' && parsedStudentId !== null) {
        payload.student = parsedStudentId;
      }

      await apiClient.post('gate-passes/', payload);
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;

      showNotice(
        'Saved',
        passTarget === 'FACULTY'
          ? 'Your travel pass request was sent to the Principal or School Admin for review.'
          : 'Student exit pass created and school office notified.'
      );

      setShowIssueModal(false);
      setForm({
        student_id: '',
        pass_type: 'Medical / Health Emergency',
        reason: '',
        duration_hours: '4',
      });
      void fetchPasses(true);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice(
        'Could Not Submit',
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Unable to submit the pass request. Please try again.')
      );
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleApprovePass = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    if (actionBusy) return;
    setActionBusy(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${encodeURIComponent(String(pass.id))}/approve/`);
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Approved', 'Travel pass approved and now active.');
      setReviewingPass(null);
      void fetchPasses(true);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Error', getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not approve this pass.'));
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setActionBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const executeDeclinePass = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    if (actionBusy) return;
    setActionBusy(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${encodeURIComponent(String(pass.id))}/decline/`, {
        reason: 'Declined after document review.',
      });
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Declined', 'Pass request has been marked as declined.');
      setReviewingPass(null);
      void fetchPasses(true);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Error', getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not decline this pass.'));
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setActionBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeclinePass = (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      if (window.confirm('Decline this travel pass request?')) {
        void executeDeclinePass(pass);
      }
      return;
    }
    Alert.alert('Decline Pass', 'Decline this travel pass request?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      {
        text: 'Confirm Decline',
        style: 'destructive',
        onPress: () => void executeDeclinePass(pass),
      },
    ]);
  };

  const handleConfirmExit = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    if (actionBusy) return;
    setActionBusy(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${encodeURIComponent(String(pass.id))}/mark-used/`);
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Pass Used', 'Departure recorded and pass marked as used.');
      setSelectedPass(null);
      void fetchPasses(true);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Error', getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not update pass status.'));
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setActionBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const executeDeletePass = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    if (actionBusy) return;
    setActionBusy(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.delete(`gate-passes/${encodeURIComponent(String(pass.id))}/`);
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      setPasses((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== pass.id));
      setSelectedPass(null);
      showNotice('Deleted', `Pass #${pass.pass_number} has been removed.`);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice('Delete Failed', getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not remove this pass.'));
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setActionBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeletePass = (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      if (window.confirm(`Delete pass #${pass.pass_number}?`)) {
        void executeDeletePass(pass);
      }
      return;
    }
    Alert.alert('Delete Pass', `Permanently delete pass #${pass.pass_number}?`, [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Delete', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => void executeDeletePass(pass) },
    ]);
  };

  const getStatusStyle = (st: GatePassItem['status']) => {
    if (st === 'ACTIVE') return { bg: palette.successLight, text: palette.success };
    if (st === 'PENDING') return { bg: palette.warningLight, text: palette.warning };
    if (st === 'DECLINED') return { bg: palette.dangerLight, text: palette.danger };
    return { bg: palette.surfaceSubtle, text: palette.textMuted };
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      {/* Header */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
          {onBack && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.backBtn}
              onPress={onBack}
              accessibilityLabel="Go back"
            >
              <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color={palette.primary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
          <View>
            <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Exit &amp; Travel Passes</Text>
            <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>Student Permits &amp; Official Travel</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {canIssuePass && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.newBtn}
            onPress={() => {
              setPassTarget('STUDENT');
              setShowIssueModal(true);
            }}
          >
            <Ionicons name="add" size={16} color={palette.onPrimary} />
            <Text style={styles.newBtnText}>New Pass</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Search Input */}
      <View style={styles.searchBox}>
        <Ionicons name="search" size={16} color={palette.textMuted} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Search pass number, name, or reason..."
          placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
          maxLength={80}
        />
        {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearchQuery('')}>
            <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={16} color={palette.textMuted} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Filter Row */}
      <View style={styles.filterRow}>
        <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 6 }}>
          {['ACTIVE', 'PENDING', 'ALL', 'USED', 'DECLINED'].map((st) => {
            const active = statusFilter === st;
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={st}
                style={[styles.filterChip, active && styles.filterChipActive]}
                onPress={() => setStatusFilter(st)}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.filterChipText, active && styles.filterChipTextActive]}>
                  {st === 'PENDING' ? 'Pending Review' : st}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>

      {/* Passes List */}
      {loading && passes.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.centerBox}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.primary} />
          <Text style={styles.centerText}>Loading passes...</Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          data={filteredPasses}
          keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
          contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl
              refreshing={refreshing}
              onRefresh={onRefresh}
              colors={[palette.primary]}
              tintColor={palette.primary}
            />
          }
          ListEmptyComponent={
            <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
              <Ionicons name="document-text-outline" size={38} color={palette.textMuted} />
              <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>No passes found</Text>
              <Text style={styles.emptySub}>
                Approved and pending student exit or faculty travel passes appear here.
              </Text>
            </View>
          }
          renderItem={({ item }) => {
            const holderName =
              item.bearer_type === 'STUDENT' ? item.student_name : item.faculty_name;
            const isPending = item.status === 'PENDING';
            const pillColors = getStatusStyle(item.status);

            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.card}
                activeOpacity={0.75}
                onPress={() =>
                  isPending && isPrincipalOrAdmin ? setReviewingPass(item) : setSelectedPass(item)
                }
              >
                <View style={styles.cardTop}>
                  <View>
                    <Text style={styles.passCode}>{item.pass_number}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.holderType}>{item.bearer_type} PASS</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.statusPill, { backgroundColor: pillColors.bg }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.statusPillText, { color: pillColors.text }]}>
                      {item.status}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                <Text style={styles.holderName}>{holderName || 'Pass Holder'}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.reasonText} numberOfLines={2}>
                  {item.reason}
                </Text>

                <View style={styles.cardBottom}>
                  <Text style={styles.dateText}>
                    Valid until:{' '}
                    {item.valid_to && !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(item.valid_to))
                      ? new Date(item.valid_to).toLocaleTimeString([], {
                          hour: '2-digit',
                          minute: '2-digit',
                        })
                      : '—'}
                  </Text>
                  {isPending && isPrincipalOrAdmin ? (
                    <Text style={[styles.cardAction, { color: palette.warning }]}>
                      Review Request &gt;
                    </Text>
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={styles.cardAction}>View Pass &gt;</Text>
                  )}
                </View>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* MODAL 1: Create Pass */}
      <Modal
        visible={showIssueModal}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setShowIssueModal(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Create Exit or Travel Pass</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setShowIssueModal(false)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color={palette.textPrimary} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
              <View style={styles.switchRow}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.switchBtn, passTarget === 'STUDENT' && styles.switchBtnActive]}
                  onPress={() => setPassTarget('STUDENT')}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.switchText, passTarget === 'STUDENT' && styles.switchTextActive]}
                  >
                    Student Exit Pass
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.switchBtn, passTarget === 'FACULTY' && styles.switchBtnActive]}
                  onPress={() => setPassTarget('FACULTY')}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.switchText, passTarget === 'FACULTY' && styles.switchTextActive]}
                  >
                    Faculty Travel Pass
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              {passTarget === 'STUDENT' ? (
                <>
                  <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Student ID Number *</Text>
                  <TextInput
                    style={styles.inputField}
                    placeholder="Enter Student ID"
                    placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                    keyboardType="numeric"
                    maxLength={12}
                    value={form.student_id}
                    onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, student_id: val })}
                  />
                </>
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.noticeText}>
                  Your travel pass request will be sent to the Principal or School Admin for review.
                </Text>
              )}

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Reason &amp; Destination *</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={[styles.inputField, { height: 72, textAlignVertical: 'top', paddingTop: 8 }]}
                placeholder="State destination and purpose..."
                placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                multiline
                maxLength={300}
                value={form.reason}
                onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, reason: val })}
              />

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Supporting Proof (Optional)</Text>
              <View style={styles.proofBox}>
                <Ionicons name="document-attach-outline" size={18} color={palette.primary} />
                <Text style={styles.proofText}>
                  Supporting files can be attached from the school web portal if needed.
                </Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Allowed Duration (Hours, 1 to 12)</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.inputField}
                placeholder="4"
                placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                keyboardType="numeric"
                maxLength={2}
                value={form.duration_hours}
                onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, duration_hours: val })}
              />

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.submitBtn, submitting && { opacity: 0.65 }]}
                onPress={() => void handleIssueSubmit()}
                disabled={submitting}
              >
                {submitting ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.onPrimary} />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={styles.submitBtnText}>Submit Pass Request</Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* MODAL 2: Principal / Admin Review */}
      <Modal
        visible={Boolean(reviewingPass)}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setReviewingPass(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Review Travel Pass</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setReviewingPass(null)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color={palette.textPrimary} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {reviewingPass && (
              <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                <Text style={styles.detailTitle}>
                  Applicant: {reviewingPass.faculty_name || reviewingPass.student_name || 'Applicant'}
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.detailDesc}>Reason: {reviewingPass.reason}</Text>

                <View style={styles.docBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.docBoxLabel}>SUPPORTING DOCUMENT</Text>
                  {reviewingPass.attachment_url ? (
                    <Text style={styles.docBoxStatus}>Proof document attached on record.</Text>
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={[styles.docBoxStatus, { color: palette.danger }]}>
                      No attachment provided.
                    </Text>
                  )}
                </View>

                <View style={styles.btnRow}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.actionBtn, styles.declineBtn, actionBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
                    onPress={() => handleDeclinePass(reviewingPass)}
                    disabled={actionBusy}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.declineBtnText}>Decline</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>

                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.actionBtn, styles.approveBtn, actionBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
                    onPress={() => void handleApprovePass(reviewingPass)}
                    disabled={actionBusy}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.approveBtnText}>Approve Pass</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              </ScrollView>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* MODAL 3: Pass QR Slip */}
      <Modal
        visible={Boolean(selectedPass)}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setSelectedPass(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Pass Slip</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSelectedPass(null)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color={palette.textPrimary} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {selectedPass && (
              <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                <View style={styles.qrArea}>
                  <View style={styles.qrQuietZone}>
                    <QRCode
                      value={`ATTENDSURE-PASS:${cleanText(selectedPass.pass_number, 64)}`}
                      size={180}
                      color="#003460"
                      backgroundColor="#ffffff"
                    />
                  </View>
                  <Text style={styles.qrHint}>
                    Show this pass code to your adviser or school office before leaving campus.
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Pass Number:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.pass_number}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Pass Holder:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>
                    {selectedPass.bearer_type === 'STUDENT'
                      ? selectedPass.student_name
                      : selectedPass.faculty_name}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Issued By:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.adviser_name || 'Class Adviser'}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Reason:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.reason}</Text>
                </View>

                {canConfirmExit && selectedPass.status === 'ACTIVE' && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.confirmExitBtn, actionBusy && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
                    onPress={() => void handleConfirmExit(selectedPass)}
                    disabled={actionBusy}
                  >
                    <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle" size={16} color={palette.onPrimary} />
                    <Text style={styles.confirmExitBtnText}>Mark Pass as Used</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}

                {canIssuePass && (isPrincipalOrAdmin || selectedPass.status !== 'USED') && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[
                      styles.confirmExitBtn,
                      { backgroundColor: palette.danger, marginTop: 8 },
                      actionBusy && { opacity: 0.6 },
                    ]}
                    onPress={() => handleDeletePass(selectedPass)}
                    disabled={actionBusy}
                  >
                    <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={16} color={palette.onPrimary} />
                    <Text style={styles.confirmExitBtnText}>Delete Pass</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </ScrollView>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const createStyles = (palette: ThemePalette, fonts: ReturnType<typeof useThemeMode>['fonts']) =>

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Sign-In Screen
 * File: mobile/src/screens/LoginScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  ScrollView,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { AttendSureLogo, TechBlazerLogo } from '../branding/BrandLogos';
import { ServerConfigModal } from '../components/ServerConfigModal';
import { isSafeUrl, cleanText, getFriendlyErrorMessage } from '../constants/theme';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { resolveFastestServer, invalidateServerCache } from '../services/configService';
import { registerPushTokenWithBackend } from '../services/pushService';
import { startPassivePresenceTracking } from '../services/backgroundLocation';

const SCHOOL_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_school_profile';
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5;
const LOCKOUT_MS = 30000;

interface PublicSchoolBranding {
  school_name?: string;
  school_id?: string;
  division?: string;
  school_logo?: string | null;
  left_logo?: string | null;
}

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};

export const LoginScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const { palette, fonts, ui, isDark, toggleDarkMode } = useThemeMode();

  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [school, setSchool] = useState<PublicSchoolBranding | null>(null);
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'ONLINE' | 'OFFLINE' | 'CHECKING'>('CHECKING');
  const [logoError, setLogoError] = useState(false);
  const [showServerModal, setShowServerModal] = useState(false);

  const passwordRef = useRef<React.ComponentRef<typeof TextInput> | null>(null);
  const attemptsRef = useRef(0);
  const lockedUntilRef = useRef(0);
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const probeAbortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const notify = (title: string, msg: string) => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.alert(`${title}: ${msg}`);
    } else {
      Alert.alert(title, msg);
    }
  };

  const checkServer = useCallback(async (forceReprobe = false) => {
    probeAbortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    probeAbortRef.current = controller;

    if (isMountedRef.current) setStatus('CHECKING');
    if (forceReprobe) invalidateServerCache();

    let timeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

    try {
      const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(SCHOOL_CACHE_KEY);
      if (cached && isMountedRef.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
        setSchool(JSON.parse(cached));
      }

      const activeBase = await resolveFastestServer();
      if (!isMountedRef.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;
      setServerBase(activeBase);
      setLogoError(false);

      timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3500);
      const ping = await fetch(`${activeBase}/api/health/db/`, {
        method: 'GET',
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      if (!isMountedRef.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;

      if (ping.status >= 200 && ping.status < 400) {
        setStatus('ONLINE');
        try {
          const res = await apiClient.get<PublicSchoolBranding>('school/public-branding/', {
            timeout: 3000,
            signal: controller.signal,
          });
          if (isMountedRef.current && !controller.signal.aborted && res.data) {
            setSchool(res.data);
            setLogoError(false);
            AsyncStorage.setItem(SCHOOL_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.data)).catch(() => {});
          }
        } catch {
          // Keep cached school branding if branding endpoint is temporarily unavailable
        }
      } else {
        setStatus('OFFLINE');
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      if (isCanceledError(err) && probeAbortRef.current !== controller) return;
      setStatus('OFFLINE');
    } finally {
      if (timeoutId !== null) {
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      }
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    void checkServer(false);
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      probeAbortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [checkServer]);

  const handleSignIn = async () => {
    if (Date.now() < lockedUntilRef.current) {
      const waitSec = Math.ceil((lockedUntilRef.current - Date.now()) / 1000);
      return notify('Please Wait', `Too many unsuccessful tries. Wait ${waitSec} seconds before trying again.`);
    }

    const userClean = cleanText(username, 150);
    const passClean = password.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '').trim();

    if (!userClean || !passClean) {
      return notify('Missing Information', 'Please enter your username and password.');
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await login(userClean, passClean);
      attemptsRef.current = 0;
      startPassivePresenceTracking().catch(() => {});
      registerPushTokenWithBackend().catch(() => {});
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      attemptsRef.current += 1;
      if (attemptsRef.current >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
        lockedUntilRef.current = Date.now() + LOCKOUT_MS;
      }
      const anyErr = err as { response?: unknown };
      const msg = !anyErr?.response
        ? 'Cannot reach the school server. Tap the settings icon at the top right to check the server address.'
        : getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Incorrect username or password. Please try again.');
      notify('Sign In Failed', msg);
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
  };

  const rawLogo = school?.school_logo || school?.left_logo || null;
  const candidateLogoUrl = rawLogo
    ? rawLogo.startsWith('http://') || rawLogo.startsWith('https://') || rawLogo.startsWith('data:')
      ? rawLogo
      : serverBase
      ? `${serverBase.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${rawLogo.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`
      : null
    : null;
  const safeLogoUrl = isSafeUrl(candidateLogoUrl) ? candidateLogoUrl : null;

  const schoolMetaLine = [
    school?.school_id ? `School ID: ${cleanText(school.school_id, 40)}` : '',
    school?.division ? cleanText(school.division, 80) : '',
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' • ');

  const statusColor =
    status === 'ONLINE' ? palette.success : status === 'OFFLINE' ? palette.danger : palette.warning;
  const statusBg =
    status === 'ONLINE'
      ? palette.successLight
      : status === 'OFFLINE'
      ? palette.dangerLight
      : palette.warningLight;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={ui.screen}>
      <StatusBar style={isDark ? 'light' : 'dark'} />
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        style={{ flex: 1 }}
        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      >
        <ScrollView
          contentContainerStyle={{ flexGrow: 1, padding: 20, justifyContent: 'space-between' }}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        >
          {/* Top Action Bar */}
          <View style={[ui.rowBetween, { marginBottom: 12 }]}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={toggleDarkMode}
              style={{
                width: 40,
                height: 40,
                borderRadius: 20,
                backgroundColor: palette.surface,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: palette.border,
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
              }}
              accessibilityLabel="Switch color mode"
            >
              <Ionicons
                name={isDark ? 'sunny-outline' : 'moon-outline'}
                size={18}
                color={palette.primary}
              />
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => void checkServer(true)}
              style={[
                ui.badge,
                ui.rowCenter,
                {
                  backgroundColor: statusBg,
                  paddingHorizontal: 12,
                  paddingVertical: 6,
                  borderRadius: 20,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View
                style={{ width: 7, height: 7, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: statusColor }}
              />
              <Text
                style={{
                  fontSize: fonts.xxs,
                  fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                  color: statusColor,
                }}
              >
                {status === 'ONLINE'
                  ? 'SERVER ONLINE'
                  : status === 'OFFLINE'
                  ? 'OFFLINE • TAP TO RETRY'
                  : 'CONNECTING...'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setShowServerModal(true)}
              style={{
                width: 40,
                height: 40,
                borderRadius: 20,
                backgroundColor: palette.surface,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: palette.border,
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
              }}
              accessibilityLabel="Server settings"
            >
              <Ionicons name="settings-outline" size={18} color={palette.primary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Center Brand + Sign-In Card */}
          <View style={{ marginVertical: 8 }}>
            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 20 }}>
              <View
                style={{
                  width: 92,
                  height: 92,
                  borderRadius: 46,
                  backgroundColor: palette.surface,
                  borderWidth: 2,
                  borderColor: palette.primary,
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  marginBottom: 12,
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  ...Platform.select({
                    ios: {
                      shadowColor: palette.primary,
                      shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },
                      shadowOpacity: 0.15,
                      shadowRadius: 8,
                    },
                    android: { elevation: 4 },
                  }),
                }}
              >
                {safeLogoUrl && !logoError ? (
                  <Image
                    source={{ uri: safeLogoUrl }}
                    style={{ width: 64, height: 64 }}
                    contentFit="contain"
                    onError={() => setLogoError(true)}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <AttendSureLogo size={58} />
                )}
              </View>
              <Text
                style={[
                  ui.title,
                  { textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 18, color: palette.textPrimary },
                ]}
              >
                {school?.school_name ? cleanText(school.school_name, 120) : 'AttendSure'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[ui.subtext, { marginTop: 3, color: palette.textSecondary }]}>
                {schoolMetaLine || 'School Attendance System'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            {/* Sign-In Card */}
            <View style={[ui.card, { padding: 18, backgroundColor: palette.surface }]}>
              <Text
                style={[
                  ui.heading,
                  { fontSize: 16, marginBottom: 10, color: palette.textPrimary },
                ]}
              >
                Sign In
              </Text>

              <View
                style={[
                  ui.box,
                  ui.rowCenter,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: palette.primarySoft,
                    marginBottom: 16,
                    borderColor: 'transparent',
                    padding: 10,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark-outline" size={18} color={palette.primary} />
                <Text
                  style={{
                    flex: 1,
                    fontSize: fonts.xs,
                    color: palette.textPrimary,
                    lineHeight: 16,
                    fontWeight: fonts.weight.medium,
                  }}
                >
                  Accounts are issued by the School Admin. Contact your school office if you need
                  help signing in.
                </Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={ui.label}>Username or Employee ID</Text>
              <View
                style={[
                  ui.input,
                  ui.rowCenter,
                  {
                    marginBottom: 14,
                    height: 48,
                    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle,
                    borderColor: palette.borderStrong,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Ionicons
                  name="person-outline"
                  size={18}
                  color={palette.textMuted}
                  style={{ marginRight: 8 }}
                />
                <TextInput
                  style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: fonts.md, color: palette.textPrimary }}
                  placeholder="Enter your username"
                  placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                  value={username}
                  onChangeText={setUsername}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  autoCorrect={false}
                  maxLength={150}
                  returnKeyType="next"
                  onSubmitEditing={() => passwordRef.current?.focus()}
                />
              </View>

              <Text style={ui.label}>Password</Text>
              <View
                style={[
                  ui.input,
                  ui.rowCenter,
                  {
                    marginBottom: 18,
                    height: 48,
                    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle,
                    borderColor: palette.borderStrong,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Ionicons
                  name="lock-closed-outline"
                  size={18}
                  color={palette.textMuted}
                  style={{ marginRight: 8 }}
                />
                <TextInput
                  ref={passwordRef}
                  style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: fonts.md, color: palette.textPrimary }}
                  placeholder="Enter your password"
                  placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                  value={password}
                  onChangeText={setPassword}
                  secureTextEntry={!showPassword}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  maxLength={128}
                  returnKeyType="go"
                  onSubmitEditing={() => void handleSignIn()}
                />
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => setShowPassword((prev) => !prev)}
                  hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
                  accessibilityLabel={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                >
                  <Ionicons
                    name={showPassword ? 'eye-off-outline' : 'eye-outline'}
                    size={19}
                    color={palette.textMuted}
                  />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  ui.buttonPrimary,
                  {
                    height: 48,
                    borderRadius: 14,
                    backgroundColor: palette.buttonBg,
                  },
                  loading && { opacity: 0.65 },
                ]}
                onPress={() => void handleSignIn()}
                disabled={loading}
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.onPrimary} />
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Ionicons name="log-in-outline" size={19} color={palette.onPrimary} />
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        ui.buttonPrimaryText,
                        { fontSize: 15, color: palette.onPrimary },
                      ]}
                    >
                      Sign In
                    </Text>
                  </>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Footer Signature */}
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: 10, gap: 4 }}>
            <View style={[ui.rowCenter, { justifyContent: 'center' }]}>
              <Text style={[ui.subtext, { color: palette.textMuted }]}>System by</Text>
              <TechBlazerLogo />
            </View>
            {serverBase ? (
              <Text
                style={{ fontSize: 10, color: palette.textMuted, fontFamily: fonts.mono }}
                numberOfLines={1}
              >
                {serverBase}
              </Text>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        </ScrollView>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>

      <ServerConfigModal
        visible={showServerModal}
        onClose={() => {
          setShowServerModal(false);
          void checkServer(true);
        }}
      />
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

export default LoginScreen;

/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Profile, Color Mode Switcher & School Tools
 * File: mobile/src/screens/MenuScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { isSafeUrl, type ThemeMode } from '../constants/theme';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { getServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';
import { TabType } from '../navigation/MainTabNavigator';

const STATS_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_menu_stats';

interface MenuStats {
  rate: number | null;
  scans: number;
  students: number;
  alerts: number;
  classes: number;
}

interface MenuScreenProps {
  onSelectScreen: (screen: TabType) => void;
}

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};

export const MenuScreen: React.FC<MenuScreenProps> = ({ onSelectScreen }) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user, logout, refreshUser } = useAuth();
  const { mode, setThemeMode, palette, ui, fonts } = useThemeMode();

  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'INFO' | 'TOOLS' | 'ACCOUNT'>('INFO');
  const [photoError, setPhotoError] = useState(false);
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState('');
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<MenuStats>({
    rate: null,
    scans: 0,
    students: 0,
    alerts: 0,
    classes: 0,
  });

  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const loadStats = useCallback(async (fromCacheFirst = false) => {
    abortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortRef.current = controller;

    if (fromCacheFirst) {
      try {
        const [cachedStats, base] = await Promise.all([
          AsyncStorage.getItem(STATS_CACHE_KEY),
          getServerBaseUrl(),
        ]);
        if (isMountedRef.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
          if (base) setServerBase(base);
          if (cachedStats) {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(cachedStats);
            if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
              setStats({
                rate: typeof parsed.rate === 'number' ? parsed.rate : null,
                scans: Number(parsed.scans ?? 0),
                students: Number(parsed.students ?? 0),
                alerts: Number(parsed.alerts ?? 0),
                classes: Number(parsed.classes ?? 0),
              });
            }
          }
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    try {
      const [ov, cls] = await Promise.allSettled([
        apiClient.get('dashboard/overview/', { signal: controller.signal }),
        apiClient
          .get('classroom/assigned/', { signal: controller.signal })
          .catch((err: unknown) => {
            if (isCanceledError(err) || controller.signal.aborted) throw err;
            return apiClient.get('classroom/class_list/', { signal: controller.signal });
          }),
      ]);

      if (!isMountedRef.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;

      const d = ov.status === 'fulfilled' && ov.value?.data ? ov.value.data : null;
      const clsRaw = cls.status === 'fulfilled' ? cls.value?.data : null;
      const clsList = Array.isArray(clsRaw)
        ? clsRaw
        : Array.isArray(clsRaw?.results)
        ? clsRaw.results
        : null;

      setStats((prev) => {
        const rawRate = d?.attendance_rate;
        const parsedRate =
          rawRate !== undefined && rawRate !== null && Number.isFinite(Number(rawRate))
            ? Number(rawRate)
            : prev.rate;

        const next: MenuStats = {
          rate: parsedRate,
          scans: d ? Number(d.classroom_scans_today ?? d.scans_today ?? 0) : prev.scans,
          students: d ? Number(d.total_students ?? 0) : prev.students,
          alerts: d ? Number(d.push_alerts_today ?? 0) : prev.alerts,
          classes: clsList !== null ? clsList.length : prev.classes,
        };
        AsyncStorage.setItem(STATS_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next)).catch(() => {});
        return next;
      });
    } catch {
      // Keep cached values when offline
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    void loadStats(true);
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      abortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [loadStats]);

  const role = String(user?.role || user?.system_role || '—').toUpperCase();
  const name =
    user?.full_name ||
    `${user?.first_name || ''} ${user?.last_name || ''}`.trim() ||
    user?.username ||
    '—';
  const empId = user?.employee_id || user?.faculty_id || user?.username || '—';
  const dept = user?.department || '—';
  const email = user?.email || '—';

  const rawPhoto = user?.photo || user?.avatar || null;
  const candidatePhoto =
    typeof rawPhoto === 'string' && rawPhoto.trim()
      ? rawPhoto.startsWith('http://') ||
        rawPhoto.startsWith('https://') ||
        rawPhoto.startsWith('data:')
        ? rawPhoto.trim()
        : serverBase
        ? `${serverBase.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${rawPhoto.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`
        : ''
      : '';
  const resolvedPhoto = isSafeUrl(candidatePhoto) ? candidatePhoto : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    setPhotoError(false);
  }, [resolvedPhoto]);

  const themes: { key: ThemeMode; label: string; icon: keyof typeof Ionicons.glyphMap }[] = [
    { key: 'light', label: 'Light', icon: 'sunny-outline' },
    { key: 'dark', label: 'Dark', icon: 'moon-outline' },
    { key: 'high-contrast', label: 'Daylight', icon: 'contrast-outline' },
  ];

  const tools: {
    key: TabType;
    title: string;
    sub: string;
    icon: keyof typeof Ionicons.glyphMap;
  }[] = [
    {
      key: 'scanner',
      title: 'Face & Location Check-In',
      sub: 'Verify your school attendance',
      icon: 'scan-outline',
    },
    {
      key: 'dtr',
      title: 'Daily Time Record (Form 48)',
      sub: 'View and submit monthly hours',
      icon: 'time-outline',
    },
    {
      key: 'reports',
      title: 'School Forms (SF1, SF2, SF4)',
      sub: 'View official attendance forms',
      icon: 'document-text-outline',
    },
    {
      key: 'calendar',
      title: 'School Calendar',
      sub: 'Holidays and class suspensions',
      icon: 'calendar-outline',
    },
    {
      key: 'settings',
      title: 'Settings',
      sub: 'Password, profile and server address',
      icon: 'settings-outline',
    },
  ];

  const onRefresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await Promise.allSettled([loadStats(false), refreshUser()]);
    if (isMountedRef.current) setRefreshing(false);
  };

  return (
    <ScrollView
      style={ui.screen}
      contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 120 }}
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      refreshControl={
        <RefreshControl
          refreshing={refreshing}
          onRefresh={() => void onRefresh()}
          colors={[palette.primary]}
          tintColor={palette.primary}
        />
      }
    >
      {/* Top Profile Banner */}
      <View
        style={{
          backgroundColor: palette.headerBg,
          paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 14),
          paddingHorizontal: 18,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          borderBottomLeftRadius: 26,
          borderBottomRightRadius: 26,
        }}
      >
        <View style={ui.rowBetween}>
          <Text
            style={{
              fontSize: fonts.xl,
              fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
              color: palette.onPrimary,
            }}
          >
            My Profile
          </Text>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => onSelectScreen('settings')}
            style={{
              padding: 8,
              borderRadius: 18,
              backgroundColor: palette.headerSurface,
            }}
            accessibilityLabel="Open settings"
          >
            <Ionicons name="settings-outline" size={18} color={palette.onPrimary} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: 10 }}>
          <View
            style={{
              width: 72,
              height: 72,
              borderRadius: 36,
              backgroundColor: palette.surface,
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              overflow: 'hidden',
              marginBottom: 8,
            }}
          >
            {resolvedPhoto && !photoError ? (
              <Image
                source={{ uri: resolvedPhoto }}
                style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }}
                contentFit="cover"
                onError={() => setPhotoError(true)}
              />
            ) : (
              <Ionicons name="person" size={36} color={palette.primary} />
            )}
          </View>
          <Text
            style={{
              fontSize: 18,
              fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
              color: palette.onPrimary,
            }}
          >
            {name}
          </Text>
          <Text
            style={{
              fontSize: fonts.xs,
              color: palette.onPrimary,
              opacity: 0.85,
              marginTop: 2,
            }}
          >
            {dept !== '—' ? `${dept} • ` : ''}ID {empId}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View
          style={{
            flexDirection: 'row',
            backgroundColor: palette.headerSurface,
            borderRadius: 14,
            paddingVertical: 10,
            marginTop: 14,
          }}
        >
          {[
            { val: String(stats.scans), lbl: 'Scans Today' },
            { val: stats.rate !== null ? `${stats.rate}%` : '—', lbl: 'Attendance' },
            { val: String(stats.classes), lbl: 'Classes' },
          ].map((s, idx) => (
            <View key={idx} style={{ flex: 1, alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <Text
                style={{
                  fontSize: 16,
                  fontWeight: fonts.weight.black,
                  color: palette.onPrimary,
                }}
              >
                {s.val}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={{
                  fontSize: fonts.xxs,
                  color: palette.onPrimary,
                  opacity: 0.85,
                }}
              >
                {s.lbl}
              </Text>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={{ padding: 16, gap: 12 }}>
        {/* Color Mode Switcher */}
        <View style={ui.card}>
          <Text style={ui.label}>SCREEN COLOR MODE</Text>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginTop: 4 }}>
            {themes.map((t) => {
              const active = mode === t.key;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={t.key}
                  onPress={() => setThemeMode(t.key)}
                  style={[
                    ui.buttonSecondary,
                    {
                      flex: 1,
                      backgroundColor: active ? palette.buttonBg : palette.surfaceSubtle,
                      borderColor: active ? palette.buttonBg : palette.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Ionicons
                    name={t.icon}
                    size={15}
                    color={active ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary}
                  />
                  <Text
                    style={{
                      fontSize: fonts.xs,
                      fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                      color: active ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary,
                    }}
                  >
                    {t.label}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Section Tabs */}
        <View style={[ui.box, { flexDirection: 'row', padding: 4 }]}>
          {(['INFO', 'TOOLS', 'ACCOUNT'] as const).map((k) => {
            const active = tab === k;
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={k}
                onPress={() => setTab(k)}
                style={{
                  flex: 1,
                  paddingVertical: 8,
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  borderRadius: 8,
                  backgroundColor: active ? palette.surface : 'transparent',
                }}
              >
                <Text
                  style={{
                    fontSize: fonts.xs,
                    fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                    color: active ? palette.primary : palette.textMuted,
                  }}
                >
                  {k}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        {tab === 'INFO' && (
          <View style={ui.card}>
            <Text style={ui.heading}>Faculty Details</Text>
            {[
              { k: 'NAME', v: name },
              { k: 'ID NUMBER', v: empId },
              { k: 'DEPARTMENT', v: dept },
              { k: 'EMAIL', v: email },
              { k: 'ROLE', v: role },
              { k: 'TOTAL STUDENTS', v: String(stats.students) },
              { k: 'PARENT ALERTS TODAY', v: String(stats.alerts) },
            ].map((r, i) => (
              <View
                key={i}
                style={[
                  ui.rowBetween,
                  {
                    paddingVertical: 8,
                    borderBottomWidth: i === 6 ? 0 : 1,
                    borderBottomColor: palette.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={ui.label}>{r.k}</Text>
                <Text
                  style={{
                    fontSize: fonts.sm,
                    fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                    color: palette.textPrimary,
                    maxWidth: '62%',
                    textAlign: 'right',
                  }}
                  numberOfLines={1}
                >
                  {r.v}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        )}

        {tab === 'TOOLS' && (
          <View style={{ gap: 8 }}>
            {tools.map((t) => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={t.key}
                style={[ui.card, ui.rowBetween, { marginBottom: 0 }]}
                onPress={() => onSelectScreen(t.key)}
              >
                <View style={[ui.rowCenter, { flex: 1 }]}>
                  <View
                    style={{
                      width: 38,
                      height: 38,
                      borderRadius: 10,
                      backgroundColor: palette.primarySoft,
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                    }}
                  >
                    <Ionicons name={t.icon} size={18} color={palette.primary} />
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        fontSize: fonts.md,
                        fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold,
                        color: palette.textPrimary,
                      }}
                    >
                      {t.title}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={ui.subtext}>{t.sub}</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
                <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color={palette.textMuted} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>
        )}

        {tab === 'ACCOUNT' && (
          <View style={ui.card}>
            <Text style={ui.heading}>Account Options</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[ui.buttonSecondary, { marginBottom: 10 }]}
              onPress={() => onSelectScreen('settings')}
            >
              <Ionicons name="key-outline" size={16} color={palette.textPrimary} />
              <Text style={ui.buttonSecondaryText}>Change Password or Server</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[ui.buttonPrimary, { backgroundColor: palette.dangerBg }]}
              onPress={() => void logout()}
            >
              <Ionicons name="log-out-outline" size={16} color={palette.onPrimary} />
              <Text style={[ui.buttonPrimaryText, { color: palette.onPrimary }]}>Sign Out</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
