/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Account, Theme & Server Settings
 * File: mobile/src/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import QRCode from 'react-native-qrcode-svg';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { getServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';
import { ServerConfigModal } from '../components/ServerConfigModal';
import {
  isSafeUrl,
  cleanText,
  getFriendlyErrorMessage,
  type ThemeMode,
  type ThemePalette,
} from '../constants/theme';

const SETTINGS_PROFILE_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_settings_profile';

export interface ExtendedUserProfile {
  id?: number | string;
  username?: string;
  first_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  full_name?: string;
  email?: string;
  contact_number?: string;
  phone?: string;
  position?: string;
  department?: string;
  role?: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  faculty_id?: string | number;
  photo?: string | null;
  photo_url?: string | null;
}

const isCanceledError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const e = err as { code?: string; name?: string };
  return e?.code === 'ERR_CANCELED' || e?.name === 'CanceledError' || e?.name === 'AbortError';
};

const showNotice = (title: string, message: string): void => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

export const SettingsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, logout, updateUser } = useAuth();
  const { palette, fonts, mode, setThemeMode } = useThemeMode();
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(palette, fonts), [palette, fonts]);

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<ExtendedUserProfile | null>(
    user as ExtendedUserProfile | null
  );
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState<string>('');
  const [showServerModal, setShowServerModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [photoError, setPhotoError] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Editable fields
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState<string>(user?.first_name || '');
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState<string>(user?.last_name || '');
  const [contactNumber, setContactNumber] = useState<string>(
    user?.contact_number || user?.phone || ''
  );
  const [email, setEmail] = useState<string>(user?.email || '');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState<string>('');

  // Optional profile photo upload state (web browser file picker)
  const [newPhotoBase64, setNewPhotoBase64] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [photoPreview, setPhotoPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const resolveSafePhotoUri = useCallback(
    (url?: string | null): string | undefined => {
      if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return undefined;
      const trimmed = url.trim();
      if (!trimmed) return undefined;
      const candidate =
        trimmed.startsWith('http://') ||
        trimmed.startsWith('https://') ||
        trimmed.startsWith('data:')
          ? trimmed
          : serverBase
          ? `${serverBase.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${trimmed.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`
          : '';
      return isSafeUrl(candidate) ? candidate : undefined;
    },
    [serverBase]
  );

  const fetchProfile = useCallback(async () => {
    abortRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortRef.current = controller;

    try {
      const [cached, base] = await Promise.all([
        AsyncStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_PROFILE_CACHE_KEY),
        getServerBaseUrl(),
      ]);
      if (isMountedRef.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {
        if (base) setServerBase(base);
        if (cached) {
          const parsed: ExtendedUserProfile = JSON.parse(cached);
          setProfile(parsed);
          setFirstName(parsed.first_name || '');
          setLastName(parsed.last_name || '');
          setContactNumber(parsed.contact_number || parsed.phone || '');
          setEmail(parsed.email || '');
        }
      }
    } catch {}

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<ExtendedUserProfile>('me/', {
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      if (!isMountedRef.current || controller.signal.aborted) return;
      if (res.data) {
        setProfile(res.data);
        setFirstName(res.data.first_name || '');
        setLastName(res.data.last_name || '');
        setContactNumber(res.data.contact_number || res.data.phone || '');
        setEmail(res.data.email || '');
        AsyncStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_PROFILE_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.data)).catch(() => {});
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (isCanceledError(err)) return;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    void fetchProfile();
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      abortRef.current?.abort();
    };
  }, [fetchProfile]);

  // Safe cross-platform photo picker (uses native browser file input on web; avoids uninstalled native picker package)
  const handlePickPhoto = () => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof document !== 'undefined') {
      const input = document.createElement('input');
      input.type = 'file';
      input.accept = 'image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp';
      input.onchange = () => {
        const file = input.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
          showNotice('File Too Large', 'Please choose a photo smaller than 5 MB.');
          return;
        }
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
          const result = typeof reader.result === 'string' ? reader.result : '';
          if (isMountedRef.current && isSafeUrl(result)) {
            setNewPhotoBase64(result);
            setPhotoPreview(result);
            setPhotoError(false);
          } else {
            showNotice('Unsupported File', 'Please select a PNG, JPG, or WebP image.');
          }
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      };
      input.click();
      return;
    }

    showNotice(
      'Update Profile Photo',
      'To upload a new profile photo, sign in to the AttendSure web portal on a computer or contact your school administrator.'
    );
  };

  const handleSaveProfile = async () => {
    const cleanFirst = cleanText(firstName, 80);
    const cleanLast = cleanText(lastName, 80);
    const cleanEmail = cleanText(email, 120);
    const cleanPhone = cleanText(contactNumber, 20);
    const cleanPassword = newPassword.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '').trim();

    if (!cleanFirst || !cleanLast) {
      showNotice('Missing Name', 'First name and last name are required.');
      return;
    }

    if (cleanEmail && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(cleanEmail)) {
      showNotice('Invalid Email', 'Please enter a valid email address.');
      return;
    }

    if (cleanPassword && cleanPassword.length < 8) {
      showNotice('Password Too Short', 'Your new password must be at least 8 characters long.');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, string> = {
        first_name: cleanFirst,
        last_name: cleanLast,
        contact_number: cleanPhone,
        email: cleanEmail,
      };

      if (cleanPassword) {
        payload.password = cleanPassword;
      }

      if (newPhotoBase64 && isSafeUrl(newPhotoBase64)) {
        payload.photo = newPhotoBase64;
      }

      const res = await apiClient.put<ExtendedUserProfile>('me/', payload);
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;

      if (res.data) {
        setProfile(res.data);
        AsyncStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_PROFILE_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.data)).catch(() => {});
        await updateUser(res.data as Partial<NonNullable<typeof user>>);
      }

      setNewPassword('');
      setNewPhotoBase64(null);
      showNotice('Saved', 'Your account details have been updated.');
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      showNotice(
        'Update Failed',
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not save your account changes. Please try again.')
      );
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current) setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSignOut = () => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      if (window.confirm('Sign out of AttendSure on this device?')) {
        void logout();
      }
      return;
    }
    Alert.alert('Sign Out', 'Sign out of AttendSure on this device?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Sign Out', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => void logout() },
    ]);
  };

  const qrPayload = JSON.stringify({
    app: 'ATTENDSURE_FACULTY_PASS',
    id: cleanText(String(profile?.employee_id || profile?.username || 'FACULTY'), 40),
    name: cleanText(profile?.full_name || `${firstName} ${lastName}`.trim(), 120),
    role: cleanText(profile?.role || 'FACULTY', 30),
  });

  const displayPhoto =
    (photoPreview && isSafeUrl(photoPreview) ? photoPreview : undefined) ||
    resolveSafePhotoUri(profile?.photo_url || profile?.photo);

  useEffect(() => {
    setPhotoError(false);
  }, [displayPhoto]);

  const themeOptions: Array<{
    key: ThemeMode;
    label: string;
    icon: keyof typeof Ionicons.glyphMap;
  }> = [
    { key: 'light', label: 'Light', icon: 'sunny-outline' },
    { key: 'dark', label: 'Dark', icon: 'moon-outline' },
    { key: 'high-contrast', label: 'Daylight', icon: 'contrast-outline' },
  ];

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {/* Profile Header & Photo */}
        <View style={styles.profileHeaderCard}>
          <View style={styles.avatarWrapper}>
            <View style={styles.avatarCircle}>
              {displayPhoto && !photoError ? (
                <Image
                  source={{ uri: displayPhoto }}
                  style={styles.avatarImg}
                  contentFit="cover"
                  onError={() => setPhotoError(true)}
                />
              ) : (
                <Ionicons name="person" size={40} color={palette.primary} />
              )}
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cameraBadgeBtn}
              onPress={handlePickPhoto}
              accessibilityLabel="Change profile photo"
            >
              <Ionicons name="camera" size={14} color={palette.onPrimary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.profileName}>
            {profile?.full_name || `${firstName} ${lastName}`.trim() || 'Faculty Account'}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileRole}>{profile?.position || profile?.role || 'Faculty'}</Text>
          {profile?.department ? (
            <Text style={styles.profileDept}>{profile.department}</Text>
          ) : null}

          <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
            <View style={styles.idChip}>
              <Ionicons name="id-card-outline" size={12} color={palette.primary} />
              <Text style={styles.idChipText}>
                ID: {profile?.employee_id || profile?.username || '—'}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.idChip, { backgroundColor: palette.successLight }]}>
              <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark-outline" size={12} color={palette.success} />
              <Text style={[styles.idChipText, { color: palette.success }]}>Verified Account</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Color Mode Switcher */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Display &amp; Color Mode</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>
            Choose a theme that is comfortable indoors or in bright daylight.
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.themeRow}>
            {themeOptions.map((opt) => {
              const active = mode === opt.key;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.key}
                  style={[styles.themeBtn, active && styles.themeBtnActive]}
                  onPress={() => setThemeMode(opt.key)}
                  activeOpacity={0.8}
                >
                  <Ionicons
                    name={opt.icon}
                    size={15}
                    color={active ? palette.onPrimary : palette.textSecondary}
                  />
                  <Text style={[styles.themeBtnText, active && styles.themeBtnTextActive]}>
                    {opt.label}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* School Server Connection */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>School Server Address</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>
            {serverBase || 'Connected automatically to your school server'}
          </Text>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.secondaryBtn}
            onPress={() => setShowServerModal(true)}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <Ionicons name="server-outline" size={16} color={palette.textPrimary} />
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Change Server Address</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* Digital Faculty ID Pass */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Digital Faculty ID Pass</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>
            Faculty check-ins use on-device facial recognition and campus GPS inside this app.
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.qrContainer}>
            <View style={styles.qrQuietZone}>
              <QRCode value={qrPayload} size={140} color="#003460" backgroundColor="#ffffff" />
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Personal Information Form */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Personal Information</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>
            Update your name, mobile number, email address, and password.
          </Text>

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>First Name</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={firstName}
            onChangeText={setFirstName}
            placeholder="Enter first name"
            placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
            maxLength={80}
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Last Name</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={lastName}
            onChangeText={setLastName}
            placeholder="Enter last name"
            placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
            maxLength={80}
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Email Address</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={email}
            onChangeText={setEmail}
            keyboardType="email-address"
            autoCapitalize="none"
            autoCorrect={false}
            placeholder="Enter email address"
            placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
            maxLength={120}
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Mobile Phone Number</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={contactNumber}
            onChangeText={setContactNumber}
            keyboardType="phone-pad"
            placeholder="09XXXXXXXXX"
            placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
            maxLength={20}
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>New Password (leave blank to keep current)</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={newPassword}
            onChangeText={setNewPassword}
            secureTextEntry
            placeholder="Minimum 8 characters"
            placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
            maxLength={128}
          />

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.saveBtn, saving && { opacity: 0.65 }]}
            onPress={() => void handleSaveProfile()}
            disabled={saving}
            activeOpacity={0.85}
          >
            {saving ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.onPrimary} />
            ) : (
              <Text style={styles.saveBtnText}>Save Changes</Text>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* Sign Out Button */}
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.logoutBtn} onPress={handleSignOut} activeOpacity={0.85}>
          <Ionicons name="log-out-outline" size={18} color={palette.onPrimary} />
          <Text style={styles.logoutBtnText}>Sign Out</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </ScrollView>

      <ServerConfigModal
        visible={showServerModal}
        onClose={() => {
          setShowServerModal(false);
          getServerBaseUrl()
            .then((url) => {
              if (isMountedRef.current) setServerBase(url);
            })
            .catch(() => {});
        }}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

const createStyles = (palette: ThemePalette, fonts: ReturnType<typeof useThemeMode>['fonts']) =>
  StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },
    scrollContent: { padding: 16, paddingBottom: 120, gap: 14 },
    profileHeaderCard: {
      backgroundColor: palette.surface,
      borderRadius: 16,
      padding: 18,
      alignItems: 'center',
      borderWidth: 1,
      borderColor: palette.border,
    },
    avatarWrapper: {
      position: 'relative',
      marginBottom: 10,
    },
    avatarCircle: {
      width: 80,

/**
 * AttendSure - Authentication Storage & Session API Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/authService.ts
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { ENDPOINTS } from '../api/endpoints';
import { cleanText, getFriendlyErrorMessage } from '../constants/theme';

export interface UserAccount {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  role: string;
}

export interface AuthResponse {
  token: string;
  user: UserAccount;
}

const TOKEN_KEY = 'attendsure_auth_token';
const USER_KEY = 'attendsure_user_data';

function stripControlChars(value: string): string {
  return String(value || '').replace(/[\u0000-\u001F\u007F]/g, '').trim();
}

const safeStorage = {
  async get(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      try {
        return typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem(key) : null;
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    }
    try {
      return await SecureStore.getItemAsync(key);
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  },

  async set(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      try {
        if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
          localStorage.setItem(key, value);
        }
      } catch {}
      return;
    }
    try {
      await SecureStore.setItemAsync(key, value);
    } catch {}
  },

  async delete(key: string): Promise<void> {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      try {
        if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
          localStorage.removeItem(key);
        }
      } catch {}
      return;
    }
    try {
      await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(key);
    } catch {}
  },
};

export const getStoredToken = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
  const token = await safeStorage.get(TOKEN_KEY);
  return token ? stripControlChars(token) : null;
};

export const storeAuthSession = async (token: string, user: UserAccount): Promise<void> => {
  const safeToken = stripControlChars(token);
  if (!safeToken) return;
  await safeStorage.set(TOKEN_KEY, safeToken);
  await safeStorage.set(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
  apiClient.defaults.headers.common['Authorization'] = `Token ${safeToken}`;
};

export const getStoredUser = async (): Promise<UserAccount | null> => {
  const raw = await safeStorage.get(USER_KEY);
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as UserAccount;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

export const clearAuthSession = async (): Promise<void> => {
  await safeStorage.delete(TOKEN_KEY);
  await safeStorage.delete(USER_KEY);
  delete apiClient.defaults.headers.common['Authorization'];
};

export const loginApi = async (
  username: string,
  password: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<AuthResponse> => {
  const safeUsername = cleanText(username, 150);
  const safePassword = stripControlChars(password);

  if (!safeUsername || !safePassword) {
    throw new Error('Please enter both username and password.');
  }

  try {
    const response = await apiClient.post<AuthResponse>(
      ENDPOINTS.LOGIN,
      {
        username: safeUsername,
        password: safePassword,
      },
      { signal }
    );
    return response.data;
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    throw new Error(
      getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Unable to sign in. Please check your username and password.')
    );
  }
};

export const fetchCurrentProfile = async (signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<UserAccount> => {
  try {
    const response = await apiClient.get<UserAccount>(ENDPOINTS.CURRENT_USER, { signal });
    return response.data;
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    throw new Error(
      getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not load your account profile right now.')
    );
  }
};

/**
 * AttendSure - Background Campus GPS Presence Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/backgroundLocation.ts
 */

import * as TaskManager from 'expo-task-manager';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import defaultApiClient, { apiClient as namedClient } from '../api/client';
import { PALETTES } from '../constants/theme';
import { getHardwareDeviceUuid } from './deviceService';
import {
  getRealDeviceModel,
  getRealBatteryPercentage,
  evaluateRealSignal,
} from './telemetryService';
import { enqueueOfflineHeartbeat, QueuedTelemetryPayload } from './offlineQueue';

const client = defaultApiClient || namedClient;
export const BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK = 'ATTENDSURE_BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY';

interface BackgroundLocationTaskData {
  locations?: Location.LocationObject[];
}

TaskManager.defineTask<BackgroundLocationTaskData>(
  BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK,
  async ({ data, error }) => {
    if (error || !data) return;

    const locations = Array.isArray(data.locations) ? data.locations : [];
    const latest = locations.length > 0 ? locations[locations.length - 1] : null;

    if (!latest || !latest.coords) return;

    const lat = latest.coords.latitude;
    const lng = latest.coords.longitude;

    if (
      !Number.isFinite(lat) ||
      !Number.isFinite(lng) ||
      lat < -90 ||
      lat > 90 ||
      lng < -180 ||
      lng > 180
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const isMock = Boolean(latest.mocked);
    const accuracy = latest.coords.accuracy ?? null;

    const [deviceRes, batteryRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
      getHardwareDeviceUuid(),
      getRealBatteryPercentage(),
    ]);

    const deviceId = deviceRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? deviceRes.value : '';
    const batteryLevel = batteryRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? batteryRes.value : null;
    const deviceModel = getRealDeviceModel();
    const signal = evaluateRealSignal(accuracy);

    try {
      await client.post(
        'geofence/check-in/',
        {
          latitude: lat,
          longitude: lng,
          accuracy,
          accuracy_meters: accuracy,
          is_mock_location: isMock,
          battery_level: batteryLevel,
          device_id: deviceId,
          client_device_id: deviceId,
          mac_address: deviceId,
          device_model: deviceModel,
          signal_strength: signal.label,
          signal_percentage: signal.percentage,
        },
        { timeout: 8000 }
      );
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const status = (err as { response?: { status?: number } })?.response?.status;
      // Never queue unauthorized (401) or forbidden (403) responses
      if (status === 401 || status === 403) return;

      try {
        const offlinePayload: QueuedTelemetryPayload = {
          client_device_id: deviceId,
          latitude: lat,
          longitude: lng,
          accuracy,
          battery_level: batteryLevel,
          is_mock_location: isMock,
          mac_address: deviceId,
          signal_strength: signal.label,
          signal_percentage: signal.percentage,
          device_model: deviceModel,
        };
        await enqueueOfflineHeartbeat(offlinePayload);
      } catch {
        // Ignore background storage failures quietly
      }
    }
  }
);

export async function startPassivePresenceTracking(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const { status: fgStatus } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
    if (fgStatus !== 'granted') return false;

    const { status: bgStatus } = await Location.requestBackgroundPermissionsAsync();
    if (bgStatus !== 'granted') return false;

    const isRegistered = await TaskManager.isTaskRegisteredAsync(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK);
    if (!isRegistered) {
      await Location.startLocationUpdatesAsync(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK, {
        accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High,
        timeInterval: 60000,
        distanceInterval: 5,
        deferredUpdatesInterval: 60000,
        pausesUpdatesAutomatically: false,
        showsBackgroundLocationIndicator: true,
        activityType: Location.ActivityType.Fitness,
        foregroundService: {
          notificationTitle: 'AttendSure Attendance Active',
          notificationBody: 'Campus location check-in is active.',
          notificationColor: PALETTES.light.primary,
        },
      });
    }
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export async function stopPassivePresenceTracking(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const isRegistered = await TaskManager.isTaskRegisteredAsync(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK);
    if (isRegistered) {
      await Location.stopLocationUpdatesAsync(BACKGROUND_TELEMETRY_TASK);
    }
  } catch {}
}

/**
 * AttendSure - Mobile Server Discovery & URL Security Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/configService.ts
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

export const SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY = 'attendsure_server_base_url';
const CUSTOM_SERVER_URL_KEY = 'attendsure_custom_server_url';
const CACHE_TTL_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;

const DANGEROUS_SCHEME_RE = /^(javascript|data|file|vbscript|blob|about):/i;
const ANY_SCHEME_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i;

export const ENDPOINTS = {
  health: '/health/db/',
  auth: {
    login: '/auth/login/',
    logout: '/auth/logout/',
    userProfile: '/me/',
  },
  faculty: {
    list: '/v1/accounts/faculty/',
  },
  classroom: {
    studentList: '/students/',
    classList: '/classroom/assigned/',
    takeAttendance: '/classroom/batch-scan/',
    logs: '/classroom/logs/',
  },
  clearance: {
    list: '/gate-passes/',
  },
  telemetry: {
    heartbeat: '/geofence/check-in/',
    geofenceStatus: '/geofence/status/',
  },
  reports: {
    dtr: '/dtr/',
    sf1: (sectionId: number | string) =>
      `/reports/sf1/?section_id=${encodeURIComponent(String(sectionId))}`,
    sf2: (sectionId: number | string) =>
      `/reports/sf2/?section_id=${encodeURIComponent(String(sectionId))}`,
    sf4: '/reports/sf4/',
  },
};

function getLoopbackFallback(): string {
  return Platform.OS === 'android' ? 'http://10.0.2.2:8000' : 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
}

function stripControlChars(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/[\u0000-\u001F\u007F]/g, '').trim();
}

export function isValidServerUrl(url?: string | null): boolean {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return false;
  const trimmed = stripControlChars(url);
  if (!trimmed) return false;

  // Reject dangerous or non-HTTP(S) schemes BEFORE normalizing or prepending http://
  if (DANGEROUS_SCHEME_RE.test(trimmed)) return false;
  if (ANY_SCHEME_RE.test(trimmed) && !/^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    return false;
  }

  try {
    const normalized = /^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed) ? trimmed : `http://${trimmed}`;
    const parsed = new URL(normalized);
    return (
      (parsed.protocol === 'http:' || parsed.protocol === 'https:') &&
      Boolean(parsed.hostname)
    );
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function cleanUrl(rawUrl?: string | null): string {
  const fallback = getLoopbackFallback();
  if (!rawUrl || typeof rawUrl !== 'string') {
    return fallback;
  }

  const sanitized = stripControlChars(rawUrl);
  if (!sanitized || DANGEROUS_SCHEME_RE.test(sanitized)) {
    return fallback;
  }
  if (ANY_SCHEME_RE.test(sanitized) && !/^https?:\/\//i.test(sanitized)) {
    return fallback;
  }

  let trimmed = sanitized
    .replace(/\/+$/, '')
    .replace(/\/api$/i, '')
    .replace(/\/+$/, '');

  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    trimmed = `http://${trimmed}`;
  }

  try {
    const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
    if (parsed.protocol !== 'http:' && parsed.protocol !== 'https:') {
      return fallback;
    }
    if (!parsed.hostname) {
      return fallback;
    }
    return trimmed;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

export const sanitizeUrl = cleanUrl;

function getMetroHostIp(): string | null {
  try {
    const Constants = require('expo-constants').default;
    const hostUri =
      Constants?.expoConfig?.hostUri ||
      Constants?.manifest?.debuggerHost ||
      Constants?.manifest2?.extra?.expoGo?.debuggerHost;
    if (hostUri && typeof hostUri === 'string') {
      const ip = hostUri.split(':')[0];
      if (ip && ip !== 'localhost' && ip !== '127.0.0.1' && !ip.includes('expo.app')) {
        return ip;
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
}

function loadRootEndpoints(): {
  active_tunnel_url?: string;
  lan_servers?: string[];
  production_domain?: string;
} {
  try {
    return require('../../../endpoints.json');
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export function getCandidateServers(): string[] {
  const candidates: string[] = [];
  const rootEndpoints = loadRootEndpoints();
  const isNativeMobile = Platform.OS === 'android' || Platform.OS === 'ios';

  // 1. Local school Wi-Fi servers first (fastest direct route on campus)
  if (Array.isArray(rootEndpoints.lan_servers)) {
    for (const lan of rootEndpoints.lan_servers) {
      if (lan && typeof lan === 'string' && isValidServerUrl(lan)) {
        candidates.push(cleanUrl(lan));
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. Environment variable from mobile/.env
  const envUrl = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL;
  if (envUrl && isValidServerUrl(envUrl)) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(envUrl));
  }

  // 3. Dynamic host IP from Expo Metro bundler during local development
  const metroIp = getMetroHostIp();
  if (metroIp && isValidServerUrl(`http://${metroIp}:8000`)) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(`http://${metroIp}:8000`));
  }

  // 4. Configured tunnel URL (skip loopback addresses on physical phones)
  if (rootEndpoints.active_tunnel_url && isValidServerUrl(rootEndpoints.active_tunnel_url)) {
    const tunnel = cleanUrl(rootEndpoints.active_tunnel_url);
    const isLoopback = tunnel.includes('127.0.0.1') || tunnel.includes('localhost');
    if (!isNativeMobile || !isLoopback) {
      candidates.push(tunnel);
    }
  }

  // 5. Production school domain
  if (rootEndpoints.production_domain && isValidServerUrl(rootEndpoints.production_domain)) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(rootEndpoints.production_domain));
  }

  // 6. Web / Emulator loopback fallback only
  if (!isNativeMobile) {
    candidates.push('http://127.0.0.1:8000');
  }

  return Array.from(new Set(candidates.filter((u) => Boolean(u && isValidServerUrl(u)))));
}

export function getDefaultBaseUrl(): string {
  return getCandidateServers()[0] || getLoopbackFallback();
}

export const DEFAULT_SERVER_URL = getDefaultBaseUrl();

let cachedUrl: string | null = null;
let lastCacheTime = 0;

export function invalidateServerCache(): void {
  cachedUrl = null;
  lastCacheTime = 0;
}

export async function resolveFastestServer(): Promise<string> {
  // Always honor an explicitly configured server URL first
  const customUrl = await AsyncStorage.getItem(CUSTOM_SERVER_URL_KEY).catch(() => null);
  const candidates = getCandidateServers();
  if (customUrl && isValidServerUrl(customUrl)) {
    const cleanedCustom = cleanUrl(customUrl);
    if (!candidates.includes(cleanedCustom)) {
      candidates.unshift(cleanedCustom);
    }
  }

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    cachedUrl = getDefaultBaseUrl();
    return cachedUrl;
  }

  const probeServer = async (
    targetUrl: string,
    externalController?: AbortController
  ): Promise<string> => {
    const controller = externalController || new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2200);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${targetUrl}/api/health/db/`, {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 400) return targetUrl;
      throw new Error(`Status ${res.status}`);
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    }
  };

  // If user manually set a custom server, try it first before failing over
  if (customUrl && isValidServerUrl(customUrl)) {
    const cleanedCustom = cleanUrl(customUrl);
    try {
      const okUrl = await probeServer(cleanedCustom);
      cachedUrl = okUrl;
      lastCacheTime = Date.now();
      return okUrl;
    } catch {
      // Fall through to race remaining candidates if custom URL is temporarily unreachable
    }
  }

  const activeControllers: AbortController[] = [];
  const abortAllProbes = () => {
    activeControllers.forEach((ctrl) => {
      try {
        ctrl.abort();
      } catch {}
    });
  };

  try {
    const fastestUrl = await Promise.any(
      candidates.map((url) => {
        const ctrl = new AbortController();
        activeControllers.push(ctrl);
        return probeServer(url, ctrl);
      })
    );
    // Cancel all losing probes immediately after the fastest responds
    abortAllProbes();
    cachedUrl = fastestUrl;
    lastCacheTime = Date.now();
    AsyncStorage.setItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY, fastestUrl).catch(() => {});
    return fastestUrl;
  } catch {
    abortAllProbes();
    const stored =
      customUrl || (await AsyncStorage.getItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY).catch(() => null));
    const fallback = stored && isValidServerUrl(stored) ? cleanUrl(stored) : candidates[0];
    cachedUrl = fallback;
    return fallback;
  }
}

export async function getServerBaseUrl(): Promise<string> {
  if (cachedUrl && Date.now() - lastCacheTime < CACHE_TTL_MS) {
    return cachedUrl;
  }
  const customUrl = await AsyncStorage.getItem(CUSTOM_SERVER_URL_KEY).catch(() => null);
  if (customUrl && isValidServerUrl(customUrl)) {
    cachedUrl = cleanUrl(customUrl);
    lastCacheTime = Date.now();
    return cachedUrl;
  }
  return resolveFastestServer();
}

export async function setServerBaseUrl(newUrl: string): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!isValidServerUrl(newUrl)) {
    throw new Error('Please enter a valid web address starting with http:// or https://.');
  }
  const cleaned = cleanUrl(newUrl);
  try {
    await AsyncStorage.multiSet([
      [SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY, cleaned],
      [CUSTOM_SERVER_URL_KEY, cleaned],
    ]);
    cachedUrl = cleaned;
    lastCacheTime = Date.now();
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export async function resetServerBaseUrl(): Promise<void> {
  await AsyncStorage.multiRemove([SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY, CUSTOM_SERVER_URL_KEY]).catch(
    () => {}
  );
  invalidateServerCache();
}

export async function buildApiUrl(endpoint: string): Promise<string> {
  const base = await getServerBaseUrl();
  const safePath = stripControlChars(endpoint).replace(/\.\.\//g, '');
  const cleanEndpoint = safePath.startsWith('/') ? safePath : `/${safePath}`;
  return `${base}/api${cleanEndpoint}`;
}

export async function testServerConnection(
  testUrl?: string
): Promise<{ ok: boolean; message: string; pingMs: number }> {
  if (testUrl !== undefined && !isValidServerUrl(testUrl)) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      message: 'Please enter a valid school server web address starting with http:// or https://.',
      pingMs: 0,
    };
  }

  const target = cleanUrl(testUrl || cachedUrl || getDefaultBaseUrl());
  const startTime = Date.now();
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${target}/api/health/db/`, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });

    const pingMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    if (response.status >= 200 && response.status < 400) {
      return { ok: true, message: `Connected to school server (${pingMs}ms)`, pingMs };
    }
    return { ok: false, message: `Server replied with status ${response.status}`, pingMs };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const pingMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    const isAbort =
      err !== null &&
      typeof err === 'object' &&
      'name' in err &&
      (err as { name?: string }).name === 'AbortError';
    return {
      ok: false,
      message: isAbort
        ? 'Connection timed out. Checking alternative route...'
        : 'Cannot reach school server. Verify your network connection.',
      pingMs,
    };
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }
}

export default {
  getServerBaseUrl,
  setServerBaseUrl,
  resetServerBaseUrl,
  resolveFastestServer,
  invalidateServerCache,
  testServerConnection,
  buildApiUrl,
  isValidServerUrl,
  cleanUrl,
  sanitizeUrl,
  getDefaultBaseUrl,
  SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY,
  DEFAULT_SERVER_URL,
  ENDPOINTS,
};

/**
 * AttendSure - Hardware Device Identity Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/deviceService.ts
 *
 * Security & 1-to-1 Device Binding:
 * 1. Cryptographic Entropy: Generates unguessable device IDs using expo-crypto.
 * 2. Encrypted Storage: Persists IDs using hardware-backed SecureStore.
 * 3. In-Memory Caching: Speeds up repeated lookups without extra disk reads.
 * 4. Decoupled Service: Prevents circular dependencies across screens and background tasks.
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import * as Crypto from 'expo-crypto';
import * as Application from 'expo-application';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

export const SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY = 'attendsure_hardware_device_uuid';

// In-memory cache to prevent repeated storage disk reads
let cachedDeviceUuid: string | null = null;

function readWebStorageSafe(key: string): string | null {
  try {
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      return localStorage.getItem(key);
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
}

function writeWebStorageSafe(key: string, value: string): void {
  try {
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.setItem(key, value);
    }
  } catch {}
}

/**
 * Generates or retrieves a single canonical, hardware-backed device UUID
 * shared across faculty facial check-ins, foreground location verification,
 * and background campus presence updates.
 */
export const getOrCreateDeviceUuid = async (): Promise<string> => {
  if (cachedDeviceUuid) {
    return cachedDeviceUuid;
  }

  try {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      const stored = readWebStorageSafe(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY);
      if (stored && stored.trim().length > 0) {
        cachedDeviceUuid = stored.trim();
        return cachedDeviceUuid;
      }
      const randomPart =
        typeof Crypto.randomUUID === 'function'
          ? Crypto.randomUUID().substring(0, 13).toUpperCase()
          : Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10).toUpperCase();
      const webUuid = `DEV-WEB-${randomPart}`;
      writeWebStorageSafe(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, webUuid);
      cachedDeviceUuid = webUuid;
      return webUuid;
    }

    // 1. Prefer hardware-encrypted SecureStore
    const secureExisting = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY).catch(() => null);
    if (secureExisting && secureExisting.trim().length > 0) {
      const clean = secureExisting.trim();
      cachedDeviceUuid = clean;
      AsyncStorage.setItem(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, clean).catch(() => {});
      return clean;
    }

    // 2. Migrate any previously saved AsyncStorage hardware ID to keep 1-to-1 bindings intact
    const asyncExisting = await AsyncStorage.getItem(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY).catch(() => null);
    if (asyncExisting && asyncExisting.trim().length > 0) {
      const clean = asyncExisting.trim();
      cachedDeviceUuid = clean;
      SecureStore.setItemAsync(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, clean, {
        keychainAccessible: SecureStore.AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY,
      }).catch(() => {});
      return clean;
    }

    // 3. Derive stable hardware ID when available, otherwise generate cryptographic entropy
    let hardwareSeed: string | null = null;
    try {
      if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
        hardwareSeed = Application.getAndroidId();
      } else if (Platform.OS === 'ios') {
        hardwareSeed = await Application.getIosIdForVendorAsync();
      }
    } catch {}

    let generated: string;
    if (hardwareSeed && hardwareSeed.trim().length > 0) {
      generated = `DEV-${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()}-${hardwareSeed.trim().toUpperCase()}`;
    } else {
      const randomBytes = await Crypto.getRandomBytesAsync(8);
      const hex = Array.from(randomBytes)
        .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join('')
        .toUpperCase();
      generated = `DEV-${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()}-${hex.substring(0, 8)}-${hex.substring(8, 16)}`;
    }

    await Promise.allSettled([
      SecureStore.setItemAsync(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, generated, {
        keychainAccessible: SecureStore.AFTER_FIRST_UNLOCK_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY,
      }),
      AsyncStorage.setItem(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, generated),
    ]);

    cachedDeviceUuid = generated;
    return generated;
  } catch {
    const fallback = `DEV-${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()}-TEMP-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`;
    cachedDeviceUuid = fallback;
    return fallback;
  }
};

export const getHardwareDeviceUuid = getOrCreateDeviceUuid;

import { apiClient } from '../api/client';

export async function fetchSchoolSettings(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<SchoolProfile> {
  try {
    const response = await apiClient.get<SchoolProfile>(ENDPOINTS.SCHOOL_PROFILE, { signal });
    return response.data;
  } catch (err: unknown) {

      getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not load school profile settings right now.')

export async function fetchFacultyDirectory(
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<FacultyProfileObject[]> {
  try {
    const response = await apiClient.get<
      FacultyProfileObject[] | { results?: FacultyProfileObject[] }
    >(FACULTY_LIST_ENDPOINT, { signal });

    if (Array.isArray(response.data)) {
      return response.data;
    }
    return Array.isArray(response.data?.results) ? response.data.results : [];
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    throw new Error(
      getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not load the faculty list right now.')
    );
  }
}

export async function fetchTodayAttendanceLogs(
  facultyId?: number,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<AttendanceLog[]> {
  const params: Record<string, number> = {};
  if (typeof facultyId === 'number' && Number.isFinite(facultyId) && facultyId > 0) {
    params.faculty_id = facultyId;
  }

  try {
    const response = await apiClient.get<
      AttendanceLog[] | { results?: AttendanceLog[] }
    >(ENDPOINTS.ATTENDANCE_LOGS, { params, signal });

    if (Array.isArray(response.data)) {
      return response.data;
    }
    return Array.isArray(response.data?.results) ? response.data.results : [];
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    throw new Error(
      getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not load today’s attendance logs right now.')
    );
  }
}

export interface FacultyWorkspaceSummary {
  school: SchoolProfile | null;
  faculty: FacultyProfileObject[];
  logs: AttendanceLog[];
}

export async function fetchFacultyWorkspaceSummary(
  facultyId?: number,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<FacultyWorkspaceSummary> {
  const [schoolRes, facultyRes, logsRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
    fetchSchoolSettings(signal),
    fetchFacultyDirectory(signal),
    fetchTodayAttendanceLogs(facultyId, signal),
  ]);

  return {
    school: schoolRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? schoolRes.value : null,
    faculty: facultyRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? facultyRes.value : [],
    logs: logsRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? logsRes.value : [],
  };
}

/**
 * AttendSure - Offline Storage & Sync Queue Engine
 * File: mobile/src/services/offlineQueue.ts
 */

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';

const OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY = 'attendsure_offline_queue';
const MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS = 8;
const MAX_HEARTBEAT_AGE_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

let isSyncing = false;

export interface QueuedTelemetryPayload {
  client_device_id?: string | null;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  accuracy?: number | null;
  battery_level?: number | null;
  is_mock_location?: boolean;
  ip_address?: string | null;
  mac_address?: string | null;
  signal_strength?: string | null;
  signal_percentage?: number | null;
  device_model?: string | null;
}

export interface OfflineQueueItem {
  id: string;
  endpoint: string;
  method: 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH';
  payload: unknown;
  timestamp: number;
  retryCount?: number;
}

/**
 * Cybersecurity: Only allow relative AttendSure API paths inside the offline queue.
 * Rejects absolute http(s):// URLs, dangerous schemes, protocol-relative URLs, and path traversal.
 */
function isAllowedRelativeEndpoint(endpoint?: string | null): endpoint is string {
  if (!endpoint || typeof endpoint !== 'string') return false;
  const trimmed = endpoint.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return false;
  if (
    /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(trimmed) ||
    trimmed.startsWith('//') ||
    trimmed.includes('..') ||
    /[\u0000-\u001F\u007F]/.test(trimmed)
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function isHeartbeatEndpoint(endpoint: string): boolean {
  const lower = endpoint.toLowerCase();
  return (
    lower.includes('check-in') ||
    lower.includes('checkin') ||
    lower.includes('heartbeat')
  );
}

async function readQueueSafe(): Promise<OfflineQueueItem[]> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as OfflineQueueItem[]) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Returns the total number of records waiting to be uploaded.
 */
export async function getUnsyncedCount(): Promise<number> {
  const items = await readQueueSafe();
  return items.length;
}

/**
 * Queues an offline campus location check-in with automatic deduplication.
 * Replaces older check-ins so records do not stack up during long offline periods.
 */
export async function enqueueOfflineHeartbeat(payload: QueuedTelemetryPayload): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const current = await readQueueSafe();

    // Keep only the newest campus location ping
    const filtered = current.filter((item) => !isHeartbeatEndpoint(item.endpoint || ''));

    filtered.push({
      id: `hb_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`,
      endpoint: 'geofence/check-in/',
      method: 'POST',
      payload: {
        ...payload,
        device_id: payload.client_device_id ?? null,
        accuracy_meters: payload.accuracy ?? null,
      },
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      retryCount: 0,
    });

    await AsyncStorage.setItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(filtered));
  } catch {
    // Storage failures are ignored safely without leaking raw error objects
  }
}

/**
 * Generic queue action for class attendance and campus check-in records.
 */
export async function queueOfflineAction(
  endpoint: string,
  payload: unknown,
  method: 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' = 'POST'
): Promise<void> {
  if (!isAllowedRelativeEndpoint(endpoint)) {
    return;
  }

  const safeEndpoint = endpoint.trim();

  try {
    let queue = await readQueueSafe();

    if (isHeartbeatEndpoint(safeEndpoint)) {
      queue = queue.filter((item) => !isHeartbeatEndpoint(item.endpoint || ''));
    }

    queue.push({
      id: `${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`,
      endpoint: safeEndpoint,
      method,
      payload,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      retryCount: 0,
    });

    await AsyncStorage.setItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(queue));
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage write errors safely
  }
}

/**
 * Uploads all queued offline records to the school server.
 * Protected by a concurrency lock to prevent duplicate submissions.
 */
export async function syncOfflineQueue(): Promise<{ synced: number; remaining: number }> {
  if (isSyncing) {
    return { synced: 0, remaining: await getUnsyncedCount() };
  }

  isSyncing = true;

  try {
    const queue = await readQueueSafe();
    if (queue.length === 0) {
      return { synced: 0, remaining: 0 };
    }

    const snapshotIds = new Set(queue.map((item) => item.id));
    const remaining: OfflineQueueItem[] = [];
    let synced = 0;

    for (const item of queue) {
      // Discard unsafe or external endpoints
      if (!isAllowedRelativeEndpoint(item?.endpoint)) {
        continue;
      }

      // Discard stale location pings older than 24 hours
      if (
        isHeartbeatEndpoint(item.endpoint) &&
        Date.now() - item.timestamp > MAX_HEARTBEAT_AGE_MS
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      try {
        if (item.method === 'PUT') {
          await apiClient.put(item.endpoint, item.payload, { timeout: 7000 });
        } else if (item.method === 'PATCH') {
          await apiClient.patch(item.endpoint, item.payload, { timeout: 7000 });
        } else {
          await apiClient.post(item.endpoint, item.payload, { timeout: 7000 });
        }
        synced += 1;
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        const status = (err as { response?: { status?: number } })?.response?.status;

        // Stop retrying immediately when session expires (401)
        if (status === 401) {
          break;
        }

        // Discard permanently rejected records (bad payload, forbidden, missing, or conflict)
        if (status === 400 || status === 403 || status === 404 || status === 409) {
          continue;
        }

        // Retry temporary network errors up to the retry limit
        const nextRetry = (item.retryCount || 0) + 1;
        if (nextRetry < MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS) {
          remaining.push({ ...item, retryCount: nextRetry });
        }
      }
    }

    // Preserve any new items queued while this sync pass was running
    const latestQueue = await readQueueSafe();
    const newlyAdded = latestQueue.filter((item) => !snapshotIds.has(item.id));
    const nextQueue = [...remaining, ...newlyAdded];

    await AsyncStorage.setItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextQueue));
    return { synced, remaining: nextQueue.length };
  } catch {
    return { synced: 0, remaining: await getUnsyncedCount() };
  } finally {
    isSyncing = false;
  }
}

/**
 * Clears the offline queue.
 */
export async function clearOfflineQueue(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await AsyncStorage.removeItem(OFFLINE_QUEUE_KEY);
  } catch {}
}

/**
 * AttendSure - Real-Time Mobile Push Notification & Presence Wake-Up Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/pushService.ts
 *
 * Reliability & Privacy:
 * 1. Expo Go Detection: Safely skips remote push registration inside Expo Go.
 * 2. Production Ready: Registers mobile push tokens for real-time parent alerts and presence updates.
 * 3. Clean Logging: Filters expected development sandbox warnings and avoids raw error dumps.
 */

import { Platform, LogBox } from 'react-native';
import Constants, { ExecutionEnvironment } from 'expo-constants';
import defaultApiClient, { apiClient as namedClient } from '../api/client';
import { PALETTES, getFriendlyErrorMessage } from '../constants/theme';
import {
  startPassivePresenceTracking,
  stopPassivePresenceTracking,
} from './backgroundLocation';

const client = defaultApiClient || namedClient;

export const BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK = 'ATTENDSURE_BACKGROUND_PUSH_TASK';
const NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID = 'attendsure-alerts';

// Ignore expected Expo Go remote push warning during local development
LogBox.ignoreLogs([
  '[expo-notifications] Push notifications (remote notifications) are unavailable in Expo Go',
]);

const isRunningInExpoGo =
  Constants.executionEnvironment === ExecutionEnvironment.StoreClient;

interface NotificationModuleLike {
  AndroidImportance?: { HIGH?: number };
  setNotificationHandler?: (handler: {
    handleNotification: () => Promise<{
      shouldShowAlert: boolean;
      shouldPlaySound: boolean;
      shouldSetBadge: boolean;
      shouldShowBanner: boolean;
      shouldShowList: boolean;
    }>;
  }) => void;
  setNotificationChannelAsync?: (
    channelId: string,
    config: {
      name: string;
      importance: number;
      lightColor: string;
      vibrationPattern: number[];
    }
  ) => Promise<unknown>;
  registerTaskAsync?: (taskName: string) => Promise<void>;
  getPermissionsAsync?: () => Promise<{ status: string }>;
  requestPermissionsAsync?: () => Promise<{ status: string }>;
  getExpoPushTokenAsync?: () => Promise<{ data?: string }>;
}

interface TaskManagerModuleLike {
  defineTask?: (
    taskName: string,
    executor: (body: { data?: Record<string, any>; error?: unknown }) => Promise<void>
  ) => void;
}

let Notifications: NotificationModuleLike | null = null;
let TaskManager: TaskManagerModuleLike | null = null;

// Only initialize remote push listeners outside the Expo Go sandbox
if (!isRunningInExpoGo && Platform.OS !== 'web') {
  try {
    Notifications = require('expo-notifications') as NotificationModuleLike;
    TaskManager = require('expo-task-manager') as TaskManagerModuleLike;

    if (Notifications?.setNotificationHandler) {
      Notifications.setNotificationHandler({
        handleNotification: async () => ({
          shouldShowAlert: true,
          shouldPlaySound: true,
          shouldSetBadge: false,
          shouldShowBanner: true,
          shouldShowList: true,
        }),
      });
    }

    if (Platform.OS === 'android' && Notifications?.setNotificationChannelAsync) {
      Notifications.setNotificationChannelAsync(NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID, {
        name: 'AttendSure Alerts',
        importance: Notifications.AndroidImportance?.HIGH ?? 4,
        lightColor: PALETTES.light.primary,
        vibrationPattern: [0, 250, 150, 250],
      }).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (TaskManager?.defineTask) {
      TaskManager.defineTask(BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK, async ({ data, error }) => {
        if (error) return;
        const action = String(data?.notification?.data?.action || '').toUpperCase();
        if (action === 'START_TRACKING_IN') {
          await startPassivePresenceTracking();
        } else if (action === 'STOP_TRACKING_OUT') {
          await stopPassivePresenceTracking();
        }
      });

      Notifications?.registerTaskAsync?.(BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK).catch(() => {});
    }
  } catch {
    // Native push modules unavailable in this runtime environment
  }
}

/**
 * Registers this device's push notification token with the AttendSure school server.
 * Safely returns null inside Expo Go or web environments.
 */
export async function registerPushTokenWithBackend(): Promise<string | null> {
  if (
    Platform.OS === 'web' ||
    isRunningInExpoGo ||
    !Notifications?.getPermissionsAsync ||
    !Notifications?.requestPermissionsAsync ||
    !Notifications?.getExpoPushTokenAsync
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    if (Platform.OS === 'android' && Notifications.setNotificationChannelAsync) {
      await Notifications.setNotificationChannelAsync(NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID, {
        name: 'AttendSure Alerts',
        importance: Notifications.AndroidImportance?.HIGH ?? 4,
        lightColor: PALETTES.light.primary,
        vibrationPattern: [0, 250, 150, 250],
      }).catch(() => {});
    }

    const { status: existingStatus } = await Notifications.getPermissionsAsync();
    let finalStatus = existingStatus;

    if (existingStatus !== 'granted') {
      const { status } = await Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync();
      finalStatus = status;
    }

    if (finalStatus !== 'granted') {
      return null;
    }

    const pushTokenData = await Notifications.getExpoPushTokenAsync();
    const token = typeof pushTokenData?.data === 'string'
      ? pushTokenData.data.replace(/[\u0000-\u001F\u007F]/g, '').trim()
      : '';

    if (!token) return null;

    await client.post('auth/register-push-token/', {
      push_token: token,
      platform: Platform.OS,
    });

    return token;
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    // Keep error handling safe without leaking raw server payloads
    getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Could not register push alerts.');
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * AttendSure - Safe Tactile & Web Audio Feedback Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/soundService.ts
 *
 * Uses built-in React Native Vibration on native devices and pure Web Audio
 * tone synthesis on web with zero external CDN dependencies or missing packages.
 */

import { Vibration, Platform } from 'react-native';

type FeedbackType = 'success' | 'warning' | 'error';

interface MinimalOscillator {
  type: string;
  frequency: {
    setValueAtTime: (frequency: number, startTime: number) => void;
  };
  connect: (destination: unknown) => void;
  start: (when?: number) => void;
  stop: (when?: number) => void;
}

interface MinimalGainNode {
  gain: {
    setValueAtTime: (value: number, startTime: number) => void;
    exponentialRampToValueAtTime: (value: number, endTime: number) => void;
  };
  connect: (destination: unknown) => void;
}

interface MinimalAudioContext {
  currentTime: number;
  destination: unknown;
  createOscillator: () => MinimalOscillator;
  createGain: () => MinimalGainNode;
  close?: () => Promise<void> | void;
}

type AudioContextCtor = new () => MinimalAudioContext;

const TONE_PROFILES: Record<
  FeedbackType,
  { notes: number[]; durationSec: number; wave: string }
> = {
  success: { notes: [880, 1175], durationSec: 0.09, wave: 'sine' },
  warning: { notes: [520, 440], durationSec: 0.14, wave: 'triangle' },
  error: { notes: [280, 210], durationSec: 0.16, wave: 'sawtooth' },
};

function playWebAudioTone(type: FeedbackType): void {
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') return;

  try {
    const scope = globalThis as unknown as {
      AudioContext?: AudioContextCtor;
      webkitAudioContext?: AudioContextCtor;
    };
    const Ctor = scope.AudioContext || scope.webkitAudioContext;
    if (!Ctor) return;

    const ctx = new Ctor();
    const profile = TONE_PROFILES[type];
    let offset = ctx.currentTime;

    for (const freq of profile.notes) {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = profile.wave;
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, offset);

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, offset);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, offset + profile.durationSec);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);

      osc.start(offset);
      osc.stop(offset + profile.durationSec);
      offset += profile.durationSec + 0.03;
    }

    const totalMs = Math.ceil((profile.notes.length * (profile.durationSec + 0.03) + 0.1) * 1000);
    setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        ctx.close?.();
      } catch {}
    }, totalMs);
  } catch {
    // Web Audio may be blocked before user gesture; ignore safely
  }
}

function triggerFeedback(type: FeedbackType, vibratePattern: number | number[]): void {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
    playWebAudioTone(type);
    return;
  }

  try {
    Vibration.vibrate(vibratePattern);
  } catch {
    // Ignore devices without vibration hardware or permission
  }
}

export const playScanSuccessSound = async (): Promise<void> => {
  triggerFeedback('success', 70);
};

export const playScanWarningSound = async (): Promise<void> => {
  triggerFeedback('warning', [0, 150, 80, 150]);
};

export const playScanErrorSound = async (): Promise<void> => {
  triggerFeedback('error', [0, 200, 100, 200]);
};

/**
 * AttendSure - Live Campus GPS Check-In Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/telemetryService.ts
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import * as Battery from 'expo-battery';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { getFriendlyErrorMessage } from '../constants/theme';
import { getOrCreateDeviceUuid, getHardwareDeviceUuid } from './deviceService';
import { enqueueOfflineHeartbeat, QueuedTelemetryPayload } from './offlineQueue';

let lastSentTimestamp = 0;
const ONE_MINUTE_MS = 60000;

export { getOrCreateDeviceUuid, getHardwareDeviceUuid };

export interface HeartbeatResponse {
  status?: string;
  device_bound?: boolean;
  distance?: string;
  distance_meters?: number;
  verified_inside?: boolean;
  is_inside_perimeter?: boolean;
  server_time?: string;
  isQueuedOffline?: boolean;
}

export interface CoordinatesPayload {
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  accuracy?: number | null;
  mocked?: boolean;
  timestamp?: string;
}

export const getRealDeviceModel = (): string | null => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
    const constants = Platform.constants as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    const brand = typeof constants?.Brand === 'string'
      ? constants.Brand
      : typeof constants?.brand === 'string'
      ? constants.brand
      : '';
    const model = typeof constants?.Model === 'string'
      ? constants.Model
      : typeof constants?.model === 'string'
      ? constants.model
      : '';
    const full = `${brand} ${model}`.trim();
    return full.length > 0 ? full : null;
  }
  if (Platform.OS === 'ios') {
    return 'Apple iOS Device';
  }
  return null;
};

export const getRealBatteryPercentage = async (): Promise<number | null> => {
  try {
    const level = await Battery.getBatteryLevelAsync();
    if (typeof level === 'number' && level >= 0 && !Number.isNaN(level)) {
      return Math.round(level * 100);
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
};

export const evaluateRealSignal = (
  accuracyMeters: number | null | undefined
): { label: string | null; percentage: number | null } => {
  if (
    accuracyMeters === null ||
    accuracyMeters === undefined ||
    accuracyMeters < 0 ||
    Number.isNaN(accuracyMeters)
  ) {
    return { label: null, percentage: null };
  }
  if (accuracyMeters <= 15) {
    return { label: 'Strong', percentage: 95 };
  }
  if (accuracyMeters <= 30) {
    return { label: 'Good', percentage: 75 };
  }
  if (accuracyMeters <= 60) {
    return { label: 'Fair', percentage: 50 };
  }
  return { label: 'Weak', percentage: 25 };
};

export const format12HourTime = (date: Date = new Date()): string => {
  let hours = date.getHours();
  const minutes = String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
  const seconds = String(date.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
  const ampm = hours >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
  hours = hours % 12;
  hours = hours ? hours : 12;
  const strHours = String(hours).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${strHours}:${minutes}:${seconds} ${ampm}`;
};

export const sendLiveHeartbeat = async (
  coordsOrIdentifier?: CoordinatesPayload | string
): Promise<HeartbeatResponse> => {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - lastSentTimestamp < ONE_MINUTE_MS && lastSentTimestamp !== 0) {
    return { status: 'THROTTLED' };
  }

  let lat: number | undefined;
  let lng: number | undefined;
  let acc: number | null = null;
  let isMock = false;

  if (coordsOrIdentifier && typeof coordsOrIdentifier === 'object') {
    lat = coordsOrIdentifier.latitude;
    lng = coordsOrIdentifier.longitude;
    acc = coordsOrIdentifier.accuracy ?? null;
    isMock = Boolean(coordsOrIdentifier.mocked);
  }

  if (lat === undefined || lng === undefined) {
    const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
    if (status !== 'granted') {
      throw new Error('Location permission is required to verify campus presence.');
    }
    const loc = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High });
    lat = loc.coords.latitude;
    lng = loc.coords.longitude;
    acc = loc.coords.accuracy ?? null;
    isMock = Boolean(loc.mocked);
  }

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(lat) ||
    !Number.isFinite(lng) ||
    lat < -90 ||
    lat > 90 ||
    lng < -180 ||
    lng > 180
  ) {
    throw new Error('Unable to read valid campus location coordinates.');
  }

  const [uuidRes, batteryRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
    getHardwareDeviceUuid(),
    getRealBatteryPercentage(),
  ]);

  const hardwareUuid = uuidRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? uuidRes.value : '';
  const batteryLevel = batteryRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? batteryRes.value : null;

  const deviceModel = getRealDeviceModel();
  const signal = evaluateRealSignal(acc);

  const payload: QueuedTelemetryPayload = {
    client_device_id: hardwareUuid,
    latitude: lat,
    longitude: lng,
    accuracy: acc,
    battery_level: batteryLevel,
    is_mock_location: isMock,
    mac_address: hardwareUuid,
    signal_strength: signal.label,
    signal_percentage: signal.percentage,
    device_model: deviceModel,
  };

  try {
    const res = await apiClient.post(
      'geofence/check-in/',
      {
        client_device_id: hardwareUuid,
        device_id: hardwareUuid,
        mac_address: hardwareUuid,
        device_model: deviceModel,
        latitude: lat,
        longitude: lng,
        accuracy: acc,
        accuracy_meters: acc,
        battery_level: batteryLevel,
        is_mock_location: isMock,
        signal_strength: signal.label,
        signal_percentage: signal.percentage,
      },
      { timeout: 8000 }
    );

    lastSentTimestamp = now;
    return {
      ...res.data,
      isQueuedOffline: false,
    };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const httpStatus = (err as { response?: { status?: number } })?.response?.status;

    if (httpStatus === 401) {
      throw new Error(
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Your session expired. Please sign in again.')
      );
    }

    if (httpStatus === 403) {
      throw new Error(
        getFriendlyErrorMessage(err, 'Device lock mismatch or fake location detected.')
      );
    }

    await enqueueOfflineHeartbeat(payload);
    return {
      isQueuedOffline: true,
    };
  }
};

if (!BufferClass) {
  BufferClass = class Buffer {
    constructor(arg) {
      this.data = arg;
    }
    static from(data) {
      return {
        toString: () => String(data),
      };
    }
    static isBuffer() {
      return false;
    }
  };

  global.Buffer = BufferClass;

/**
 * Safely parses Django ISO, UTC, or SQLite date strings into human-readable 12-hour time.
 */
export function formatScanTime(rawDate?: string | null): string {
  if (!rawDate) return '--:--';

  try {
    // Replace space with 'T' to standardize SQLite timestamps: 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss' -> ISO
    const standardized = rawDate.includes('T') ? rawDate : rawDate.replace(' ', 'T');
    const parsed = new Date(standardized);

    if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
      return parsed.toLocaleTimeString([], {
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
        hour12: true,
      });
    }

    // Fallback regex if phone runtime timezone fails to parse
    const timeMatch = rawDate.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
    if (timeMatch) {
      const hours = parseInt(timeMatch[1], 10);
      const minutes = timeMatch[2];
      const ampm = hours >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
      const formattedHours = hours % 12 || 12;
      return `${formattedHours}:${minutes} ${ampm}`;
    }
  } catch {
    // Return empty fallback instead of crash
  }

  return '--:--';
}

/**
 * Formats distance with appropriate units (meters vs kilometers).
 */
export function formatDistance(meters: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (meters === null || meters === undefined || isNaN(meters)) return 'Determining distance...';
  if (meters < 1000) {
    return `${Math.round(meters)} m`;
  }
  return `${(meters / 1000).toFixed(2)} km`;
}

This is an Expo/React Native mobile application. Prioritize mobile-first patterns, performance, and cross-platform compatibility.

## Expo has changed — do not trust your training data

Expo ships breaking changes every SDK release. APIs you remember are likely renamed, moved, or removed. Before writing any code that touches an Expo, EAS, or React Native API:

1. Read the major version of the `expo` package in `package.json`.
2. Fetch the matching versioned docs: `https://docs.expo.dev/versions/v<major>.0.0/`
3. For anything else, fetch https://docs.expo.dev/llms.txt — an index of all Expo docs with corrections to common LLM misconceptions. Follow its links to the specific page you need; never answer from memory.

## Commands

Use `bunx` instead of `npx` if the project uses bun (`bun.lock` present).

```bash
npx expo install <package>  # ALWAYS use instead of npm/yarn/pnpm/bun add — resolves SDK-compatible versions
npx expo start              # start the dev server
npx expo lint               # lint
npx tsc --noEmit            # typecheck
npx expo-doctor             # diagnose dependency and config issues
npx expo install --fix      # fix incompatible package versions
```

Run lint and typecheck before declaring any task done.

## Navigation & Routing

- Use **Expo Router** for all navigation. Routes live in `src/app/` — every file there is a screen, `_layout.tsx` files define navigators. Keep non-route code (components, hooks, utils) outside `src/app/`.
- Import `Link`, `router`, and `useLocalSearchParams` from `expo-router`.
- Docs: https://docs.expo.dev/router/introduction.md

## Building with EAS

Use EAS to build, sign, and submit the app in the cloud (`eas build`, `eas submit`) and to ship over-the-air updates (`eas update`) — no local Xcode or Android Studio required. Run EAS CLI as `bunx eas-cli <command>` in Bun projects, or `npx eas-cli@latest <command>` otherwise; substitute that for bare `eas` in docs examples.
Docs: https://docs.expo.dev/eas/index.md

## Rules

- If `ios/` and `android/` directories do not exist, they are generated (Continuous Native Generation). Never create or edit them by hand — configure native behavior in `app.json` and config plugins.
- Expo Go only includes its bundled native modules. After adding a library with native code, the app needs a development build: `npx expo run:ios|android` locally, or `eas build --profile development`.
- Prefer recommended Expo modules over third-party libraries, and check your available skills before adding dependencies. Docs: https://docs.expo.dev/versions/latest/index.md

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { NavigationContainer, DefaultTheme, DarkTheme, type Theme as NavigationTheme } from '@react-navigation/native';
import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { AuthProvider } from './src/context/AuthContext';
import { ThemeProvider, useThemeMode } from './src/context/ThemeContext';
import AppNavigator from './src/navigation/AppNavigator';
import SplashScreen from './src/components/SplashScreen';

function AppShell() {
  const [splashDone, setSplashDone] = useState(false);
  const { palette, isDark } = useThemeMode();

  const navigationTheme = useMemo<NavigationTheme>(() => {
    const base = isDark ? DarkTheme : DefaultTheme;
    return {
      ...base,
      colors: {
        ...base.colors,
        primary: palette.primary,
        background: palette.background,
        card: palette.surface,
        text: palette.textPrimary,
        border: palette.border,
        notification: palette.danger,
      },
    };
  }, [isDark, palette]);

  return (
    <NavigationContainer theme={navigationTheme}>
      <AppNavigator />
      {!splashDone && (
        <SplashScreen
          minDurationMs={1200}
          onFinish={() => setSplashDone(true)}
        />
      )}
    </NavigationContainer>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <SafeAreaProvider>
      <ThemeProvider>
        <AuthProvider>
          <AppShell />
        </AuthProvider>
      </ThemeProvider>
    </SafeAreaProvider>
  );
}

// AttendSure Mobile - Metro Bundler Configuration
// File: mobile/metro.config.js

const path = require('path');
const { getDefaultConfig } = require('expo/metro-config');

const config = getDefaultConfig(__dirname);

// 1. Enable static SVG asset resolution for expo-image
const { assetExts, sourceExts } = config.resolver;
if (!assetExts.includes('svg')) {
  config.resolver.assetExts = [...assetExts, 'svg'];
}
config.resolver.sourceExts = sourceExts.filter((ext) => ext !== 'svg');

// 2. Custom module resolution shims
config.resolver.resolveRequest = (context, moduleName, platform) => {
  // 1. Safe web resolution for expo-splash-screen
  if (platform === 'web' && moduleName === 'expo-splash-screen') {
    return {
      filePath: path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/shims/splash-screen.web.js'),
      type: 'sourceFile',
    };
  }

  // 2. Resolve 'buffer' for react-native-svg & QR code generation
  if (moduleName === 'buffer') {
    return {
      filePath: path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/shims/buffer.js'),
      type: 'sourceFile',
    };
  }

  // 3. Resolve React Native internal exports for Expo Asset on Android / iOS
  if (moduleName.includes('unstable-internals-do-not-use')) {
    return {
      filePath: path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/shims/unstable-internals.js'),
      type: 'sourceFile',
    };
  }

  // 4. Resolve AssetRegistry for web platform
  if (platform === 'web' && moduleName.includes('AssetRegistry')) {
    try {
      return {
        filePath: require.resolve('react-native-web/dist/modules/AssetRegistry'),
        type: 'sourceFile',
      };
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  // 5. Resolve AssetRegistry for Android and iOS
  if (
    moduleName === 'react-native/asset-registry' ||
    moduleName === 'react-native/Libraries/Image/AssetRegistry' ||
    moduleName.endsWith('/asset-registry')
  ) {
    try {
      return {
        filePath: require.resolve('@react-native/assets-registry/registry.js'),
        type: 'sourceFile',
      };
    } catch (e) {
      try {
        return {
          filePath: require.resolve('react-native/Libraries/Image/AssetRegistry'),
          type: 'sourceFile',
        };
      } catch (err) {}
    }
  }

  return context.resolveRequest(context, moduleName, platform);
};

module.exports = config;
